# Sail More Now

## Changelog - Jun 2026 (POSTTYPES-2: mobile New post Type as a dropdown; NAMES-0 report only)
POSTTYPES-2 (mobile only, JS only, code-only/not device-tested): replaced the Type chip row on app/new-post.tsx with the app's existing Picker dropdown (the same component the Market dropdown on that screen uses), labeled "Type". Options/order: General, For sale, Sailing opportunity, Volunteer opportunity (existing label spellings, stored values unchanged). Default general from Hub; sailing_opportunity still preselected from the Crew Bank entry points (preselect effect unchanged). For_sale price/condition/photos + Marketplace routing and all type-dependent logic unchanged. No web change, no schema change, no data touched. Verified: all four types post via the mobile /posts payload (general, sailing_opportunity, volunteer_opportunity, for_sale with price kept); temp posts/accounts deleted (zero residue). Metro bundles clean (2232 iOS modules). Picker trigger already has MOBILE-FIX-1 auto-fit (adjustsFontSizeToFit + capped scaling).
NAMES-0 (report only, nothing changed): author names are denormalized at create time (author_name on posts/comments/discussions/reviews, user_name on crew-bank + event registrations, from_name/title on messages/notifications, organizer_name on events). Read paths refresh author_handle/account_type/verified live but NOT the name (list_posts even omits name from the user projection), which is why a post card shows the old name while the hover card (live profile lookup by handle/id) shows the new one. Live surfaces: hover card, public profile, directory, Following, admin, messaging thread peer label, event CSV export. Stale surfaces (web + mobile, same shared fields): posts of every type incl marketplace + sailing opportunities, comments, forum, blog, reviews, crew bank listings, event RSVP/crew pool in-app lists, stored message from_name, notification titles. A `handle` (username) field exists on users and is shown as @handle alongside the full `name`. Recommended fix = Option A: in the ~6-8 read enrichers that already join users for handle, also refresh the name (keep the business-identity stored block); no frontend change, no schema change, no backfill needed. Option B (write-time propagation + one-time backfill) would need a backup + dry run + undo and is not recommended.



## Changelog - Jun 2026 (POSTTYPES-1: align post types web/mobile; mobile JS only)
No testing agent. No publish. No schema change (create_post stores any type; volunteer_opportunity needs no extra fields). No existing data touched; temp user + 2 temp posts were created via the real mobile payload for verification and then deleted (zero residue), so no backup/undo script needed.
PART A (report): sailing opportunity posts default to 30 days (PostCreate.expires_days=30, clamped 1-365); expires_at set server-side to now+30d (no ends_at). Neither composer exposes a duration field, so the author cannot choose/change it. POST /posts/{id}/extend exists but is wired to NO web or mobile UI, so there is no author-facing renew for an expired sailing opportunity. Feed rules unchanged.
PART B (build, mobile only):
1. Renamed "Discussion" to "General" on mobile. Only ONE place renders that post-type label: app/new-post.tsx TYPES. Feed cards badge by _kind ("Post" for all post types), never "Discussion"; no filter was named Discussion; so that single chip label was the only change. Stored value stays general.
2. Removed "Question" from app/new-post.tsx composer. Backend + stored value untouched. 0 question posts exist today; if any existed they appear under the Hub "Feed" and "All" filters (web "All posts") and keep doing so (load for feed/all returns all non-for_sale post types).
3. Added "Volunteer opportunity" (volunteer_opportunity) to app/new-post.tsx. New chip order matches web: Sailing opportunity, Volunteer opportunity, General, For sale.
4. app/(tabs)/feed/index.tsx Hub "Volunteer" filter now fetches volunteer_opportunity POSTS plus volunteer EVENTS and renders two labeled, non-collapsible groups: "Volunteer opportunities" (posts) then "Volunteer events" (events). optimisticPrepend + the /posts fetch in All/Feed already surface a new volunteer post in Hub (All) immediately. Group headers use auto-fit (numberOfLines 2 + capped scaling).
5. Web: no composer/filter change. Verified web already has a "Volunteer opportunity" composer type and feed filter; no mismatch surfaced.
Verified LIVE on web: temp volunteer_opportunity + general posts (created via the exact mobile /posts payload) appear under /community?type=volunteer_opportunity, ?type=general, and unfiltered; "Volunteer opportunity" badge renders; New post button present (web posting intact). Mobile: Metro bundles clean (1774 modules); code-only, NOT device-tested.



## Changelog - Jun 2026 (CREWBANK-OPP-2: add a sailing opportunity from Crew Bank; web + mobile)
No testing agent. No publish. No schema change. No data touched (only code; a temp DB user+session was created for web screenshots and deleted, zero residue). No backup/undo needed (no migration). Mobile is JS only and NOT device-tested; web verified live in-browser.
PART A built:
- Link text (web CrewBank.jsx + mobile CrewBankScreen.tsx): "Looking for a boat? See Sailing Opportunities" -> "Looking for a boat? Click here to see Sailing Opportunities" (still a link, same destination).
- Web Crew Bank: added "Add a sailing opportunity" button directly below My Crew Listings / List yourself; links to /community?compose=1. Feed.jsx now auto-opens the EXISTING composer (unchanged) on ?compose=1 with type preselected (draft already defaults to sailing_opportunity); free individuals get the existing upgrade prompt.
- Mobile Crew Bank: (a) added an "Add a sailing opportunity" row below the List-yourself/upgrade block, styled like the list-yourself card, using the existing boatOwner (MaterialCommunityIcons sail-boat) icon tinted orange (colors.accent), shown to all users; (b) the top-right plus now opens a two-choice menu (Add a sailing opportunity / List yourself as available crew) and is always visible (was gated to paid users before - NOTE: contradicts the brief's "already visible to free users"; made it always visible to meet the requirement). Free users choosing List yourself get the existing upgrade route (/pricing). Labels wrap to two lines + capped font scaling (MOBILE-FIX-1 patterns).
- Mobile New post type chip "Crew / Opportunity" -> "Sailing opportunity"; preselect generalized so ?type=sailing_opportunity (and any valid type) preselects.
- My Crew Listings button (web + mobile Crew Bank): shown only when the user has >=1 per-event crew listing (GET /crew-bank/mine, kind=="event"); hidden for none or standalone-only. Confirmed standalone-only users still view/edit from the "your listing" card on both platforms.
- After publish (unchanged): WEB lands on the /community Community feed (existing composer behavior, no redirect added); MOBILE returns to Crew Bank via existing router.back(). Posting from the Hub is unchanged on both.
Verified LIVE (web, temp premium user, cookie session): add button below List yourself at 1440/820/390 and light+dark; My Crew Listings hidden for a no-event user; composer deep-link opens with "Sailing opportunity" preselected. Mobile: Metro bundles clean (1767 web / 2210 iOS modules); code-only, not device-tested.
PARTS B and C: report only, delivered in chat, no code changed. Awaiting go before any Part B change.



## Changelog - Jun 2026 (MOBILE-FIX-1: Android Large-font / 3-button-nav layout fixes; mobile Expo only)
Mobile only. No testing agent. No em dashes. JS/TS only (no app.json / native change), so the fixes appear on a normal Expo Go reload, no rebuild needed. Could NOT verify on a real Android device (none available); the Expo web preview runs at font-scale 1 with no system nav bar so it cannot reproduce "Display size Large" or the 3-button nav. Verified: Metro bundles clean (1774 modules). The tsc warnings seen are all pre-existing project noise (Icon color prop typing on unchanged tabBarIcon lines, Pro[]|null FlatList data, EventMap module resolution, User.logo_url), none from these edits.
CHANGES:
1. Bottom tabs (app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx): tab labels now adjustsFontSizeToFit + numberOfLines=1 + minimumFontScale 0.7 + maxFontSizeMultiplier 1.15, tabBarItemStyle paddingHorizontal 0, label paddingHorizontal 1 so all five (Hub, Events, Pros/Orgs, Messages, Profile) stay full and on one line at Large (labels auto-shrink, NOT shortened). Tab bar height/padding still = base + insets.bottom (sits above the system nav). Added an absolute surface-coloured filler across the bottom insets.bottom strip so the deep-navy app background never shows behind the 3-button/gesture nav (the "maroon").
2. Profile (app/(tabs)/profile/index.tsx): "Switch account" + "Log out" now use the measuring ButtonRow (auto-stacks full width when they do not fit, so neither runs off the edge). Account Details inputStyle changed from fixed height:44 to minHeight:48 + paddingVertical:10 + fontSize:15 so large text no longer clips at the top. Subscription "Change plan" + "Cancel subscription" buttons given numberOfLines=2 so the full label always shows.
3. Headers/filters: directory (Pros/Orgs) "Boat Pros" title adjustsFontSizeToFit/minFontScale 0.7 (no more "Bo.."); "Clubs & Orgs" link capped. MultiSelectPicker trigger ("All markets"/"All specialties") and ui Picker trigger (Events "My markets"/"All categories") now adjustsFontSizeToFit + maxFontSizeMultiplier 1.2. Button label text globally capped at maxFontSizeMultiplier 1.3. Following card ("Latest from clubs & orgs you follow") already numberOfLines=1; no overflow with the capped Button/label changes (left as-is).
4. Events (app/(tabs)/events/index.tsx): date badge "NOV"/day now adjustsFontSizeToFit + maxFontSizeMultiplier + box paddingHorizontal so the month stays inside the badge. Added a weekday+PT-time line to each list row via new helper format.ts fmtListDateTime -> "Mon, Nov 9 · 6:30 PM PT" or "Mon, Nov 9 · Time not listed" (reuses the existing PT ptFields/dLabel/tLabel helpers).
ITEM 5 (report only, nothing changed): a Premium trial member reaches Pricing from Profile only -> Subscription card -> "Change plan" button (router.push("/pricing")); yes, "Change plan" opens Pricing. From My plan there is NO link to the Pricing page for members (org accounts get a "Contact us" button instead); My plan shows the per-level comparison ("plans stack" chips) inline. Pricing links back to My plan via "See full plan details". So: Profile = the one tap to Pricing; My plan = inline comparison but no Pricing link for members.



## Changelog - Jun 2026 (EVENTS-ADMIN-BULK-1: admin bulk actions, recoverable delete, Mark confirmed; web admin only)
Web admin + backend only (mobile untouched). No testing agent (manual: curl + DB invariants + browser screenshots at 1920 and 390 widths, temp admin + temp events created and DELETED, zero residue). No em dashes. No data migration run (all changes are admin-initiated at runtime and reversible). Standing rules honored (no publish, Stripe test, no secrets printed in report, no bot-protection bypass).
STEP 1 sanity (read-only, passed): the 3 OWSA titles appear once each; 0 duplicate identities; unique index agg_source_identity present; next-scrape sim 0 inserts/121 updates; sarc-48north disabled; env keys present (RESEND/EMAIL_LOGO/Stripe test/admin). Preview URL owsa-events-preview--7evdsk8f. Google sign-in redirect is origin-derived so it targets THIS job; the Stripe TEST webhook endpoint is registered in the Stripe dashboard against the prior job's URL (preview works anyway via post-checkout polling/reconcile).
BUILD:
- Backend new module backend/event_recovery.py: soft_delete_event (snapshot event + its registrations + crew into events_deleted, tombstone aggregator events by source identity, then remove live event + signups) and restore_event (re-insert event + signups, drop tombstone, delete recovery copy). NO auto-purge: recoverable_until (deleted_at + 30 days) is stored for display only; nothing deletes copies on a timer.
- Backend new endpoints (admin_router.py, all require_admin): POST /admin/events/bulk-delete (recoverable, one audit entry with who/when/which), POST /admin/events/bulk-review (clears category_needs_review only, category unchanged), POST /admin/events/confirm (single+bulk: strips leading "[Draft]" from title and $addToSet "title" to admin_locked_fields so the scrape cannot re-add it), GET /admin/events/deleted, POST /admin/events/{id}/restore. Single row Delete now also routes through bulk-delete so it is recoverable + audited.
- Web AdminEvents.jsx: bulk bar now has Delete selected (confirm modal) + Mark reviewed + Mark confirmed alongside the existing Set-organizer tool; per-row Tentative badge + "Mark confirmed" button on [Draft] rows; a "Deleted events" panel with Restore; titles render via eventTitleParts (so [Draft] shows as a Tentative pill, not raw text). Item 4 scroll-lock: could NOT reproduce a lock on single delete (wheel + programmatic scroll both fine at 1920, community + aggregator); only one place app-wide locks body scroll and it cleans up. Added a mount-time safety net that clears any stray body overflow/position/pointer-events lock leaked from another route/modal, and kept all new modals as plain overlays that never touch body.
VERIFIED LIVE: all 5 backend features via curl + DB (review clears flag & keeps category; confirm strips title & locks it; bulk-delete 2 + recovery copies + aggregator tombstone; deleted list with 30-day window; restore re-adds event + its RSVP registration + drops tombstone; 0 leftover copies). Web UI at 1920 and 390: bulk bar buttons, per-row Tentative + Confirm, Deleted events panel + Restore, no horizontal overflow at phone width.
CODE-ONLY / NOT CHECKED: the title-lock blocking a real re-scrape was verified by the aggregator code path (locked fields excluded from the scrape $set) + admin_locked_fields now holding "title", not by running a live OWSA/OCSA scrape against a confirmed feed event. Mobile Admin Events not touched (web-only task). Dark mode not re-screenshotted (existing dark: classes preserved). Tablet width inferred from the same stacking classes proven at 390 and 1920.



## Changelog - Jun 2026 (EVENTS-FIX-2: admin time bug, end-after-start, 48N disabled)
- AdminControls single "Date & time" editor now reads AND writes Pacific via isoToPTInput/ptInputToISO (same helpers as the Starts/Ends screen) and sends ONLY changed fields; a no-change save writes/locks nothing. Fixes the UTC-vs-local shift-on-save bug.
- Backend "end must be after start" guard (date_utils.end_before_start) on all write paths: owner create/update, admin update; importer drops a backwards end instead of storing it; web editors (AdminEvents, Events owner form) show a clear message; mobile already validated. Date-only / time-not-listed still allowed.
- Fixed BoD Dec record start back to 2026-12-10T02:30Z (Dec 9 6:30 PM PT), end kept. Undo: python bod_fix.py --undo 20261101T190042Z.
- Disabled sarc-48north source (enabled=False); config + history retained. Scheduler skips it; re-enable via admin Event Sources toggle or PATCH /admin/event-sources/sarc-48north {enabled:true}.
- Report: preview restarts frequently so the in-process weekly scheduler is unreliable there (Nov 1 3am run did not auto-fire; Jenn ran it manually). A published deployment runs continuously; misfire_grace_time=3600 only covers brief in-process pauses, not restarts. Safeguard proposed (startup catch-up if a scheduled Sunday was missed), not built.

## Changelog - Jun 2026 (PACIFIC-2: PT display fix, optional times, scraper date-only, data repair dry-run)
Fixed the mobile display bug (dayjs timezone did not offset on Hermes, showing UTC digits labelled PT) with a pure-math Pacific converter in mobile/src/lib/format.ts; verified 2026-11-07T01:00:00Z -> "Fri, Nov 6 · 5:00 PM PT". Design rules: all event times Pacific + "PT", no UTC, no all-day; date-only events show the date + "Time not listed, see details"; start/end optional. Scraper (aggregator.py) now imports VALUE=DATE events as date-only (no invented time), subtracts the exclusive DTEND day for multi-day ranges, reads floating times as feed tz, and normalises HTML-source times to UTC. Optional-time added to web + mobile create forms (toggle). Emails/scheduler say "Time not listed" not "All day". Web eventCategories.js adds formatEventWhen. Data repair (pacific2_repair.py): backup (JSON + events_backup_pacific2 collection) + DRY RUN done - 119 scraped, 113 would become date-only, 3 timed, 3 protected (admin-edited/locked/tombstoned, incl. OCSA Tropical Party). STOPPED, awaiting Jenn's go for Step B (--apply). Report-only: description-time extraction (0 found today) and post-scrape anomaly check proposed. Build stamp mobile rv3a-16 -> rv3a-17. Detail in /app/memory/PACIFIC-2_REPORT.md.

## Changelog - Jun 2026 (VERIFY-1: email verification, 24h grace then lock, signup anti-abuse)
No testing agent (curl/requests + read-only DB + browser smoke), no em dashes, no tokens printed, exactly one live test email to jenn@. Build stamp rv3a-13, mobile restarted. Full detail in /app/memory/VERIFY-1_REPORT.md.
- SIGN-UP VERIFICATION: register sends a single-use 7-day verification link via the existing Resend mailer, logged in /admin/email. Password accounts get 24h full access, then are locked behind a Verify screen until verified. Google sign-ins are already verified.
- STATE/GATE: verification_state() on /auth/me (verified/required/locked/hours_remaining); require_not_locked gate inside require_interaction (posting/DMs/reviews/crew) and /payments/checkout. Checkout allowed during grace, blocked once locked.
- ENDPOINTS: POST /auth/send-verification (3/hr/account, 10/hr/IP), POST /auth/verify-email (no auth, token is proof - works cross-device/new tab), POST /auth/change-email ("Wrong email? Fix it"), POST /admin/users/verify-email (verify-by-admin, audit-logged, for comp/reviewer logins).
- EXEMPT from lock: jenn@, comp/complimentary, verified_by_admin.
- SIGNUP ANTI-ABUSE: honeypot field + 3s min fill time + disposable-domain blocklist; rate limits 10/hr/IP and 20/hr/email-domain. CAPTCHA (Turnstile recommended), card-fingerprint reuse rule, and an unverified-account cleanup job were PROPOSED only (not built).
- UI: web VerifyBanner + VerifyLocked + /verify-email page + /admin/verify-user; mobile VerifyGate (banner + lock screen) in the tabs layout. Web /verify-email browser-verified; authed web banner/lock and all mobile UI are code-only (session-token cookie not handled in-browser).

No testing agent (curl/requests + read-only DB + one browser smoke), no em dashes, no secrets printed, temp data only (0 residue). Build stamp rv3a-11 -> rv3a-12. Full detail in /app/memory/SECURITY-2_REPORT.md.
- ADMIN GOOGLE-ONLY: admin accounts' password hashes stripped on startup; login endpoint refuses password login for any admin (401 "sign in with Google"). ADMIN_PASSWORD removed from backend/.env and from server.py (old seeder replaced by a Google-only bootstrap). Legacy backend/tests/*.py that read ADMIN_PASSWORD can no longer do admin password login (left as-is).
- BREAK-GLASS (backend/break_glass.py): off-by-default, env-only switch (BREAK_GLASS_ENABLED/EMAIL/PASSWORD_HASH, window default 30 min capped 60) that re-enables password login for ONE admin; bcrypt hash only (never plaintext); auto-expires; every use audit-logged + emails ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL; normal tiered lockout applies.
- ADMIN NOW DB-BACKED: is_admin() reads platform_role only; ADMIN_EMAILS is bootstrap-only; login/session sync is promote-only (never demotes a DB-granted admin). Risk assessed and reported before the change.
- ADMIN ACCESS PAGE (/admin/access + admin_access_router.py): grant/revoke FULL admin by email with required reason; target must have Google sign-in + verified email; granting admin must re-authenticate with Google within 120s (last_reauth_at); last admin can never be removed; grant strips target password; all actions audit-logged + email ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL; lists current admins with granted-by/when.
- VERIFIED: route loads in browser (signed-out redirect clean); all grant/revoke/list + admin-login-block behavior verified via backend API with seeded admin session + throwaway target (then cleaned). Authed UI is code/API-verified, not browser-verified (session token is a secret not printed).

## Changelog - Jun 2026 (REVIEWS-4c - other-party dispute view, one removal path, hide-not-delete, restore; browser-verified)
Verified on a REAL review (reviewer submits -> reviewee reviews back -> reveal -> dispute), temp paid accounts, all deleted after. No publish; Campaigns OFF; temp emails on a non-deliverable domain (no real inbox).
- ITEM 1 (other party could not see the dispute; dead bell click) - WHAT WAS WRONG: GET /review-disputes/mine returned ONLY disputes filed_by the current user, so the reviewer whose review was disputed saw an empty Disputes tab (the bell link landed on a page with nothing). My earlier seeded test hid this because I only checked the URL changed, not that the dispute was listed. FIX (backend): /review-disputes/mine now also returns `about_me` = disputes involving me that I did NOT file (I am review_author or target), SAFE fields ONLY (status, outcome, standard reason ONLY if my review was removed, one-time "ask again" when allowed; NEVER filer, explanation, attachments, or filer reason). FIX (frontend): MyDisputesPanel renders a "Reviews you're part of" section. _dest now sends BOTH parties to /reviews?tab=disputes. VERIFIED IN BROWSER: reviewer bell shows "A review you're part of was reported", click lands on the Disputes tab, the safe entry shows (open: "being looked at"; removed: "Your review was removed. Reason: It included private information." + "Ask us to look again"); no filer name or explanation leaked.
- ITEM 2 (one removal path) - CONFIRMED Decide > Removed covers every case the old "Remove this side" did (it operates on the dispute's session_id + review_author_id regardless of held/pending/pre-reveal state), so nothing needed extending. REMOVED the "Remove this side" button from the admin UI. The old session endpoint remains but is now soft-hide (and no longer leaks the internal note); the single removal path is Decide > Removed (standard reason required).
- ITEM 3 (hide, do not delete) - WAS Decide > Removed HARD-DELETING? YES (db.reviews/mutual_reviews delete_one) - and self-removal + "Remove this side" too. FIX: all removal paths now set {removed:True, removed_at, removed_by, removed_reason_code} and KEEP the record + text. Added `removed:{$ne:True}` to every public average/listing read: compute_summary (org one-way + org two-way + MEMBER two-way - the member line was initially missed due to indentation and is now fixed), mutual_reviews_for, /reviews/list, directory pro listing, /reviews list, content review_highlights. Removed reviews never appear to normal users. Added admin "Restore review" (reason required, audit logged, unsets removed, re-reveals the session, both people get a NEUTRAL notice + CoC link). VERIFIED NUMERICALLY via the real summary endpoint: reviewee overall 2.0/count 1 -> removed -> count 0/overall None -> restored -> 2.0/count 1. Restore notices confirmed delivered to BOTH parties with the preview CoC link.
- ITEM 4 ("Flag filer") - label kept; added admin "Unflag filer" (audit logged, decrements dispute_flags, never below 0).
- ITEM 5 (end-to-end) - browser-verified on the real review: reviewer bell + click + safe Disputes view (open and removed states); admin standard-reason picker + Decide; Restore button (after switching the filter to All/Decided); averages drop on removal and return on restore (numeric). Stays / kept-details-removed / self-removal copy were verified at the endpoint+DB level in REVIEWS-4b and are unchanged. The admin decision/removal/restore actions were exercised in the browser; the per-outcome notice COPY for stays/kept/self was not re-screenshotted this round (same code path as the browser-proven removed/restore notices).
- Files changed: backend review_disputes_router.py (about_me, soft-remove, restore, unflag, _dest, admin list `restored`), reviews_router.py (soft-remove in remove-review + `removed` filters), directory_router.py, content_router.py, review_copy.py (NOTICE_RESTORED_BOTH); frontend components/ReviewDisputeForm.jsx (about_me section), pages/AdminModeration.jsx (remove the button, add Restore + Unflag); backend/.env (APP_BASE_URL already added in 4b).
- NOTE: a temp admin is recognized only when its email is in ADMIN_EMAILS (login syncs platform_role). I toggled ADMIN_EMAILS for testing and restored it to jenn only; this is a TEST-HARNESS fact, not a product bug (the real admin jenn@sailmorenow.com is always recognized).


## Changelog - Jun 2026 (REVIEWS-4b - dispute notifications fixed + browser-verified; web/backend)
Verified in a REAL browser with temp accounts (reviewer/reviewee/admin, all deleted after). No publish; Campaigns OFF; temp emails used a non-deliverable domain so no real inbox was hit. ADMIN_EMAILS was temporarily extended for the temp admin then restored to jenn only.

FOUR SYMPTOMS - root cause + fix + how verified:
- (a) "only the reviewee got an email": the email links were hard-coded to https://app.sailmorenow.com (not the preview), so the other party's email pointed at a dead domain; also both notices/emails are sent in _notify_filed. After the base-URL fix, BOTH parties get an in-app notice AND an email with a working preview link. VERIFIED: real file_dispute (HTTP 200) created in-app notices for BOTH reviewee ("We got your review request") and reviewer ("A review you're part of was reported"); email HTML now contains the preview "See the details" + CoC links and no app.sailmorenow.com.
- (b) "admin removal screen had NO standard-reason picker": there are TWO removal paths. The picker lives on the Decide dropdown (select "Removed"); Jenn most likely used the separate prominent red "Remove this side" button, which bypasses the picker. The picker also only appears after choosing "Removed". FIX kept the picker on the Decide flow and made the backend REQUIRE removal_reason_code for an admin removal. VERIFIED in browser: selecting "Removed" reveals "Standard reason shown to the reviewer"; removing without a reason returns HTTP 400 "Pick a standard removal reason."
- (c) "tapping the in-app notification did nothing": notifications store a `link` and NotificationsBell navigates to it; the stored links are now valid app routes. VERIFIED in browser: reviewee notice click -> /reviews?tab=disputes (Disputes tab, shows the dispute); reviewer notice click -> /reviews?session=<id>. (My first probe used a bad selector; the app itself navigates correctly.)
- (d) "email link did not work": review_disputes_router + reports_router hard-coded the live domain. FIX: added a single configurable APP_BASE_URL (backend/.env, = preview now, set to live at publish) read via _base_url()/_coc_url() in both routers; all dispute + flag emails and the in-app CoC meta now use it. VERIFIED: generated email HTML uses the preview domain for both buttons; /community-guidelines returns 200.

ITEM 2 - APP_BASE_URL: added to backend/.env (preview). Fixed ONLY dispute + flag emails. Other templates that STILL hard-code app.sailmorenow.com (left as-is per instruction, listed for later): auth_router.py (~255 welcome/reset, ~666 onboarding), admin_router.py (~661, ~816), messaging_router.py (~72 new-message), community_router.py (~1811), mailer.py (~330 admin-events, ~533 default param). Note mailer._app_url() already reads APP_PUBLIC_URL; _base_url() prefers APP_BASE_URL then APP_PUBLIC_URL.

ITEM 3 - notification destinations stored: each notice's `link` is persisted by notify() and used on click. Filer -> /reviews?tab=disputes (added ?tab= support to Reviews.jsx in WP2); other party -> /reviews?session=<id> (two-way) or /reviews (one-way).

ITEM 4 - picker shows + required: why it did not show before = wrong button (Remove this side) and/or "Removed" not selected. Now: picker renders on selecting "Removed" in the Decide dropdown (data-testid admin-removal-reason-<id>); client blocks Decide without it and the server returns 400. The admin's internal note (decision_reason) is stored + shown in the admin queue only and is NEVER in any member notice/email.

ITEM 5 - END-TO-END outcomes verified:
  - REMOVED (admin): full BROWSER run - reviewee bell+click, reviewer bell+click, admin picker+decide, and reviewer's decision notice read in the bell = "Your review was removed. Reason: It included private information. If you have new information, you can ask us to look again one time. Please read our Code of Conduct." (internal note NOT leaked). CoC page 200.
  - STAYS / KEPT-WITH-DETAILS-REMOVED / SELF-REMOVAL: exercised through the REAL admin decide endpoint (HTTP 200) and the stored notices read back - exact approved copy + CoC + correct links for each party (filer vs reviewer vs other). These three were endpoint+DB verified (not individually screenshotted); the bell-display + click-through mechanics were browser-proven on the removed/filed notices which use the identical path.

ITEM 6 - the two admin buttons (reported; behavior NOT changed, confirmation added):
  - "Remove this side" -> POST /admin/review-sessions/{sid}/remove-review. Before confirm: admin just types an internal note and clicks the red button (NOW: a confirmation dialog states the consequence). Data: HARD-DELETES that one person's review (irreversible, no snapshot); if it was the last review in the session the session is marked "removed". Notifies BOTH people with a generic "A review between you and <name> was removed by our team." and sends the reviewer "Reason: <admin's free-text note>." Reversible: NO. Other person's review: stays; Boat Pro averages recompute. Audit logged: YES (admin_actions remove_review). CAUTION/finding: this path LEAKS the admin's free-text note to the reviewer and has NO Code of Conduct link and does NOT use the approved standard-reason copy. RECOMMENDATION (pending approval, not done): route "Remove this side" through the same standard-reason + approved-notice flow as Decide>Removed, and relabel to "Remove only this review".
  - "Flag filer" -> POST /admin/review-disputes/{id}/flag-filer. Before confirm: types internal note, clicks (NOW: confirmation dialog). Data: sets flagged_filer=true on the dispute and increments the filer's dispute_flags counter. Notifies: NO ONE. Reviews/averages: unaffected. Reversible: not from the UI. Audit logged: YES (admin_actions dispute_flag_filer). It is an internal "this person may be misusing disputes" marker. RECOMMENDATION: relabel to "Flag filer for misuse (internal)".


## Changelog - Jun 2026 (WEB-POLISH-2 items 1, 2, 4, 5 built; item 3 reported - web/backend)
Rules honored: no testing agent (manual python-script test for item 1 + curl + public screenshots), no em dashes added (fixed one in ProPhotosUploader toast), no publish, Campaigns untouched/OFF, temp users deleted after the item-1 script.

- ITEM 1 (TOP PRIORITY - dispute notifications) - BUILT + TESTED.
  ROOT CAUSE of "the other person got no notification": file_dispute notified ONLY admins; neither the filer nor the other party ever got a "a dispute exists" notice. On DECISION both parties were notified, but copy was generic AND the reviewer's removal notice leaked the admin's INTERNAL free-text note; the filer's "See the details" link pointed at /my-disputes which is NOT a route (dead link).
  WHAT EACH PARTY RECEIVED BEFORE: filed -> only admins; decided -> both parties a generic notice, reviewer saw the admin's internal note as the reason; filer deep link broken.
  FIX (backend review_disputes_router.py + review_copy.py + mailer.py): added approved verbatim copy to review_copy.py (NOTICE_* + REMOVAL_REASON_MESSAGES standard reasons + with_coc()); added _notify_filed() so on filing the filer gets NOTICE_FILED_FILER and the other person gets NOTICE_FILED_OTHER (skipped for a reviewer's own self-removal); rewrote _notify_decision() to send the exact approved copy per outcome (stays / removed / kept_with_details_removed / self-removal), each in-app AND email, each ending with the Code of Conduct link (meta.code_of_conduct_url + email CoC link). The reviewer now sees ONLY a STANDARD removal reason; the admin's note (decision_reason) stays internal. decide endpoint now requires removal_reason_code for an admin removal; meta exposes removal_reasons. Admin UI (AdminModeration.jsx) got a standard-reason picker shown when "Removed" is selected (hidden for self-removal) and the note field relabelled "never shown to members". Working deep links: filer -> /reviews?tab=disputes (added ?tab= support to Reviews.jsx); other party -> /reviews?session=<id> (two-way) or /reviews (one-way).
  TESTED via a temp-user python script (deleted after): verified all 5 outcomes land the exact approved body + CoC link + correct per-party dest for both people, and 10 emails were generated (captured, NOT sent - monkeypatched to honor "no real emails"). Admin internal note confirmed absent from every notice.
  Self-removal detection uses reason == remove_my_own_review (so a reviewer contest, reason other_rule_violation, is not mis-routed as self-removal).
- ITEM 2 (wrong edit form) - BUILT. AdminControls.jsx FIELD_DEFS.post always included Price + Condition; now filtered out unless item.type === "for_sale". Other entities (discussion/comment/review/event) never had marketplace fields. The post's OWN author uses a different window (Feed.jsx inline editor / EditPost.jsx) which already gates price/condition by type - unchanged and still works. Code-verified (admin UI is auth-gated; not screenshot-tested).
- ITEM 4 (Free price) - BUILT. Added a "List this as Free" checkbox to the marketplace create form (Feed.jsx) and the owner editor (EditPost.jsx); checking it sets price 0 and disables the price input. Cards now show "Free" when price === 0 (Feed.jsx). Backend already stores/filters price 0 correctly (model_dump keeps 0; $gte/$lte include 0; PATCH guards price is not None). Sorting unaffected (marketplace sorts sold-first + price-drop bump; no price sort exists). Code-verified (marketplace is auth-gated).
  MOBILE follow-ups (NOT changed): mobile/src/screens/PostDetailScreen.tsx (price display + lowerPrice), mobile new-post.tsx / edit-post.tsx (price input), mobile feed/index.tsx (marketplace card) all need the same Free toggle + "Free" label.
- ITEM 5 (HEIC upload) - BUILT (web). CAUSE: HEIC/HEIF from iPhones cannot be decoded by most browsers in an <img>/canvas, so compressImageToDataURL/resizeImageToDataURL silently failed (onerror -> file skipped); some HEIC files also have empty MIME and were dropped by startsWith("image/") filters. FIX BELONGS IN THE BROWSER. Added heic2any + normalizeImageFile() in lib/imageCompress.js (lazy-imports heic2any, converts HEIC/HEIF File -> JPEG before any canvas work) and call it at the top of both compress functions -> fixes 6 uploaders that share the lib (MultiPhotoUploader/marketplace, Flyer, OrgGallery, ProfileCard, ProPhotos, Profile). Routed the 2 inline uploaders (CoverPhotoUploader, LogoUploader) through normalizeImageFile too. Relaxed the image-type filters (MultiPhoto, Flyer, ProPhotos, OrgGallery) to also accept .heic/.heif by extension, with a friendly toast if conversion fails. Code-verified + compile-verified (needs a real iPhone HEIC to fully exercise). MOBILE: expo-image-picker handles HEIC natively on device; report-only, no change.
- ITEM 3 (editable + enlargeable photos, carousel viewer) - REPORTED, NOT BUILT (bigger than a small fix).
  Findings you asked for: (a) STORAGE DELETE: storage.py has only init_storage/put_object/get_object - NO delete function, so files cannot be hard-deleted from object storage. BUT post/marketplace photos are stored as compressed data URLs inline in the Mongo post document (not as storage objects), so replacing/removing a photo just drops the data URL from the doc - there is no orphaned storage file to clean up. Only dispute attachments use object storage (private), and those have no delete path. (b) Backend ALREADY supports editing image_url/image_urls on non-for-sale posts (PATCH /posts/{id} else-branch), so adding forum/general photo editing is a FRONTEND-only change (wire MultiPhotoUploader into the Feed.jsx edit modal, which today patches body only). (c) The enlarge/carousel VIEWER is the larger new piece and must be built for web with swipe + arrow + keyboard + screen-reader support; MOBILE ALREADY HAS IT (mobile/src/components/ImageLightbox.tsx + MultiPhotoPicker) - use it as the reference. (d) WEB photo-display places that should get the viewer: Feed.jsx marketplace + forum/general cards (image_urls grid), DiscussionDetail.jsx cover, CommunityHub expanded image, DirectoryDetail/BoatProListing galleries, OrgListing gallery, Profile/ProfileCard images, CrewBank photos. Recommend building item 3 as a dedicated next task.


## Changelog - Jun 2026 (WEB-POLISH-1 items 4, 6, 7, 9, 10, 12 - web/backend)
Standing rules honored: NO testing agent (manual code review + py_compile + backend curl + one public-page screenshot); no em dashes added (removed one in DigestPreview); seed data untouched; no temp data created; Campaigns stays OFF.
- ITEM 9 (FIX, forum post photos on web): ROOT CAUSE - web gated discussion images behind `kind === "blog"` in THREE spots (CommunityHub create uploader + Hub expanded render + DiscussionDetail full-page render), while mobile HubDetailScreen renders `image_url` for ANY discussion and mobile hub/new allows forum photo upload. Fix: CommunityHub.jsx now shows the photo uploader for forum topics too (label "Photo (optional)") and renders `d.image_url` in the expanded row regardless of kind (testid `disc-photo-<id>`); DiscussionDetail.jsx renders the photo for any kind. Data-confirmed: 2 forum discussions carry image_url. OTHER post types NOT affected - for_sale marketplace images already render; general/sailing/volunteer feed posts never support images on either surface (Feed create exposes photos only for for_sale). Could not screenshot (Hub is behind Google-OAuth login, not automatable; testing agent forbidden) - verified by code parity + data.
- ITEM 7 (FIX, event creation validation): Events.jsx submit now computes per-field errors for title/market/starts_at/category(non-admin)/description, outlines each missing field (red border), shows a short per-field message + an aria-live summary banner (role="alert"), sets aria-invalid + aria-describedby, and scrolls to + focuses the FIRST missing field (visual order title>market>start>category>description). Errors clear on edit and on form open/success. Could not screenshot (Events behind auth + org/admin posting gate).
- ITEM 4 (FIX, shared star component): there were TWO display-star implementations (Rating.jsx using CSS classes star-full/star-empty vs ReviewRatings.jsx Stars using text/fill-[#F2A03B]) plus duplicated interactive star rows. Unified: Rating.jsx now renders the shared `Stars`; new shared `StarInput` in ReviewRatings.jsx used by MutualReviewForm (StarRow=StarInput) and ReviewButton one-way (testids unchanged: star-overall-N, star-<key>-N, oneway-star-N). VISUALLY CONFIRMED on public /directory (orange filled + slate empty, "2.0 (1)"). MOBILE star places (NOT changed, for a later prompt): mobile/app/review-compose.tsx (inline `Stars` input, lines 17-27, used 178/190), mobile/app/(tabs)/profile/reviews.tsx (266/276 icons), mobile/app/(tabs)/directory/index.tsx:215, mobile/src/screens/PublicProfileScreen.tsx:207, mobile/src/components/partner.tsx:156/167, mobile/src/screens/CrewBankScreen.tsx:143, mobile AdminScreen testimonials icons. All use `Icon name="star"/"star-outline"` directly; no single shared mobile star component exists.
- ITEM 6 (FIX, following banner "orgs"): the /follows/feed includes posts+events from ALL followed accounts (people AND orgs), not just orgs, so the heading was inaccurate. BEFORE: Clubs.jsx "From orgs you follow"; DigestPreview pill "Following — latest from orgs you follow". AFTER: "From people and orgs you follow"; "Following - latest from people and orgs you follow" (also fixed that pill's em dash to a hyphen). follows_router docstring corrected to "all followed accounts (people and orgs)". Candidate note: these were the only two user-facing "orgs you follow" strings on web (FollowingList already splits People/Organizations correctly).
- ITEM 10 (VERIFY + ENHANCE, Code of Conduct link in every flag/dispute notification): decision notify (_notify_decision) ALREADY carried CoC in both the in-app meta (code_of_conduct_url) and the email (coc_url) and the "See details" dest already works for both parties (filer -> /my-disputes, other party -> /reviews?session=). ADDED the CoC link to the previously-missing notifications: dispute-filed admin notify (review_disputes_router file_dispute) + both generic flag/report admin notifications (reports_router _notify_admins_hidden and _notify_admins_new_report, new COC_URL const). Only user-facing dispute EMAIL is the decision email (already has CoC).
- ITEM 12 (REPORT ONLY, stale Crew Bank photo): ROOT CAUSE - POST /crew-bank/standalone (community_router upsert_standalone_crew) DENORMALIZES a SNAPSHOT of the user's photo into the listing: `"user_avatar": display_image(u)` at write time, plus user_name/user_bio/user_primary_market. GET /crew-bank/standalone returns that stored user_avatar verbatim; `_hydrate_crew_verified` re-fetches from users but ONLY identity_verified + handle (NOT the photo). So changing your profile photo does NOT update the Crew Bank photo until you re-save the crew listing. SAME stale-snapshot pattern elsewhere: event crew sign-up (join_crew, same `"user_avatar": display_image(u)` + user_name/user_bio), and generally any denormalized author_* snapshot. NO CODE CHANGED (report only). Suggested future fix: hydrate avatar/name live from users in _hydrate_crew_verified (and crew-bank reads) instead of trusting the snapshot.
- ITEM 11 (REPORT, already-existing): comment notifications already fire and were verified previously; no change.
- ITEM 8 (code-verified only): Marketplace thumbnail + no-photo (no placeholder) was code-verified prior; could not screenshot now because the Marketplace lives under /community (RequireAuth) and the testing agent is forbidden.


## Changelog - Jun 2026 (REVIEWS-1B: personal/business target, admin-remove keeps other side, flag removed + new prompt)
Backend only (web/mobile UI in REVIEWS-2/3). Standing rules honored: no testing agent (20/20 manual httpx checks with temp accounts, all deleted), no em dashes, no publish, Campaigns OFF, no data migration (legacy defaults to business).
- A) TARGET IDENTITY: sessions + mutual_reviews carry `target_identity` ("business"|"personal", null for non-pro targets). create_session accepts `target_identity` (default "business" when reviewing a Boat Pro). Review-back mirrors the pair's identity for a Boat Pro target. compute_summary(identity=) + /reviews/summary, /reviews/list, /mutual-reviews/for all take `?identity=business|personal` (default business; business also matches legacy/missing). Business rating = business+legacy reviews only; personal rating = personal only. reviews_list suppresses the author's business name/logo when the review was authored personally (pro_pro). pro_pro rules unchanged (reviewer personal, no shared category, one-per-pair) under either target.
- B) ADMIN REMOVE keeps the other side: removing one review leaves the counterpart live and counted; session is not torn down (status -> "removed" only when none remain). Neutral notifications unchanged.
- C) FLAG REMOVED: POST /review-sessions/{id}/flag deleted (now 404); admin-notify helper + flags/flagged removed from the admin list (legacy flag data ignored). review_request notification/reminders reworded to a review-back prompt - paid: "<name> said they {worked/sailed} with you and left you a review. Do you want to review them? It stays private until you review them back or 7 days pass." meta.cta="review_back", link /reviews?session=<id>; free: "... It goes live on <date>. Upgrade to review them back." meta.cta="upgrade". "sailed with" for owner_crew/sailor_sailor/owner_owner, "worked with" otherwise. No ratings/text in any notification/email.
- Endpoint params the apps need: GET /api/reviews/summary/{id}?identity=, /api/reviews/list/{id}?identity=, /api/mutual-reviews/for/{id}?identity= (business|personal); POST /api/review-sessions body adds target_identity (+ existing as_identity for pro_pro).
- Review endpoint consumers to wire in REVIEWS-2/3: web ReviewButton.jsx, ReviewsSection.jsx, Reviews.jsx, DirectoryDetail.jsx; mobile review-compose.tsx, (tabs)/profile/reviews.tsx, ReviewsSection.tsx, NotificationsContext.tsx. Business surfaces (directory cards, business listing/detail, search) use default identity=business; personal-profile surfaces must pass identity=personal.


## Changelog - Jun 2026 (REVIEWS-1: blind two-way reviews, backend rules)
Backend only (web/mobile review UI deferred to REVIEWS-2/3). Standing rules honored: no testing agent (31/32 manual httpx checks passed with temp accounts, all deleted; the 1 "fail" was a mistargeted test assertion, feature verified separately), no em dashes, no seed data touched, no publish, Campaigns OFF. Legacy statuses (accepted/rejected/auto_revealed) kept so old demo rows don't crash; NO data migration.
- LEAK FIXED: GET /review-sessions/mine and /{id} now set show_other = live only; a reviewee never receives other_review (ratings/comment) before reveal, incl. ?session= links.
- NEW WINDOW: REVIEW_WINDOW_DAYS=7 (replaced 48h). reveal_at = first submission + 7 days. Reveal on review-back OR window end. New status "revealed" (legacy accepted/auto_revealed still counted). Nightly job + lazy-on-read reveal; skips held.
- REMINDERS: day-3 and day-6 emails (separate flags reminder_d3_sent_at/reminder_d6_sent_at), replacing the single 24h; stop once revealed/withdrawn/held; no ratings/text. review_request reworded to "<name> submitted a review of you. It stays private until you review them back or 7 days pass."
- REJECT removed -> POST .../reject returns 410. accept kept temporarily as review-back: with ratings (paid) writes + reveals both; without ratings returns a clear "stays private" error (free accounts reveal at window end).
- NEW endpoints: POST .../flag (did_not_work_together + note<=1000, notifies admins, does NOT hide/hold); POST .../withdraw (reviewer, pending-only, deletes review, status "withdrawn", notifies reviewee); PUT .../review (reviewer edit while pending, no window restart, no notify); replies (POST/DELETE .../review/{author_id}/reply two-way + POST/DELETE /reviews/{id}/reply one-way: reviewed party only, after reveal, one per review, 500 cap, free can reply, no edit, author/admin delete, notifies reviewer, returned in list/detail); admin GET /admin/review-sessions, POST .../hold|release|remove-review (reason required, logged to admin_actions, neutral both-party notifications).
- DELETE rule: author can delete own review pending-only (else 409); admin override anytime.
- PAIRING: added sailor_sailor, owner_owner, pro_pro; org->org still blocked. pro_pro allowed only when reviewer acts PERSONAL (as_identity param or stored last_identity via resolve_active_identity) AND shares NO boat_pros.categories; business identity blocked; one-per-pair (mutual_pair marker); displays reviewer personal name (business suppressed in _hydrate); counts toward reviewed pro's business rating.
- ID VERIFICATION: identity_router no longer blocks Basic Boat Pros (any paid account incl Basic can verify on personal profile). plan_features already matched; scripts/check_plan_copy.py PASSES.
- COPY: backend review notifications/emails/digest reworded to 7-day/reveal/review-back (no 48h/24h/accept/reject). Text caps enforced: comment 1000, context note 200, reply 500, dispute 1000.
- KNOWN GAP (item 10): target-side personal-vs-business separation is NOT modeled (a review always targets a user_id = business rating). Mobile lacks the business/personal switch; backend reads as_identity param + stored last_identity so it is client-agnostic. See report for mobile switch estimate.
- WEB/MOBILE still show accept/reject/48h wording on the Reviews screens (Reviews.jsx, reviews.tsx, review-compose.tsx, MutualReviewForm) - to be replaced in REVIEWS-2/3.

## Changelog - Jun 2026 (PLAN-UPGRADE-1: prorated Boat Pro tier upgrade + shared My Plan screen)
Build, web + mobile + backend. No testing agent (manual: 20/20 backend checks on temp accounts all deleted, plus web + mobile screenshots at laptop/tablet/phone). No em dashes in new copy. No seed data touched. Campaigns switch stays OFF. No CRM backfill, sync switch untouched.

PART 1 - Prorated upgrade (RULE 2, Boat Pro only). Files: backend/constants.py (BOAT_PRO_TIER_RANK, BOAT_PRO_MONTHLY, upgrade_checkout_msg), backend/routers/payments_router.py.
- compute_upgrade_quotes(user): active paid Boat Pro only; partner/admin grants out of scope. For each HIGHER tier: gap = (monthly_target - monthly_current) * current_term_months * (1 - term_discount); charge = round(gap * remaining_seconds/total_seconds, 2); comp=True when < $0.50. Term taken from subscription_package_id, else zoho_plan_term_months. total/remaining use calendar start->end (start falls back to add_months(end, -months) when subscription_started_at is null).
- GET /payments/upgrade/quote (banned -> 403). POST /payments/upgrade/checkout: comp path applies free via _apply_upgrade_direct; else one-time Stripe Checkout with metadata kind=upgrade (+ a payment_transactions row carrying a snapshot of from_tier/expires/started_at).
- _apply_upgrade_direct: sets higher tier NOW, KEEPS expiry + start, re-runs _ensure_boat_pro_listing, writes history "Upgraded (prorated)" with from/to + amount + "(Stripe test mode)" for cs_test_, enqueues plan_purchase. Stale guards: not boat_pro, tier moved, lapsed, or expiry changed since quote -> not applied.
- _apply_upgrade(session_id): idempotent on subscription_applied; stale/invalid -> needs_admin_review (reason upgrade_stale_or_invalid), money kept. _apply_tx dispatcher routes webhook + poll by tx.kind (handles double delivery). GET /payments/subscription now also returns can_upgrade + upgrades[], and months now falls back to _current_term_months (fixes "Recurring" display).
- Tests passed: quotes at day 1 (near full gap), mid-term, last day, 12-month discounted term, under-$0.50 comp applied free, two sequential upgrades (charged step by step from the then-current tier), queued plan untouched, double webhook applied once, banned quote computes but endpoint 403s, expired-during-checkout flagged needs_admin_review, partner grant not eligible, premium already = no upgrades.

PART 2 - Shared My Plan screen. Web: frontend/src/pages/MyPlan.jsx + route /my-plan (RequireAuth); "My plan" rows added to Header account dropdown + mobile drawer. Mobile: app/(tabs)/profile/my-plan.tsx + a "My plan" row in the profile menu. Shows current plan+tier (owner-only), start + end date, queued-plan line ("Next: Premium, 3 months, starts DATE"), prorated upgrade buttons (amount + unchanged end date), "Buy more time" explaining stacking (and the queue-full message when can_queue is false), and a Boat Pro "What's included" grid with locked (logo/cover/gallery/credentials) placeholders. Free accounts see "You are on the free plan" + See pricing. MyListing.jsx: gentle Basic/Featured nudge with a "See what's included" link to /my-plan; "Manage plan" now points to /my-plan. Verified responsive at laptop (1440), tablet (834), phone (390).

PART 3 - Copy + Zoho. upgrade_checkout_msg ("Upgrade now, pay only the difference for the time left. Your end date stays [date]."). Pricing (web + mobile) shows "Plans stack: a new plan starts when your current one ends." and surfaces the stacked/queue message from create_checkout (web confirm before Stripe; mobile inline note). Subscription_History variants all produced: Started, Queued (starts DATE), Upgraded (prorated), Renewed, Lapsed. At upgrade the plan_purchase enqueue rebuilds the Zoho contact from the live user doc, so Subscription_Plan (tier) updates while Subscription_Start_Date / End_Date / Plan_Term_Months stay (end date unchanged by design); event only, no backfill.

PART 4 - TERMS CHECKLIST for Jenn (plain English; rules the code now enforces, to take to counsel):
1. Plan length is counted in CALENDAR months (a plan bought on the 12th ends on the 12th; if that day does not exist, it uses the last day of that month). All dates are tracked in UTC.
2. Plans STACK. If you buy while a plan is active, the new plan is queued and starts the moment the current one ends. A free or lapsed account starts the new plan immediately.
3. You can have at most ONE plan waiting at a time.
4. A queued plan keeps the tier you bought it at. A later upgrade does not change a queued plan.
5. No refunds, credits, or proration on stacked purchases.
6. UPGRADES (Boat Pro only): moving to a higher tier takes effect immediately, keeps the same end date, and costs only the prorated price difference for the days left. If that difference is under $0.50 (Stripe's minimum) the upgrade is applied free. Sailors and boat owners have a single paid tier, so upgrades do not apply to them.
7. Start and end dates: a queued plan's start = the current plan's end date; its end = that start plus the purchased number of calendar months.
8. When a plan ends with nothing queued, the account drops to the free plan (lapses). Paid-only features (reviews, listing richness, etc.) turn off.
9. If an account is banned/suspended, a queued plan is HELD (not started, not refunded) and promotes only on unban if the active plan has by then expired.
10. On account deletion, a queued (already paid) plan is handled by an admin case by case (no automatic refund, no automatic code).
11. Payments are in Stripe TEST MODE during alpha (no real charges) until live mode is turned on.

OPEN / COULD NOT VERIFY: no live Stripe charge was run (comp path + logic verified directly; the paid Checkout + webhook path verified with a simulated paid transaction row, not a real card). No real phone (web preview only for Expo). Native iPhone/Android keyboard/inset/truncation behavior not verifiable here.
WANT TO CHANGE NEXT: fix the directory listing's transient pre-nightly "lapsed" window (it reads stored expiry and does not itself promote); decide the deletion policy for a queued paid plan (item 10); consider a small admin action on /admin/payments/review-queue to refund or manually place a flagged upgrade/duplicate.

## Changelog - Jun 2026 (ZOHO-EMAIL-STOP-0: Zoho Campaigns kill switch + email-origin investigation)
Urgent stop. No testing agent. No em dashes. No seed data altered. No emails sent. No secret printed.
WHAT CHANGED (files):

- backend/zoho.py: subscribe() and unsubscribe() short-circuit with {status:skipped, reason:campaigns_disabled} when the switch is OFF, BEFORE any _headers()/token()/HTTP call. New _campaigns_allowed() helper reads the DB switch (defaults OFF on any error). This is the single chokepoint; every caller (signup welcome, footer/newsletter box, profile newsletter toggle, opt-out/unsubscribe, payments level sync, resubscribe backfill) funnels through it.
- backend/zoho_sync.py process_outbox: camp_active now also requires campaigns_enabled(db). When Campaigns is configured but OFF and a user has opt-in drift, the row is HELD (stays pending, last_error=campaigns_held_kill_switch), NOT marked sent, and the user's zoho_campaigns_optin is NOT stamped (intent preserved). Added a held counter to the return.

- frontend/src/pages/AdminZoho.jsx: new toggle "Zoho Campaigns (can send email)" (default OFF) with a window.confirm warning before turning ON ("Turning this on lets Zoho send confirmation and marketing emails to people who subscribe.").

Zero Campaigns token refreshes and zero Campaigns HTTP calls across subscribe/unsubscribe/sync(opt_in true/false) and the outbox worker; held row stays pending; no live CRM call (mocked). Admin status returns campaigns_enabled false; toggle endpoint flips correctly; UI renders OFF with Turn on.
INVESTIGATION (item 3): the "Sail More Now - Waitlist Confirmation" email is NOT produced by our app. Our mailer.py (Resend) has no such template; the word "waitlist" only appears in auth_router comments noting app signups do NOT use the Zoho waitlist path. mailed-by mail3.zcsignup.com = Zoho Campaigns double opt-in. This burst = the ZOHO CRM backfill/reconcile outbox drain calling campaigns.subscribe for every opted-in user with list drift (the old code did this unconditionally). So YES, the backfill subscribed people to Campaigns. The "Dear test" Gmail recipient matches the account with first name "test" on a gmail address (handle testacctcreation; now also on the never-sync list). Handles that got a NEW confirmation in the burst (masked): raejen, hankcleward, ashleyburke, giblufferson/gilliangates area, jeffcarr, samjohnson, vinayaaugusty, karensmith, misstest, lizlaflove, andrewhaliburton, erichopper, eringaliger, seastr, scottcaldwell, craiggarrison, damoreclub, and more (~20). captain_jenn, jeffmcdonald (comcast), vintagerestorationsoffice were "already in list" (NO new mail). ## Changelog - Jun 2026 (PLAN-QUEUE-1: calendar-month add_months + RULE 1 plan stacking/queue)
Backend-first build + minimal web/mobile copy constants. No testing agent (27/27 manual checks on temp accounts, all cleaned up). No em dashes. Prorated tier upgrade (RULE 2) intentionally deferred to PLAN-UPGRADE-1.
- date_utils.add_months(dt, months): calendar months, UTC, keeps time-of-day, clamps the day (Jan 31 + 1 = Feb 28/29). Replaces the old 30*months math (only site was payments_router).
- user.queued_plans (array, max 1): {package_id, tier, audience, months, amount_paid, session_id, purchased_at, status}.
- payments_router: create_checkout blocks a 2nd queued plan with HTTP 409 (queue_full_msg) BEFORE Stripe, and returns will_queue + queue_message. _apply_paid_subscription branches: free/lapsed = immediate start (add_months); active = atomic QUEUE (Queued history line "starts DATE"); concurrent 2nd paid session = needs_admin_review (money never dropped). GET /payments/subscription now returns started_at, queued_plan {tier, months, starts_on, ends_on}, can_queue.
- deps.promote_or_lapse(user): lazy + nightly promotion. On lapse, promotes the queued plan (start = old expiry instant, end = add_months, re-runs boat-pro listing, Started/Renewed history + Zoho event) atomically/idempotently; drops to free + Lapsed only when the queue is empty. No-op if not actually expired. Banned users held; promote on unban.
- scheduler nightly: winback skips banned + queued; 2c iterates lapsed non-banned users through promote_or_lapse instead of a blanket downgrade.
- admin_router: GET /admin/payments/review-queue surfaces needs_admin_review transactions.
- constants: TIER_LABELS, tier_label, PLAN_STACK_PRICING_LINE, stacked_checkout_msg, queue_full_msg (shared copy; no em dashes).
- subscription_expires readers that respect promotion (read post-promotion field): deps.can_review, directory listing visibility (transient pre-nightly window only), admin active-member counts, community lapsed-owner query, scheduler expiring/winback/renewal. None downgrade independently of promote_or_lapse.
- PLAN-UPGRADE-1 (next): RULE 2 prorated Boat Pro tier upgrade; also fix directory transient-lapse window; decide queued-plan-on-delete/ban policy.

Added `crm.mark_account_deleted(app_user_id)` (find by App_User_ID, set App_Account_Status=Deleted, KEEP record) and changed process_outbox so a hard-deleted (missing) user is marked Deleted in Zoho instead of silently skipped - never a hard delete. NOTE: jenntest/misstest are still member_status=active in THIS preview DB (no user-delete code path exists; "there is no self-serve delete"), so the soft-delete path could not be live-exercised here.
- PART 2a (Paid rule): any completed Stripe checkout (test or live) while active -> Subscription_Status=Paid (expired -> Cancelled). Added "(Stripe test mode)" to the Subscription_History line when the session id starts cs_test_. Never send Trial/Founding Member Trial (no trial state exists).
- PART 2b (granted partners): changed org branch to leave Subscription_Status EMPTY (was "Paid"). Chose EMPTY over Free because Free is dishonest for a comped partner; their status lives in Org_Status (Founding Partner / Community Partner) + Founding_Member.
- PART 3 (plan-length backfill, approved): filled `zoho_plan_term_months` from the single matching completed transaction and a Zoho-ONLY `zoho_sub_start` (real subscription_started_at left untouched; build_contact_fields now falls back to zoho_sub_start for Subscription_Start_Date). 20 users updated + enqueued; jeffmcdonald left empty (ambiguous 3 vs 12 month paid txns); 3 granted partners left empty. The every-2-minute drain (sync already ON) synced all 20 (sent=20, failed=0); I did not force-run anything.
- Remaining outbox failures: 4 rows = 2 fake-email accounts (oliveowner @ex.com, shilsholesailingsoci @example.com) failing Campaigns only; now covered by the domain guard and will self-clear to skipped on the next drain. Their CRM Contacts are fine.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (ZOHO-CRM-6: URGENT backfill fix; datetime format, full errors, throttling, background job)
No testing agent (live drain observed + unit tests). No em dashes. No seed data touched. No emails sent. No secret read/printed. The live backfill had failed with INVALID_DATA on datetime fields; now fixed and re-run via the drain worker (76 sent).
- CAUSE 1 (datetime INVALID_DATA on App_Signup_Date): Mongo stores ISO with microseconds (e.g. 2026-10-18T18:40:34.932524+00:00); Zoho datetime fields need ISO 8601 with offset and NO microseconds. Added `_zoho_datetime` (naive=UTC, strips microseconds, keeps offset) and `_zoho_date` (yyyy-MM-dd) in zoho_sync.py; applied to App_Signup_Date, App_Last_Active, Subscription_Start_Date, Subscription_End_Date. Added `_coerce_types` that coerces EVERY field to the live Zoho data_type (boolean/integer/double/datetime/date/multiselect/text-with-length) using new field metadata (`crm._fieldmeta` incl. `length`) so we never hit INVALID_DATA one field at a time. Unit-checked with 6 datetime shapes + a coercion set.
- CAUSE 2 (same user twice): outbox has one row per event; recent_failures now returns ONE row per user (coalesced) with a structured `error_detail` {code, api_name, message, stage}; admin list shows 2 lines + a Details expand toggle.
- CAUSE 3 (crm:network: empty message): httpx timeout/SSL exceptions stringify empty; `_request` now includes the exception TYPE (network:<Type>:<msg|no_detail>) and surfaces 429 Retry-After. Added per-user throttle (0.3s + jitter) and `_sync_with_retry` (exponential backoff + jitter, honors Retry-After) for transient errors (network/5xx/429); 4xx like INVALID_DATA fail fast.
- CAUSE 4 (14 email-only Contacts from lead convert): the convert succeeded but the follow-up field update failed on the datetime bug, leaving email-only Contacts. CONFIRMED idempotent adoption: on re-run, find-by-App_User_ID misses, find-by-email ADOPTS the converted Contact and update_contact fills every field (App_User_ID, Market, etc.) with NO duplicate. The live re-drain did exactly this.
- BACKGROUND JOB (fixes the Cloudflare banner): Live backfill + Reconcile no longer run in one long HTTP request. New `start_job`/`_run_job`/`job_status` run them as an asyncio task writing progress to app_meta `zoho_crm_job` {status, kind, total, sent, failed, skipped, remaining, message}. Endpoints `/admin/zoho/crm/backfill/run` and `/reconcile` now return `{started:true}`; new `GET /admin/zoho/crm/job`; the admin page polls every 2s and shows a progress banner. `retry-failed` now hard-requeues (status->pending, attempts->0, clears error) so rows past MAX_ATTEMPTS retry too.
- REQUEUE DONE: all 80 failed rows (40 users, 2 events each) requeued; the 2-min drain worker then synced them against LIVE Zoho: sent=76, failed=4. The 4 failures are 2 QA accounts with non-deliverable emails (owner_5115@ex.com, qa.club1814@example.com) that Zoho CAMPAIGNS rejects; their CRM Contact sync SUCCEEDED. Campaigns error detail now includes http + body + exception type.
- ACCOUNTS: no `created an Account` warnings in the logs during the successful drain, so the lead-convert path created no Zoho Accounts (no Leads forced one). Could not independently query Zoho Accounts here.
- DID NOT start a new live backfill and DID NOT change the sync switch (left ON as Jenn set it).
- COULD NOT VERIFY: Zoho's exact acceptance was only observed via the live drain (76 sent, 0 datetime errors), which confirms the format; I did not separately read the Zoho API docs page in this session but the format matches the known v3 spec (ISO 8601 + offset, no milliseconds). `+00:00` offset is used for UTC values; Zoho accepts it.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (ZOHO-CRM-5: secure in-app CRM credential screen + DB sync switch; works in preview, CRM OFF by default)
No testing agent (manual unit tests of the secrets module + endpoint wiring curl + frontend compile). No em dashes. No seed data touched. No emails sent. No real secret read/printed. The CRM refresh token is encrypted at rest and never returned/logged.
- NEW `backend/zoho_secrets.py`: Fernet-encrypted refresh-token store in the `zoho_secrets` collection (doc key "crm", field `refresh_token_enc`), key from NEW env `ZOHO_SECRETS_KEY` (generated by the agent and written to backend/.env WITHOUT printing). Helpers: store/get/token_present/token_source/token_updated_at/delete; DB sync switch (set_sync_enabled/sync_enabled, default OFF, takes precedence over ZOHO_CRM_ENABLED, stored in app_meta "zoho_crm_switch"); connection-test timestamp (record_conn_test/last_conn_test in app_meta "zoho_crm_conn_test"); and `exchange_grant_code` (server-side grant_type=authorization_code exchange at ZOHO_ACCOUNTS_URL using the EXISTING ZOHO_CLIENT_ID/SECRET; returns ok + token or an error CODE; the code, token and secret are never logged or returned).
- TOKEN PRECEDENCE: encrypted DB token first, then ZOHO_CRM_REFRESH_TOKEN env fallback. `zoho_crm.ZohoCRM.set_refresh_token()` points the client at the resolved token and clears its access-token cache. `zoho_sync.process_outbox` and `backfill` now resolve `sync_enabled(db)` for the on/off gate and prime the token from the store before any CRM call.
- NEW backend endpoints (all admin, rate-limited, audited via audit.log_admin_action entity "zoho_crm"; none ever return a token value):
  - GET `/admin/zoho/crm/connection` and the extended GET `/admin/zoho/crm/status` return only booleans + timestamps (token_present, token_source stored/env, token_updated_at, sync_enabled, client_present, last_conn_test).
  - POST `/admin/zoho/crm/exchange-code` {code}: exchanges + stores; maps Zoho errors to plain English (expired/used code, wrong client, missing scopes, no token, data-center hint) with no secret echoed.
  - POST `/admin/zoho/crm/set-token` {refresh_token}: direct paste fallback, stored encrypted.
  - POST `/admin/zoho/crm/disconnect`: deletes the stored token AND forces sync OFF.
  - POST `/admin/zoho/crm/test`: read-only Contacts field-metadata call; records the conn-test timestamp; returns Connected yes/no + a scopes/auth error + the Market mapping table. No writes.
  - POST `/admin/zoho/crm/sync-toggle` {on}: ON requires a token present AND a connection test that passed within the last hour, else 400.
  - Live backfill still requires a dry run in the last hour AND sync ON (now from the DB switch), with the confirm modal. Seed/test accounts still excluded.
- FRONTEND `AdminZoho.jsx`: new "CRM connection" section (responsive): client/token/updated pills, sync On/Off with a confirm modal, Test connection (shows Connected + market table), a write-only grant-code input (type=password) with Connect, a write-only refresh-token input with Save, and Disconnect. Secrets inputs are cleared on success; nothing secret is ever displayed.
- SECURITY: the token/code never appear in logs, responses, the outbox, console, or this PRD; exception paths log generic messages only. Verified: encryption round-trip works and the stored ciphertext does not contain the plaintext; endpoints return 401 without an admin session; a live grant-code exchange with a fake code reached Zoho and returned the mapped "invalid_code" message (so the real exchange path is wired correctly).
- PREVIEW vs PRODUCTION: this screen writes to the database, so it works in THIS preview (where Jenn's alpha lives) with no Secrets tab needed. In production later, the same screen is the way to connect: open /admin/zoho, paste a fresh grant code (or token), run Test, then turn sync ON. Production will need its own ZOHO_SECRETS_KEY set once (and its own token, re-entered via the same screen).
- COULD NOT VERIFY: a full live grant-code -> refresh-token -> Contacts write round-trip (requires Jenn's real Self Client code and CRM scopes); the admin page rendered while signed in (Google OAuth is not automatable in the preview). Jenn to connect on /admin/zoho and run Test.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (ZOHO-CRM-4: picklist display/actual mapping + newsletter market segmentation; CRM gated OFF)
No testing agent (manual py mapping unit tests + frontend compile + endpoint wiring). No em dashes. No seed data touched. No emails sent. No .env values changed. ZOHO_CRM_ENABLED still false.
- (A, CRM picklists) `zoho_crm.py`: `read_fields` now keeps BOTH `display_value` and `actual_value` per picklist entry; `load_picklists` stores (display, actual) pairs; replaced `valid_picklist` with `map_picklist(field, value)` which matches the app value against a live entry's display OR actual (exact, case + spacing) and returns the entry's `actual_value` (the write value; display is the fallback), else None. `zoho_sync._validate_picklists` rewritten to use `map_picklist` for EVERY validated picklist (Market, Subscription_Plan, Subscription_Status, Billing_Cycle, Contact_Types, Org_Status, Signup_Source, ID_Verification, Listing_Status, App_Account_Status) so a future relabel never breaks sync. Market never fails (falls back to Other, logged). Verified by unit test: Greater Seattle->Seattle, Portland/Vancouver->Portland, Anacortes/San Juan Islands->Anacortes, unknown->Other, Quarterly (not yet in live Billing_Cycle)->dropped+logged.
- (A, admin preview) Dry-run backfill now returns `market_map` {live, rows:[{app, sends}]} computed from the LIVE picklist (needs only read creds, works with sync gated off). `AdminZoho.jsx` renders a "Market field mapping" table in the dry-run result (or "connect credentials to preview" when metadata is not loadable).
- (B, Campaigns market) `zoho.py`: new `ZOHO_CAMPAIGNS_MARKET_FIELD` env (default "Market"). Subscribe now sends the user's exact app primary market (e.g. "Portland/Vancouver") as a list field; if the subscribe-with-field call returns non-200 it retries a PLAIN subscribe (field never blocks the opt-in) and logs `extra_field_dropped`. `process_outbox` Campaigns dedupe is now state-change aware on opt-in AND primary market (`zoho_campaigns_optin` + new `zoho_campaigns_market`), so a market change re-syncs for segmentation but an unchanged user is never re-subscribed. Followed-markets field: SKIPPED (comma-list segmentation in Campaigns is unreliable; primary market covers the need).
- COULD NOT VERIFY (no live Zoho creds here): whether the CRM write API strictly requires actual_value vs display (we send actual, which is the documented v3 behavior; matching tolerates both; a mismatch would surface as a visible outbox failure, never a silent drop); whether Zoho Campaigns returns non-200 for an unknown custom field (our plain-subscribe retry fires on non-200; if Zoho instead returns 200 and ignores the unknown field, the opt-in still succeeds and the field is simply not set until Jenn creates it).

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (ZOHO-CRM-2: Jenn's field decisions + admin web page; CRM stays gated OFF)
No testing agent (manual py checks + endpoint wiring curl; admin page is behind Google OAuth so not screenshot-verified). No em dashes. No seed data touched. No emails sent. ZOHO_CRM_ENABLED still false.

  - community_partner (org) -> Subscription_Plan EMPTY, Status "Paid", Org_Status "Community Partner"
  - founding_partner (org) -> Subscription_Plan EMPTY, Status "Paid", Org_Status "Founding Partner", Founding_Member true

Only sent if present in the live Billing_Cycle picklist, else dropped + logged. `Plan_Term_Months` sends the integer 3; the Subscription_History line renders "3 months". Verified via unit check of build_contact_fields.
- (4 Topic) ZOHO_CAMPAIGNS_TOPIC_ID left unset; no change.
- (5 Admin page) NEW `frontend/src/pages/AdminZoho.jsx` at route `/admin/zoho`, linked from the admin menu (Header.jsx adminLinks "Zoho sync"), admin-guarded, responsive (grids collapse on phone/tablet). Shows: CRM enabled/credentials-present pills, API domain, outbox counts (pending/sent/failed/skipped), recent failures, sync exclusions, and the Campaigns connection check (connected / list key / list names / missing env). Buttons: Dry run backfill (renders eligible + excluded HANDLES with reasons, never emails), Retry failed, Reconcile, and Live backfill (disabled until a dry run in the last hour exists AND sync is on; opens a confirm modal). All call the existing `/api/admin/zoho/crm/*` + `/api/admin/zoho/campaigns/check` endpoints.
- Logging: `sync_user_to_crm` now logs dropped/mapped picklist values per user.
- VERIFIED: zoho_sync compiles; build_contact_fields unit check across 6 account shapes (no App_Plan_Tier / App_Primary_Market; correct Plan/Status/Org_Status/Billing/Market); frontend compiles (only pre-existing exhaustive-deps warnings, none in AdminZoho.jsx); endpoints respond 401 (auth) / 405 (method) without a session, confirming wiring.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (REFRESH-0: one shared "content changed" signal so lists refresh after create/edit/delete — MOBILE fix; web already fresh)
No testing agent (manual code audit + tsc + Expo bundle smoke screenshot). No em dashes. Seed data untouched. Native iPhone + authed tab lists (behind Google OAuth) NOT verifiable in the web preview.
- PART A (audit): WEB is already fresh — Feed.jsx/Events.jsx/CrewBank.jsx use inline forms/modals on the list page and call load() (or optimistic setPosts) after create/edit/delete; separate edit routes remount the list on return. MOBILE was the problem: only the Feed listened (to MARKETPLACE_CHANGED) and only for for_sale; the Events tab had NO focus-refetch and NO listener, so new/edited/cancelled events never showed until a manual pull. Crew Bank (CrewBankScreen), My-* screens, InlineComments and Messages already refetch on focus and were fine. Backend GET /search (admin_router) and GET /posts?q= (community_router) are live DB regex queries with NO cache and NO scheduled index, so new items are searchable IMMEDIATELY server-side; the "not searchable until later" report was the same client-staleness root cause, no backend change needed.
- PART B (fix, mobile only): NEW shared signal CONTENT_CHANGED in src/lib/events.ts, payload { kind: post|discussion|event|crew, action: create|edit|delete, item?, id? }. Feed list (app/(tabs)/feed/index.tsx) now listens to CONTENT_CHANGED (replacing the marketplace-only listener): on a post create with item it optimistically prepends at top (guarded by id; only in the recency-sorted post views + market match) then always refetches; the refetch replaces the array so no duplicate. Events list (app/(tabs)/events/index.tsx) listens to CONTENT_CHANGED kind=event and reloads (date-sorted, so no top-insert). Emitters wired: new-post (create all types + edit), hub/new (forum/blog create + edit), new-event (single create / series create / edit), edit-post, delete-post, crew-listing, cancel-event, PostDetailScreen (sold + price). MARKETPLACE_CHANGED kept for the targeted in-place consumers (PostDetailScreen, profile listings). Pull-to-refresh unchanged.
- VERIFIED: mobile tsc at the known baseline (17 pre-existing errors, none in changed files); Expo web bundle compiles and renders (no error screen). NOT verifiable here: the authed Feed/Events tab lists (Google OAuth login not automatable in the preview) and native iPhone behavior — Jenn to confirm the "new item shows without manual refresh" on device.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (ZOHO-CRM-0: report only — why alpha signups never reached Zoho CRM + proposed design. NO code/data changes.)
No testing agent. No writes to Zoho. Read-only DB audit via a temp script (deleted). Findings: the app talks ONLY to Zoho Campaigns (newsletter) via backend/zoho.py; nothing anywhere calls Zoho CRM. zoho.sync is invoked from exactly two places and only for opted-in users: PATCH /api/auth/newsletter (auth_router.py:488) and the subscription-apply path (payments_router.py:134-143). Sign-up (email /auth/register:222, Google /auth/session:133) and onboarding (/auth/onboarding) do NOT call Zoho at all; they insert the user and (register only) send a Resend welcome email. The admin "backfill" (POST /admin/zoho/backfill, auth_router.py:686) pushes newsletter_opt_in users to CAMPAIGNS, not CRM. Zoho is effectively a no-op in this env: ready_for_list_ops is false unless ZOHO_* env vars + ZOHO_CAMPAIGNS_LIST_KEY are set (they are not here), so every subscribe/unsubscribe logs "skip ... (no list key)". Net: NO user has ever reached Zoho CRM because no CRM integration exists. This env DB_NAME=test_database (51 users, 40 likely-real, 11 seed/demo/.dev) is NOT production; the real backfill must run in the deployed prod environment. Full field mapping, module choice (Leads vs Contacts), outbox/retry reliability design, test-account exclusion, OAuth scopes, and backfill plan delivered to Jenn in chat. Pending her decisions before any build.
