## Changelog — Jun 2026 (PW-1: password reset — backend + web + reset email)
No testing agent (manual httpx/motor test + screenshots). Routed through integration_expert (custom JWT/bcrypt auth playbook) before writing. No em dashes in UI/email copy. No Stripe. Native iPhone NOT verifiable in web preview.
- BACKEND `routers/auth_router.py`: `POST /api/auth/forgot-password {email}` always returns the same 200 `{"message":"If an account exists for that email, we sent a reset link."}` (unknown / Google-only / normal all identical). Normal account: SHA-256 hash of `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)` stored in new `password_resets` collection (user_id, expires_at = now+60min ISO, used flag); prior unused tokens for that user are invalidated; reset email sent. Google-only (no password_hash): sends the Google-variant email, no token. Unknown: nothing. Rate limit: 3/email/hour and 10/IP/hour via new `password_reset_requests` collection; when limited, same 200 and nothing sent. `POST /api/auth/reset-password {token,new_password}`: verifies SHA-256 hash + not-expired + not-used, enforces the SAME `_validate_password_strength` as register, sets new bcrypt hash, marks token used (single use), clears login lockout (`login_attempts` rows for that email), and invalidates ALL sessions (`user_sessions.delete_many({user_id})`). Invalid/expired/used → one generic `400 "This reset link is invalid or has expired. Request a new one."` Register 409 changed to "An account with this email already exists. Sign in or use Forgot password." Login messages left as-is.
- `server.py`: indexes on `password_resets.token_hash`, `password_resets.user_id`, `password_reset_requests.created_at`.
- `mailer.py`: `app_public_url()` + `password_reset_email_html` (orange "Reset your password" -> {base}/reset-password?token=..., "works for 60 minutes and once", "If you did not ask for this, you can ignore this email.") and `password_reset_google_email_html`. Subject "Reset your Sail More Now password". Same navy/teal/orange palette + LOGO_ROUND_URL as welcome.
- APP URL SETTING: name = `APP_PUBLIC_URL` (backend/.env); current value = `https://owsa-events-preview--7evdsk8f.preview.emergentagent.com` (so reset links resolve in the preview). mailer fallback if unset = https://app.sailmorenow.com (not live). Change APP_PUBLIC_URL when the real domain goes live.
- WEB: `Login.jsx` "Forgot password?" link (login mode) -> `/forgot-password`. New `pages/ForgotPassword.jsx` (/forgot-password) and `pages/ResetPassword.jsx` (/reset-password?token=), routes added to `App.js`. Styled like Login; responsive light/dark.
- SESSIONS ON RESET: all existing sessions for the user are deleted from `user_sessions` (the app's opaque session store) so the old password cannot keep a session alive; user must sign in again with the new password.
- VERIFIED (temp account on delivered@resend.dev, then deleted): unknown == normal identical 200; Google-only path creates 0 tokens; 4 requests -> 3 tokens (4th blocked, still 200); valid reset works (pw changed, 0 sessions left, 0 lockout rows left); token reuse -> 400; expired -> 400; weak password -> 400 strength. Web pages render + states correct at 1440 and 390, light and dark. Managed email proxy ACCEPTED the safe delivered@resend.dev sink.
- MOBILE reset screen is NOT in this task (PW-2).

(6) TestimonialsStrip moved above FoundingWall, both below the CTA. (7) Removed CommunitySample from Landing (file kept).

- WAVES/BACKGROUNDS: driven by one in-order sequence of sections + a single `Wave` helper placed BETWEEN sections (never inside one); replaced the old inside-section `TopWave`. `Wave` paints above-curve = section-above color (svg currentColor) and its wrapper bg = section-below color shows beneath the curve; marginTop/Bottom -1px kills hairlines. ## Changelog — Jun 2026 (R5g: close event posting for sailors + boat owners for the alpha — server + web + mobile)
No testing agent (forbidden). Manual verification via a temp-data python script (deleted after). No em dashes in new copy. Seed data (Wash N Go, PSSS, WTYC, seed Boat Pros) untouched. KeyboardFormScreen / MultiSelectPicker / shared Button defaults NOT touched. Native iPhone behavior NOT verifiable in the web preview (Jenn to confirm on device). DECISION (alpha): only clubs/orgs (incl. community partners and free partner orgs), Boat Pros and admin can post events; sailors and boat owners (paid or free) cannot, because editing + event guidelines do not exist for them yet. GATE-1 org rules preserved.
- REPORT (confirmed in code): Server `POST /events` (community_router.py) before this change: 401 if signed out; rate-limited; free non-partner (sailor/owner/free Boat Pro) -> require_interaction -> 403 upgrade_required; free partner org -> volunteer-only + 1/month (402 upgrade_required); any PAID account (incl. paid sailor/owner) + partners -> allowed. So a PAID sailor or PAID owner COULD create events. Web: `canPostEvents = partner || boat_pro || admin` already hid the Events "Post event" button + "My events" link, and the Dashboard "Post an event" card only showed for partner; BUT the `?post=1` deep-link opened the create form regardless, and MyEvents showed a "Post event" link to anyone who landed there. Mobile: add-event entries were gated only by `isFreeIndividual` (so a PAID sailor/owner still saw them): events tab create-event-btn, My Events "Post" button, feed compose sheet "New event" row; the My Events tab icon was already partner-only. No event entry in the web feed/new-post chooser or in Crew Bank on either platform.
- SERVER (community_router.py): added, right after the rate limit, a block for `account_type in ("sailor","boat_owner") and not is_admin(u)` -> 403 `{code: "event_posting_closed", message: "Event posting is open to clubs, organizations and Boat Pros for now."}`. NOT upgrade_required. Orgs/partners/Boat Pros/admin fall through to the unchanged GATE-1 rules.
- MOBILE: new `canPostEvents(user)` helper in `src/lib/plan.ts` (partner OR boat_pro OR platform_role admin; isFreeIndividual kept). Hid the add-event entries for everyone else: events tab create-event-btn (`events/index.tsx`), My Events "Post" button (`my-events.tsx`), feed compose "New event" row (`feed/index.tsx`, conditionally spread). `new-event.tsx` submit: on a 403 with code `event_posting_closed` shows the server message as a plain toast; org 402 upgrade_required still routes to the upgrade prompt (unchanged).
- WEB: gated the Events `?post=1` create form render with `canPostEvents` (`Events.jsx`) so a direct `/events?post=1` cannot open the form for sailors/owners; gated the MyEvents "Post event" link with `partner||boat_pro||admin` (`MyEvents.jsx`). Events button/My-events link + Dashboard card were already partner/pro/admin only. Existing events, editing, RSVP, volunteering and crew-pool joining unchanged.
- VERIFIED (temp accounts + temp events, ALL deleted after; 0 leftover temp events/users/sessions; seed boat_pros still 5): paid sailor, free sailor, paid owner each -> 403 event_posting_closed; community-partner org, Boat Pro, and a premium boat_owner+admin (the real admin shape) each -> 200 create; free partner org non-volunteer -> 402 upgrade_required, 1st volunteer -> 200, 2nd in month -> 402 upgrade_required (1/month intact); signed out -> 401. NOTE: a hypothetical FREE sailor/owner admin still hits the pre-existing require_interaction (admin handling intentionally unchanged); the actual admin is a premium boat_owner so posts fine. Mobile tsc back to the baseline 17 pre-existing errors (none in changed files; events/index EventMap + id-any are baseline); web compiles.
- UNVERIFIED: real iPhone rendering of the hidden add-event entries and the plain-toast fallback cannot be verified in the web preview.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (BP-4b: delete pending + mutual two-way reviews — backend + web + mobile)
No testing agent (forbidden). Manual verification via a temp-data python script (deleted after). No em dashes. Seed data (Wash N Go, PSSS, WTYC, seed Boat Pros) untouched. Native iPhone behavior NOT verifiable in the web preview (Jenn to confirm on device). Scope (a): endpoint is admin-capable; admin Delete added only where admins already see reviews (public LIVE two-way lists). No new admin pending-reviews screen (post-alpha).
- REPORT (confirmed in code): One-way ORG reviews are never pending (instant LIVE in `reviews`; already deletable via `DELETE /reviews/{id}` + `DELETE /admin/content/review/{id}`). Two-way reviews live in `review_sessions` (status pending/accepted/rejected/auto_revealed) + `mutual_reviews` (one doc per author). "Pending" = reviewer submitted, review hidden, reviewee has 48h to accept/reject (else auto-reveal). The author sees a pending review under "Waiting for others", the reviewee under "Needs your response" (`GET /review-sessions/mine`; web `/reviews`, mobile Profile>Reviews). Admins see pending reviews NOWHERE (no review queue; reviews are not reportable, so there was no admin notification or open report on a pending review). Before this change, a two-way review could NOT be deleted by anyone in any state (reject only hides).
- BACKEND (`routers/reviews_router.py`): new `DELETE /review-sessions/{session_id}/review/{author_id}` — allowed for that review's author OR an admin. Deletes that one `mutual_reviews` doc; when no reviews remain on the session it tears down the session, removes ALL notifications with `meta.session_id == session_id` (incl. the reviewee's pending `review_request`), and defensively closes any open report pointing at the session. Partial delete (one side of a live two-way) keeps the session and the other side intact; averages recompute live. `GET /reviews/list/{target}` now sets `can_delete` for two_way live items (author or admin) and carries `review_session_id` + `review_author_id` so the client picks the right delete call.
- WEB: `pages/Reviews.jsx` inbox — author Delete + confirm dialog on their own pending review ("Waiting for others") and their own live review ("Your review" in Live). `components/ReviewsSection.jsx` — Delete on two-way live items routes to the new endpoint (one-way still uses `DELETE /reviews/{id}`); admins see Delete on any live two-way via `can_delete`.
- MOBILE: `app/(tabs)/profile/reviews.tsx` — author Delete via native Alert (44pt, full "Delete review" label) on pending + own live review. `src/components/reviews/ReviewsSection.tsx` — two-way Delete routes to the new endpoint; admin Delete on any live two-way.
- VERIFIED (temp accounts/sessions/review_sessions/mutual_reviews/notifications, ALL deleted after; 0 leftover; seed boat_pros still 5): non-author/non-admin blocked 403 (notif untouched); author deletes own pending -> session gone, mutual_review gone, reviewee `review_request` notification removed (count 1 -> 0, `notifications_removed`=1); live two-way both-sided -> delete one side keeps session + other side + its notifications (count unchanged), deleting the last side tears down session and clears all its notifications; admin can delete any (session torn down, notif cleanup verified); missing session -> 404. `/reviews/list` exposes can_delete+ids correctly for author/admin and false for other/anon, and delete via those ids returns 200. Web compiles; mobile tsc unchanged (exactly 17 pre-existing errors, none in changed files).
- UNVERIFIED: real iPhone native Alert rendering/tap behavior in the mobile reviews inbox and ReviewsSection cannot be verified in the web preview.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (MSG-2: 403 + "Business" header when messaging a Boat Pro — BACKEND fix, web+mobile benefit)
- CAUSE (one root cause, two symptoms): `GET /api/messages/{thread_id}` and `POST /api/messages/{thread_id}/read` validated the caller by splitting the thread_id on "-" when the thread doc did not exist yet (brand-new conversation). That split is wrong for business threads (the "~b" suffix) and for UUID user_ids (dashes), so a paid user opening a not-yet-created Boat Pro thread got **403 Forbidden**. On web the Boat Pro Message button routes to `/messages?to=<id>&as=business`; the resolver's business branch is `api.get('/boat-pros/{id}').then(d => openDirect(company_name || "Business")).catch(() => openDirect("Business"))`. `openDirect` sets the header to the real company name, then its `GET /messages/{tid}` 403s; that rejection flows into the `.catch`, which re-runs `openDirect(..., "Business")` and overwrites the good name. So the 403 crash (Uncaught runtime error overlay, doubled by StrictMode) AND the literal "Business" header both came from the same 403.
- MSG-1 did NOT cause it: the `as=business` branch and `openDirect` were untouched by MSG-1 (verified via `git show` of the MSG-1 commit). Pre-existing; just newly noticed. MSG-1's personal-thread peer-name fix remains intact.
- FIX (smallest, backend only — `backend/routers/messaging_router.py`): in `list_messages`, if the thread doc does not exist return `[]` (no messages to leak); in `mark_thread_read`, if it does not exist return `{ok:true}` no-op. Removed the unreliable `thread_id.split("-")` entirely; the participant check now runs only when the thread exists (`thread.participants`). No frontend change needed — the business `.catch` no longer fires because the 403 is gone, so the real company name stays.
- VERIFIED (TEMP A paid boat_owner, B boat_pro+listing "Harbor Rigging Co", C free; UUID ids; deleted after): new business thread GET+read now 200; new personal thread 200; browser: business header "Harbor Rigging Co" / tag HARBOR RIGGING CO, personal header "Bruno Pro" / tag PERSONAL, zero overlay, zero 403; existing-thread participant check intact (A 200, non-participant C 403); free send still gated (403 upgrade_required); MSG-1 crew-bank personal path still correct. All temp data deleted (3 users, 1 boat_pro, 3 sessions, 1 thread, 1 msg, 1 notif, 13 listing_views; 0 remaining); seed_marina intact.
- MOBILE (report only, NOT changed): `mobile/app/messages/[id].tsx` always builds the PERSONAL thread id (`[user,id].sort().join('-')`, no "~b") and wraps the load GET in try/catch -> `setMessages([])`, so it silently swallowed the same new-thread 403 (no crash, title came from the nav param = correct). The shared backend fix now turns that silent 403 into a clean 200. No mobile code change.


## Changelog — Jun 2026 (MSG-1: thread header showed "Sail More Now member" — WEB fix, mobile report-only)
- CAUSE: web `Messages.jsx` `?to=` resolver only sourced a name from an existing thread or the `/boat-pros` list; for a NEW thread with a non-Boat-Pro target (e.g. a Crew Bank crew member) it fell back to the literal "Sail More Now member". After switching threads and back, `/messages/threads` now had the row so `openThread` showed the real name — matching Jenn's report. All entry points (Crew Bank card, Crew Listing popup, profile Message, event crew row) hit the same resolver via `MessageLink` -> `/messages?to=<id>`.

- VERIFIED (temp paid users A+B via API+cookie, deleted after): peer endpoint 200/401/404; new-thread first open header = "Bob Crewmate · PERSONAL" (no "Sail More Now member"), existing-thread first open = same, left list matches, no console errors. Did NOT change sending, list, unread, or the free-account gate.
- MOBILE (report only, NOT changed): mobile passes the name as a nav param from the calling screen (which already has it) and renders `<Screen title={name || "Chat"}>` synchronously; the "Sail More Now member" string does not exist in mobile. Worst case is a generic "Sailor"/"Organizer"/"Chat" only when the source record truly lacks a name. Not the reported bug, no fix made.


## Changelog — Jun 2026 (R3d-2c: comment box not scrolling above keyboard — MOBILE ONLY)
No testing agent. Web/backend/billing + protected accounts + Profile tab fix + review field order untouched. No em dashes. Native keyboard NOT testable in web preview (Jenn must confirm on iPhone).
- **Root cause:** the old scroll-into-view used `TextInput.State.currentlyFocusedInput().measureLayout(...)` fired ONLY on keyboard-show, with an empty onFail callback, so when it failed for the comment box it failed silently (no per-input onFocus; switching inputs did not re-scroll). "What was this for?" happened to work; the comment did not. Also the Admin Support reply and FAQ edit were inline inputs in a long scroll that did NOT use KeyboardFormScreen.
- **Fix (deterministic, no measureLayout):** `KeyboardFormScreen` now registers each input via `useKbField(id)` -> `onLayout` records the field's y, `onFocus` scrolls to it. Wired over DeviceEventEmitter (NOT React context, because the hook is called in the parent screen which renders above the provider). On focus (and again on keyboard-show) it `scrollTo(y - 16)`; the footer is lifted by the keyboard height and the ScrollView bottom padding = keyboard height + footer reserve so lower inputs reach the top. `keyboardDismissMode="none"` (drag never closes) + header "Done" button dismisses. A temporary KB-DEBUG line (`KB_DEBUG = true` in KeyboardFormScreen.tsx, remove before ship) shows kbHeight / focused id / target offset.
- **Applied to every screen using KeyboardFormScreen:** review-compose (comment + "What was this for?" + one-way comment), report (detail), admin-edit (all fields). **Moved onto the shell:** Admin Support reply (new `app/admin-reply.tsx`) and Admin FAQ edit (new `app/admin-faq-edit.tsx`); AdminScreen now navigates to them and refreshes via `ADMIN_SUPPORT_CHANGED` / `ADMIN_FAQS_CHANGED`.
- **Verified here (web, deterministic path only):** focusing the comment sets focused=comment, target=457 and scrolls it into view; context sets target=575. kbHeight stays 0 on web (no native keyboard) — the keyboard overlap itself is NOT verifiable in the preview.
- Files new: `app/admin-reply.tsx`, `app/admin-faq-edit.tsx`. Changed: `src/components/KeyboardFormScreen.tsx`, `app/review-compose.tsx`, `app/report.tsx`, `app/admin-edit.tsx`, `src/screens/AdminScreen.tsx`, `src/lib/events.ts`.


## Changelog — Jun 2026 (R3d-2 addition: sheets converted to full-screen routes — MOBILE ONLY)
No testing agent. Web/backend/billing + protected accounts + review field order untouched. R3d-1 (Profile tab) untouched. No em dashes. Native keyboard NOT testable in web preview (Jenn must confirm on iPhone).
- **Root cause (confirmed):** all input sheets rendered inside a React Native `<Modal>`; on iOS `KeyboardAvoidingView` mis-measures inside a Modal (separate native view hierarchy), and the old ScrollView lift did not keep fields visible. Per the user's instruction, the review form, Report and Admin edit were converted from Modal sheets to full-SCREEN expo-router routes using the proven `messages/[id]` pattern (screen-level `KeyboardAvoidingView` behavior padding/height + `keyboardVerticalOffset={insets.top + 52}`, scrollable content, footer pinned as the last KAV child).
- **New files:** `src/components/KeyboardFormScreen.tsx` (shared keyboard-safe full-screen shell: Screen+Back, KAV, scroll-into-view on focus via `TextInput.State.currentlyFocusedInput().measureLayout`, `keyboardDismissMode="none"`, a header "Done" button to dismiss the keyboard), `app/review-compose.tsx` (write / one-way / review-back + two-way success), `app/report.tsx`, `app/admin-edit.tsx`, `src/lib/events.ts` (DeviceEventEmitter names).
- **Rewired triggers:** `ReviewButton` navigates to `/review-compose` (keeps elig + upgrade line + openSignal); `reviews.tsx` "Review back" navigates there (mode=back) and reloads on `REVIEWS_CHANGED`; `ReportButton` navigates to `/report`; `AdminControls` Edit navigates to `/admin-edit` and updates via `ADMIN_CONTENT_CHANGED` (delete confirm stays a no-input Modal); `ProDetailScreen`/`PublicProfileScreen` refresh reviews on `REVIEWS_CHANGED` (onPosted prop removed).
- **Deleted (dead after conversion):** `src/components/KeyboardSheet.tsx`, `src/components/reviews/MutualReviewForm.tsx`.
- **Keyboard close:** dragging never dismisses (`keyboardDismissMode="none"`); a header "Done" button dismisses.
- **Verified here (layout + navigation only):** write-review-btn now navigates to the full-screen `/review-compose` with a Back control and all fields in the unchanged order; footer Cancel + Send review present. NOT verified: real native keyboard behavior (no keyboard in web preview) — Jenn must confirm on iPhone that tapping the comment / "What was this for?" boxes scrolls them above the keyboard with the footer visible, and that swiping never closes the keyboard, in the review, review-back, Report and Admin edit screens.


## Changelog — Jun 2026 (R3d-2: keyboard still covered inputs in sheets — MOBILE ONLY)
No testing agent. Web/backend/billing + protected accounts + review field order untouched. No em dashes. R3d-1 (Profile tab) untouched.
- **Root cause (real iPhone):** every sheet (review / review-back / one-way org / Report / Admin) uses `KeyboardSheet`, which rendered inside an RN `<Modal>` (MotionModal) and used `KeyboardAvoidingView` (behavior padding iOS / height Android). On iOS, KAV mis-measures inside a Modal (separate native view hierarchy -> documented coordinate mismatch), so the comment / "What was this for?" boxes were not lifted. `keyboardDismissMode="interactive"` also dismissed the keyboard when the user swiped up to reach a lower input. Only the footer appeared above the keyboard because it sat at the sheet bottom. The working `messages/[id]` uses KAV at SCREEN level (not in a Modal), which is why it behaves correctly.
- **Fix (KeyboardSheet.tsx rewritten, no KAV):** read the real OS keyboard height from the `Keyboard` module — iOS `keyboardWillShow`/`keyboardWillHide`, Android `keyboardDidShow`/`keyboardDidHide` (verified against current RN docs) — via `event.endCoordinates.height` (screen coordinates, correct inside a Modal). iOS does not resize, so lift the whole sheet by that height (`backdrop paddingBottom = kb`) and cap `maxHeight = winH - kb - insets.top - 24` so the top stays on screen; the footer is lifted with it. Android resizes (adjustResize), so no manual lift (avoids double-pad). On show, scroll the focused input above the keyboard via `TextInput.State.currentlyFocusedInput()` + `measureLayout` against the scroll content + `scrollTo`. `keyboardDismissMode="none"` so swiping never closes the keyboard; `keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"`.
- **Benefits every sheet** using KeyboardSheet: review form, review-back, one-way org, success, Report, Admin edit.
- **Cannot verify in web preview (no native keyboard).** Only a layout smoke was done (sheet + inputs + footer render, no regression). Jenn must confirm on the iPhone: tapping the comment box and "What was this for?" scrolls each just above the keyboard with Cancel + Send review still visible; swiping up never closes the keyboard; same in Report and Admin edit sheets.
- Files changed: `mobile/src/components/KeyboardSheet.tsx` (rewritten). New/moved: none.


## Changelog — Jun 2026 (R3d-1: Profile tab stuck on Reviews — MOBILE ONLY)
No testing agent (per user). Verified via Expo web preview (390) with temp accounts (all deleted). Web/backend/billing + Wash N Go/PSSS/WTYC/seed pros untouched. No em dashes.
- **Root cause:** Reviews reached via cross-tab `router.push("/profile/reviews")` ("See my reviews", notification tap) built the Profile stack as `[reviews]` with no `index` beneath, so `router.back()` left the tab and a focused Profile-tab press had nothing to pop to (R3c's `popToTopOnBlur` only helps when LEAVING a tab). Verified current expo-router docs: `initialRouteName` is deprecated for `anchor`, and `anchor` only applies on cold deep-links; in-app pushes need `{ withAnchor: true }`.
- **Fix:** (1) `app/(tabs)/profile/_layout.tsx` exports `unstable_settings = { anchor: "index" }` (cold deep-links keep the menu beneath). (2) `app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx` profile `Tabs.Screen` gets a `tabPress` listener: `e.preventDefault(); router.navigate("/profile")` so tapping the Profile tab lands on the menu from ANY depth, even while already inside Reviews (kept `popToTopOnBlur`). (3) `{ withAnchor: true }` added to the two in-app pushes into the Profile stack: `ReviewButton` "See my reviews" (success screen) and `NotificationsScreen` tap, so the Reviews Back control returns to the menu.
- **Verified (console assertions, 390):** menu->Reviews: menu shown, Back present, tabPress->menu True; cold deep-link: Back present, Back->menu True; notification tap (pro): ->Reviews with Back, tabPress->menu True. "See my reviews" uses the identical withAnchor push as the verified notification path. Reviews screen has a visible Back (`header-back-btn`) via `<Screen showBack>`.
- Files changed: `mobile/app/(tabs)/profile/_layout.tsx`, `mobile/app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx`, `mobile/src/components/reviews/ReviewButton.tsx`, `mobile/src/screens/NotificationsScreen.tsx`. New/moved: none.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Batch H: messaging fixes — web + mobile)
No testing agent (per user). Verified via live API tests with temp accounts (deleted after) + Playwright screenshots (web 1440 + 390, light+dark; mobile Expo web preview 390). No billing/review/identity-rule changes. Real data (Wash N Go, PSSS, WTYC, seed Boat Pros, Jenn) untouched.
- **Entry "+":** web "New Chat" (crew icon) -> "New message" with `add` icon (testID `new-message-btn`); mobile DMs header button changed from people icon to `add` "+" (testID `new-message-btn`). Added shared web icon `more` (lucide MoreHorizontal).
- **Compose (one-to-one by default):** rewrote web `NewGroupModal` -> `NewMessageModal` (uses shared `Modal` + `PostingAs`) and mobile `messages/new.tsx`. Recipient picker accepts one or more; group name field shows ONLY when >1 selected (required when shown); send button says "Send". One recipient -> opens the existing 1:1 thread if present (deterministic thread_id upsert, no duplicates) else creates it; >1 -> group.
- **Business is always one-to-one:** when "Posting as" is the business, the picker allows exactly ONE recipient and the group-name field never shows; adding a 2nd shows the quiet line "Business messages are one-to-one. Switch to You to start a group." Server enforcement in `messaging_router`: `create_group` and `send_group_message` return 400 "Business messages are one-to-one..." when `identity == "business"` (groups are always authored personal). Mobile shows a local "Posting as" toggle (defaults to the account's active identity) since there is no global mobile switcher.
- **Delete conversation (new):** backend `DELETE /api/messages/thread/{id}` adds the caller to `thread.deleted_for` (removes from caller's inbox only; others keep their copy); `list_threads` filters `deleted_for`; send paths `$pull` recipients from `deleted_for` so a new message re-surfaces it. Web: "..." menu on each thread row + a thread-header "..." menu -> confirm "Delete conversation?" / "This removes it from your inbox. The other person keeps their copy." / Cancel+Delete. Mobile: row "..." + header "..." -> same confirm.
- **Per-message edit/delete made visible:** web own-message hover-trash replaced with an always-visible "..." menu (Edit + Delete) with adaptive up/down positioning (no clipping); mobile adds a visible "..." affordance on own bubbles (plus long-press) opening Edit/Delete. Delete opens confirm "Delete message?" (Delete for everyone) / Cancel+Delete. Removed the received-message delete-for-me menu (own messages only).
- **Block (web):** removed from the sending side — the thread-header Block now shows ONLY when the thread has an inbound message from the other user (a thread you received). Mobile (report only): same inbound gate applied to the header Block.
- **Keyboard (mobile messaging):** existing shared `KeyboardAvoidingView` pattern (behavior padding/height, `keyboardVerticalOffset=insets.top+52`, input bar `paddingBottom=insets.bottom+10`) preserved on both `messages/[id].tsx` (input + edit share the one bar) and `messages/new.tsx`. Not changed; could NOT validate native keyboard rise on the Expo web preview (no native keyboard).
- **Auth payload fix (web + mobile):** `POST /api/auth/login` and `/api/auth/register` now merge `account_identity_payload` (active_identity, can_use_business, business_name/logo/listing_id) into the returned user — previously only `/auth/me` did, so mobile's post-login user lacked `can_use_business` and the business one-to-one rule could not render until app reload.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Step 9 follow-up 4: Boat Pro listing guard + post-payment landing)
No testing agent (per user). Verified with a direct function-level test + screenshots. Temp accounts deleted; real/seed accounts untouched.
- **1. Guard — listing only for boat_pro accounts.** Reported: `_apply_paid_subscription` keyed the listing branch off the PACKAGE audience (`pkg["audience"] == "boat_pro"`), NOT the user's account_type. (The `/payments/checkout` endpoint already 403s a mismatched audience, so a Boat Owner normally can't complete a Boat Pro checkout — but the apply step itself was unguarded.) Verified the hole with a temp Boat Owner + a paid Boat Pro transaction: a `boat_pros` listing WAS created and `subscription_tier` was stamped `premium`. **Fix:** `_ensure_boat_pro_listing` now loads the user's `account_type` and returns early (logging a warning) for any non-boat_pro account — no listing is created. Per instruction, billing was NOT otherwise changed, so the user's `subscription_tier` is still stamped with the Boat Pro package tier upstream (only the directory listing is withheld). Re-tested post-fix: Boat Owner → listing = None, tier still `premium`.
- **2. Post-payment landing + "Set up your listing".** Reported: web `/payment/success` (paid) showed "You're all set!" with only a **Go to dashboard** button; mobile `payment/success` (paid) showed only a **Done** button → Profile. Neither pointed a new Boat Pro at the editor. **Added:** a **"Set up your listing"** button (opens the editor: web `/boat-pro-listing`, mobile `/profile/boat-pro-listing`) shown on the paid success screen only when `user.account_type === "boat_pro"`, keeping Dashboard/Done as the secondary action. Verified live on mobile (`result=paid` + Boat Pro session → button shows). Could NOT render the web paid branch (needs a real Stripe test checkout → paid status); the page compiles and renders its other states.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Step 9 follow-up 3: listing-created-after-payment + org logos on white circle in activity)
Web + mobile. No testing agent (per user); verified via curl + a direct creator-function test + screenshots. All temp test data deleted; Wash N Go / PSSS / WTYC / seed Boat Pros untouched.

### Item 1 — New Boat Pro listing created only AFTER payment (Jenn's rule)
- **1A report:** Tier is set after a confirmed payment in `_apply_paid_subscription(session_id)` in `backend/routers/payments_router.py`, called from TWO confirmed-paid paths — `GET /payments/status/{session_id}` (return/polling page, when Stripe `payment_status == "paid"`) and `POST /webhook/stripe` (when `evt.payment_status == "paid"`). The "succeeded" state = Stripe Checkout session `payment_status == "paid"`; `_apply_paid_subscription` is already idempotent via the `tx.subscription_applied` guard. Signup DOES collect `company_name` (RegisterBody) on web + mobile, stored on the `users` doc. Boat Pro accounts with no listing today: **0 of 4** (all seeded have docs). A Boat Pro with no listing previously saw: editor bounced out (web → toast + `/profile`; mobile → toast + back), and `GET/PATCH /boat-pros/*` 404'd; they never appeared in the public directory (the list queries `boat_pros`).
- **1B build (minimal):** Added `_ensure_boat_pro_listing(user_id, tier)` and call it from the ONE boat_pro branch of `_apply_paid_subscription` (replaced the old `update_one(tier)`). It upserts on `user_id`: `$set {tier, updated_at}` + `$setOnInsert {user_id, company_name (from signup), created_at}` — creates the listing with ONLY company_name + tier (everything else empty), idempotent (repeat webhook/return/refresh never duplicates or overwrites; a tier change only updates tier). No other billing change. Pre-payment client state: both editors (web `BoatProListing.jsx`; mobile `profile/boat-pro-listing.tsx`, which the directory route re-exports) now detect the 404 and show "Your listing is created after your plan is active" + a **Choose a plan** button → `/pricing`, with NO error toast and no bounce.
- **1C test (no live charge):** Called `_ensure_boat_pro_listing` directly on a temp Boat Pro: before = no listing; after 1st call = listing with only `company_name` ("Temp Test Marine Co") + `tier=premium`; 2nd call = still 1 doc (no duplicate/overwrite); tier change to featured = still 1 doc, company unchanged. After-payment editor opens with company name filled + all else empty (captured). Pre-payment state captured on web 1440/768/390 (light+dark) and mobile 390 (light+dark). **Could NOT test:** a real Stripe test checkout → webhook/return round-trip (tested the creator the payment path calls, directly, not a live charge).

### Item 2 — Org logos on the fixed white circle in activity
- **Mechanism (minimal, surface-agnostic):** An org account's logo always resolves to a `.../org/...` storage path (via `display_image`, partner accounts), while personal photos are `.../avatars/...`. The shared avatar components now draw any `/org/` image on a FIXED white circle with cover fit (matching LogoCircle), light + dark; personal photos & initials fallbacks are untouched. Changed: `frontend/src/components/Avatar.jsx` (used by Feed, Forum, Blog, Comments) and `mobile/src/components/ui.tsx` Avatar (Feed, Forum/Hub, Comments, Messages). Verified live: web + mobile feed show a transparent org logo on a white circle in dark mode, while an adjacent personal Boat Pro photo stays teal-tinted (before = teal tint `bg-[#55B3AD]/15`; after = white).
- **NOT changed (flagged):** Messages and Following lists do NOT draw org logos today — their clients read `picture || avatar` (null for orgs) and the follows API doesn't even return `logo_url`, so there is no org logo to wrap. Surfacing org logos there needs a backend field (follows) + client change, beyond "put the drawn logo on a white circle." Awaiting user decision.

### Files changed (all edits; no new/moved — temp test script deleted)

- frontend/src/pages/BoatProListing.jsx
- frontend/src/components/Avatar.jsx

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Web Boat Pro /profile = personal details only; business lives on the listing)
Web only (`/app/frontend`); no mobile files, no `DirectoryDetail.jsx` change, no backend change (rules already existed, re-verified by curl).
- **Profile.jsx (Boat Pro only):** REMOVED every business section from Account details — the old "Your Boat Pro listing" block (website, Contact email, Contact phone, Short bio, Categories, Markets), the "Edit your public listing card" link, and the whole "Trust & credentials" (certifications/licenses/insurance) editor. Removed dead pro state/helpers (`form`, `pro`, categories/markets fetch, cred drafts, `saveProListing`, focus-scroll effect, `useSearchParams`, tier calcs). KEPT personal fields (first/last name, @-handle, Email, personal photo) and all other sections untouched (newsletter, push, my listings, following, saved, testimonial, subscription, contact, ID verification).
- **Business name KEPT in Account details (Jenn's change):** same stored field as the listing (`company_name`); editing it in either place updates both. Saving it from /profile writes ONLY `company_name` via a partial `PATCH /api/boat-pros/listing {company_name}` (never resends other listing fields — the old POST /boat-pros upsert clobbered bio/website/phone, which is why the partial PATCH is used). Added a "View listing card" link → `/directory/<user_id>` under the field.
- **Two new links under Email (Boat Pro only):** "View public profile" → `/u/<handle>`; "Your Boat Pro Listing" → `/boat-pro-listing` (Orgs-style button pattern). Other account types unchanged (verified: sailor profile shows neither link nor business name).
- **Contact email given a home on /boat-pro-listing (option a):** added a "Public contact email (shown on your listing)" field bound to the existing `contact_email` (the field the public listing actually displays), placed next to Contact phone. Labeled the two emails distinctly: "Public contact email (shown on your listing)" (helper: "Shown on your public listing. Leave blank to show no email.") vs "Business email" (helper: "Private. Reserved for your business identity and not shown on your public listing."). No data migration; `DirectoryDetail.jsx` untouched.
- **HandleProfile.jsx redirect:** `/u/<business_handle>` (API `resolved_identity === "business"`) now `navigate(\`/directory/<user_id>\`, {replace:true})` BEFORE `setData`, so the personal profile never flashes. `/u/<account_handle>` still renders the personal profile as before. Old links do not 404.
- **Server (verified, unchanged):** non-Premium credential writes are refused on BOTH endpoints that can write certifications/licenses/insurance — `PATCH /api/boat-pros/listing` and `POST /api/boat-pros` — with 400 "Credentials are included with Premium. Upgrade to add credentials." No other endpoint writes those fields (`/auth/profile`, `/account/personal` schemas don't carry them). Empty personal email already rejected by `PATCH /auth/profile` → 400 "Enter a valid email". Blank `contact_email` stored as None and shows no email publicly (no fallback to account email / business_email_effective; `business_email_effective` is present in the API payload but rendered nowhere public on web or mobile).
- **Seed pros (handle / business_handle / personal_handle_pending):** sailor(seed_marina, premium) salishmarinaservices / pending=true; sailor2(seed_rigging, featured) northsoundriggingco / pending=true; sailor3(seed_canvas, basic) rivercitycanvas / pending=true. Handle collisions: NONE. The old "Featured accepts credentials" was pre-rule data (`washngo`); the rule now blocks any add/change for non-Premium (re-verified: Featured add → 400).
- Verified live (web preview, screenshots 1440/768/390 light+dark; curl both endpoints all three tiers): Business + credentials gone, personal intact, both links present/working; redirect + account-handle URLs; non-Pro unchanged. Temp owner sessions deleted; seed data restored. No testing agent (per user).

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Mobile tab bar — Pass 2 follow-ups: messages keyboard, feed scroll, feed profile in-tab)
Three phone-reported items fixed. No new/moved routes this turn; edits only.
- Messages keyboard (messages/[id] + messages/new): keyboard partly covered the input/Send. Applied the same handling as the post comment bar — `KeyboardAvoidingView` behavior iOS `padding`/Android `height`, `keyboardVerticalOffset={insets.top + 52}`, bottom bar `paddingBottom = insets.bottom + 10/16`. messages/new's member search + first-message form is now wrapped in a KeyboardAvoidingView. Bar stays hidden (root screens). On-device keyboard rise still to be confirmed (web preview has no native keyboard).
- Feed scroll reset — CAUSE: `useFocusEffect(() => { setItems(null); load(); })` ran on EVERY focus, so returning from a detail cleared the list (-> spinner, remount) and refetched, resetting to top. FIX: load on mount + deps only via `useEffect([load])` (no focus reset). Same applied to Events (`setEvents(null)` on focus) and Pros/Orgs directory (refetched on focus). Verified: Back from a post triggers 0 posts/discussions refetches; list stays mounted so React Navigation preserves native scroll offset.
- Feed public profile hid the bar — CAUSE: the global ProfilePreview sheet's "View profile" pushed root `/u/[handle]`. FIX: `show(handle, base?)` now takes a tab base; `viewProfile` pushes `${base}/u/[handle]`. Feed card passes `/feed`; Post/Hub detail pass their `useStackBase()`. Also My Listing "View public page" -> `/profile/u/[handle]`; Following tab event row -> `/events/events/[id]`. (The Following LIST screen already routed in-tab via `/profile/u/...`.)
- Files changed this turn: app/(tabs)/directory/index.tsx, app/(tabs)/events/index.tsx, app/(tabs)/feed/index.tsx, app/(tabs)/following.tsx, app/(tabs)/my-listing.tsx, app/messages/[id].tsx, app/messages/new.tsx, src/context/ProfilePreviewContext.tsx, src/screens/HubDetailScreen.tsx, src/screens/PostDetailScreen.tsx. Bundle clean (web+iOS). No testing agent (per user). No backend/billing changes.

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Mobile tab bar — Pass 2: all detail screens keep the bar + comment-bar fix)
Continued the nested-stack model so detail screens keep the bottom tab bar in every tab they are reached from. The cluster {public profile, post, hub, event, boat-pro} is mutually referential, so each content tab carries the whole set. Shared screens were extracted once into `src/screens/*` and navigate TAB-RELATIVE via a new helper `src/lib/stackBase.ts` (`useStackBase()` returns `/feed|/events|/directory|/profile|""`; `useGo()` pushes within the current tab). Thin re-export route files mount each shared screen per tab.
- Also fixed the Pass-1 "Back to directory" bug: it used `router.push` (and `navigate` still stacked a 2nd Clubs). Now `router.back()` (org is always reached from Clubs inside the directory tab), gated to the directory tab. Verified: org -> Back to directory -> single Clubs -> header Back -> Pros list.
- Comment bar fix (post/[id] + hub/[id]): input+Post now sit above the bottom safe area, the tab bar (when shown), and the keyboard. `KeyboardAvoidingView` behavior iOS `padding` / Android `height`, `keyboardVerticalOffset={insets.top + 52}` (header height), inputBar `paddingBottom` = tab ? 10 : insets.bottom + 10. (Keyboard overlap needs final on-device check; web preview has no native keyboard.)
- NEW shared screens (src/screens/): EventDetailScreen, PostDetailScreen, HubDetailScreen, HubIndexScreen, FollowingListScreen, CrewBankScreen, NotificationsScreen, SavedScreen, FaqScreen, AdminScreen, ProDetailScreen (+ PublicProfileScreen from Pass-1 fix). NEW helper: src/lib/stackBase.ts.
- Root routes now re-export the shared screen (kept for deep links; bar hidden there, acceptable for external entry): app/events/[id], app/post/[id], app/hub/[id], app/hub/index, app/following-list, app/crew-bank, app/notifications, app/saved, app/faq, app/admin, app/u/[handle].
- NEW tab stack layouts (anchor index): app/(tabs)/feed/_layout, app/(tabs)/events/_layout (directory from Pass-1). Moved tab indexes: feed.tsx->feed/index.tsx, events.tsx->events/index.tsx. directory/[id].tsx -> src/screens/ProDetailScreen.tsx.
- NEW per-tab cluster routes: feed/{events/[id],post/[id],hub/[id],u/[handle],pro/[id],crew-bank,notifications}; events/{events/[id],post/[id],hub/[id],u/[handle],pro/[id]}; directory/{pro/[id],events/[id],post/[id],hub/[id]}; profile/{following-list,saved,faq,admin,u/[handle],events/[id],post/[id],hub/[id],pro/[id]}.
- Resource sub-paths are consistent per tab: `u/[handle]`, `post/[id]`, `hub/[id]`, `events/[id]`, `pro/[id]`. Boat-pro detail renamed `/directory/[id]` -> `/directory/pro/[id]` (shareLink still points at canonical web `/directory/<id>`). Events tab uses `/events/events/[id]` (avoids the tab-name collision).
- Origin pushes updated to tab-relative: feed/index, events/index, directory/index (pro), directory/clubs + directory/org/[id] (events), profile/index (following-list,saved,faq,admin,view-public), profile/boat-pro-listing + profile/org-listing (view-public).
- HIDE-bar screens stay at app root (render over tabs, bar hidden): messages/[id], messages/new, new-post, new-event, hub/new, pricing, signin, register, onboarding, index, payment/success, verify/return.
- Bundle clean (web 1498 / iOS 2127 modules), no route/duplicate/unmatched errors. No testing agent (per user). No backend/billing changes.
- Known remaining (NOT in Pass-2 list): the `following` tab screen and `my-listing` tab push event detail / `/u/` at root (bar hidden). Can fold into a later pass if desired.

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Mobile tab bar kept on detail screens — Pass 1)
Cause: every non-tab screen lived at the ROOT of `app/`, so it rendered in the root Stack ON TOP of the `(tabs)` navigator and the bottom tab bar vanished. Fix: nested Stack per tab so detail screens live inside the tab and keep the bar (no fake/second bar).

- `profile` tab is now a Stack: `app/(tabs)/profile/` = `_layout.tsx` + `index.tsx` (was `(tabs)/profile.tsx`) + `org-listing.tsx` + `boat-pro-listing.tsx` + `pro-edit.tsx` (all moved from root).

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Logo crop: fit small/square logos in the circle + white logo background)
- Root cause: `CropModal` (web react-easy-crop) had `minZoom` defaulting to 1, slider `min={1}`, `restrictPosition` on — react-easy-crop scales media to COVER the round crop area at zoom 1, so a small/square logo was upscaled (~blurry) and couldn't be zoomed out; only the centre fit.
- WEB fix (round/logo only): on media load, measure the viewport, set an explicit round `cropSize` and compute the zoom that fits the WHOLE image inside the circle; set that as both the initial zoom and the slider/`minZoom` floor; `restrictPosition={false}` so a logo smaller than the circle can be positioned; slider `min` is now dynamic. Save clamps logo output to `min(512, croppedWidth)` so a 200px logo saves at ~200px (never upscaled); canvas pads out-of-circle area (transparent for PNG, white for JPEG) so non-square logos show fully. Cover/gallery (rect) unchanged: cover-fill, `minZoom` 1, `restrictPosition` on, 1600px output.
- MOBILE fix (same spirit, custom gesture cropper): opens with the whole image fitted (initial scale = min/max dimension ratio), pinch can zoom out to contain (min clamp = that ratio instead of 1), save clamps crop to image bounds (no manipulator crash) and output to `min(512, cropWidth)` (no upscale). LIMITATION: the mobile image library can't pad to a square, so a fully-contained NON-square logo is saved non-square and the circle (object-cover) crops it; SQUARE logos (the common case) fit perfectly.
- White logo background (display only; stored PNG stays transparent): `LogoCircle` now has a fixed `bg-white` behind the image (theme-independent), the editor logo preview gets `bg-white` when an image is present, and the round crop viewport is white. Before: `LogoCircle` had NO background, so a transparent PNG logo showed whatever was behind the circle (navy Clubs card / hero / page / dark surface) and thus looked different between light and dark and between screens. Checked: profile, Clubs card, public page, listing editor, crop modal. (Org cards use `LogoCircle`; boat-pro directory cards render their logo separately and were not in scope.)
- Transparent PNG path from the prior task kept intact. Verified: web + mobile build clean (tsc + webpack). NOT verified here (needs the browser/device): the actual crop UX (open-fit, zoom-out, tight zoom-in), transparent logo showing white in both themes across all surfaces, and the mobile non-square limitation.
---

WEB: `imageCompress.resizeImageToDataURL` gained a format arg ("auto" scans canvas alpha → PNG, else JPEG composited over white); `CropModal` gained a `format` prop (PNG branch skips the white fill); `ProfileCardEditor.onFile` passes "auto" for the logo, `openCropCurrent` infers PNG from a stored `.png` URL. No listing data changed; test org untouched (upload-endpoint-only check).

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Bugfix: mobile crop Save silently did nothing)
Root cause (two parts): (1) mobile `CropModal.save()` had a `try/finally` with NO `catch`, so any `ImageManipulator.manipulateAsync` rejection was swallowed — `onSave` never fired, no message, crop screen stayed open. (2) `ProfileCardEditor.cropCurrent()` passed a REMOTE `/api/files` URL straight to `manipulateAsync`, which can't process a remote URL on native — so re-cropping ANY already-saved photo (gallery/cover/logo) always hit (1). Fresh picks worked because their uri is a local `file://`.
Fix: `cropCurrent` now downloads the remote original to a local file first (native: `expo-file-system/legacy` `downloadAsync` to cache; web: `fetch`→`URL.createObjectURL` blob) before cropping, keeping `original` as the stored URL so it's reused (not re-uploaded) on save. `CropModal.save()` gained a `catch` + new `onError` prop → shows a friendly toast and leaves the screen usable; Save can NEVER do nothing now. Shows a brief busy overlay on the slot while the stored photo downloads. Originals still stored at 2000px/0.8.
Verified (non-device): tsc clean for both files; stored `/api/files` image downloads 200 image/jpeg (native downloadAsync will succeed); `/api/files` sends `ACAO: *` (web blob path works); upload + re-crop data path confirmed. NOT verifiable here (needs a real phone): on-device ImageManipulator crop of the downloaded local file, HEIC decode, and the authenticated Expo editor UI (preview gates backend; AsyncStorage-web token injection doesn't persist). Files: `mobile/src/components/CropModal.tsx`, `mobile/src/components/ProfileCardEditor.tsx`.

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (High-quality stored originals + full-original full-size viewer)
The page keeps showing each photo's CROP (cover 3.5:1, gallery tiles, logo circle); the full-size viewer now opens the full uncropped ORIGINAL scaled to fit. Viewer sequence/controls unchanged (cover, gallery 1-5, logo, "n of m" counter, close).
- **Stored originals upgraded** (uploads go to object storage, not base64, so the old ~400KB base64 cap no longer applies): web `lib/imageCompress.resizeImageToDataURL()` (NEW) + `ProfileCardEditor.onFile` now store the original at up to **2000px long side / 0.8** for cover+gallery and **1000px / 0.8** for the logo (shown small). Mobile `ProfileCardEditor.pickFor` ImageManipulator raised from 1000px/0.5 to **2000px/0.8** (logo 1000px/0.8). Display crop output bumped 1024->**1600** (web `CropModal`, mobile `CropModal`), round logo stays 512. Web input cap raised 10MB->15MB (matches backend) with friendly over-size message.
- **Typical sizes (measured on a detailed 4000px photo):** stored original 2000px/0.8 ≈ **410 KB**; logo 1000px/0.8 ≈ 123 KB; cover display-crop 1600px/0.85 ≈ 327 KB. An org with cover + 5 gallery originals ≈ ~2.4 MB of originals.
- **Cover original** kept the same way as gallery/logo (`cover_original_url`, object-storage URL, fallback to crop when absent).
- **Viewer loads originals**: web `HandleProfile` orgMedia + `DirectoryDetail` proMedia and mobile `u/[handle]` orgMedia now use `cover_original_url` / `photos[].original` / `logo_original_url` with crop fallback. Backend `GET /api/u/{handle}` now INCLUDES the originals (added `cover_original_url`/`logo_original_url` to the projection; photos now carry `original` — previously stripped; they're short URLs now so it's cheap).
- **Re-crop**: reads `original || url` — new uploads re-crop from the 2000px original; OLD photos re-crop only from their stored ~1000px/0.5 copy (no quality recovered), falling back to the crop if no original. No change to re-crop source logic.
- **Existing data (no recovery, nothing deleted)** — accounts still holding OLD-size (~1000px/0.5) photos: ORGS pugetsoundsailingsoc (2 gallery + cover), whitetrashyachtclub (5 gallery + cover + logo); BOAT PROS Wash N Go (cover+logo), Cascade Haul-Out & Diesel (3), North Sound Rig & Sail (3), Harbor Shine Detailing & School (3). They keep opening at the stored size. (Jenn to re-upload PSSS + WTYC demo/real photos.)
- **Verified LIVE**: uploaded a 4000px photo to test org iter69org as cover + gallery via the real `/org-listing/upload` path -> stored original 419,376 bytes; `/api/u/iter69org` returns distinct `cover_url` (crop) vs `cover_original_url` (original) and `photos[0]` with both; web page shows the 3.5:1 crop + tile, and the full-size viewer opens the ORIGINAL (lightbox img src == cover_original_url, counter "1 of 2"). iter69org restored to empty afterward. Web compiles; mobile tsc clean for touched files.
- Scope lock honored: no status/badge/billing changes; Boat Pro editors untouched (will reuse this same upload path in Steps 5/9); no data changed except the test org (restored).

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (ONE org status: free | community_partner | founding_partner)
Single source of truth for an org/club account's standing, stored in `users.subscription_tier` (one of free | community_partner | founding_partner). FP gets all CP benefits (logo, cover, 5-photo gallery) and shows ONLY the Founding Partner badge. Admin sets status by hand (payments are off-app).
- **Backend single source**: `deps.org_status()` (new) + `deps.partner_badge()` now read ONLY `subscription_tier`; the legacy `founding_partner` boolean + `founding_cohort` are kept in the DB but no longer read for badges/gating/counts, so a stale flag can never add a 2nd badge. `can_interact()` = `tier != "free"` (flag fallback removed). `deps.PAID_TIERS` + `admin_router.PAID_TIERS` (analytics "upgraded") now include `founding_partner`. Gallery gating (`directory_router` `bool(partner_badge(u))`) unlocks logo+cover+5 photos for BOTH CP and FP; free hides gallery (kept-not-deleted), cover+logo stay.
- **Admin**: removed the auto-Founding assignment + global/seattle cohort logic from `POST /api/admin/community-partner/activate` (now CP/Free only, partner-only). New admin-only `POST /api/admin/org-status {email|user_id, status}` sets exactly the chosen status; rejects non-partner accounts (400), non-admin (403), no-auth (401). `GET /api/admin/partners/search` returns per-org `status` + per-market `founding_counts` (by `primary_market`) + `founding_cap:5`. `GET /api/founding-partners` now queries `subscription_tier=="founding_partner"`. `/org-listing/mine` returns `status` + `partner_badge`.
- **Stripe-safe**: orgs have no Stripe package (audiences are sailor/boat_owner/boat_pro only) so webhooks/`_apply_paid_subscription` can never set an org tier; admin status writes set `subscription_expires=None` (lifetime) so the nightly expiry sweep + lazy-downgrade never touch orgs.
- **Web**: AdminPartners rewritten with a 3-way control (Free/Community Partner/Founding Partner) + FP count header + "Switch to Free?" confirm + FP cap(5)-per-market WARNING (not a block). Clubs cards + public page + Profile chip + OrgListing editor badge + FollowingList + PartnerBadge now show the single status (CP=teal flag, FP=orange flag, Free=none). `plan.js` flag fallback removed.
- **Mobile**: clubs cards (CP teal / FP orange flag), public org chip (single), Profile tier chip (single — hide account-type chip for partners), PartnerBadge (FP orange), following-list flag color, `plan.ts` flag fallback removed.
- **Colors**: CP = teal `#55B3AD` / `colors.teal`; FP = orange `#F2A03B` / `colors.accent`. (Before this change CP badge was teal, FP was amber, free "Club/Org" pill teal, Clubs card flag always orange with no FP distinction.) Verified distinguishable on web light+dark.
- **Migration (approved)**: users backed up to `/app/backend/migration_backups/users_before_org_status_20261012_054317.json` (166 docs). White Trash Yacht Club + Puget Sound Sailing Society -> `founding_partner` (expires=None). iter69org + demo-salish-bay-sailing-club stayed `community_partner`; 16 others stayed free. Final: FP=2, CP=2, free=16.
- **Verified**: curl (badge-from-tier, stale-flag=no-badge, can_interact, hide-but-save on iter69org then restored, 401/403/400 gates, honor roll); testing_agent iter98 web+mobile PASS; one mobile double-chip bug found + fixed (`profile.tsx` hides account-type chip for partners).
- **Gallery originals (report, no change)**: gallery photos stored as `{url, caption, original}` where `original` is a RESIZED copy (~1000px/0.5), NOT the full-res camera file; cover=`cover_url`+`cover_original_url` (also resized copy); logo likewise. Crop on a saved photo re-crops from the stored `original` (else `url`), uploads a new cropped url, keeps the original — so you can zoom back out only to the resized copy. Public reads strip `original` (url+caption only).

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Messaging plan-gate centralization + locked-notification read-time fix + unlock banner)
### Diagnosis (community_partner was NOT actually blocked)
Every live gate already treated `community_partner` as paid; the "Upgrade to read and reply" White Trash saw was 2 STALE notification records created while the account was free (their text/link/locked flag were frozen at creation, `messaging_router._notify_recipient`). Confirmed live: threads load, reads 200, reply gate passes, and the app showed "now unlocked".

### A — Single plan helper (reusable by the upcoming org-status task)
- **Server:** `deps.can_interact(user)` in `/app/backend/deps.py` (+ `PAID_TIERS` constant). Any paid tier qualifies; `community_partner` and `founding_partner` explicitly included; free = read-only. `is_paid_subscriber` now delegates to it, so `require_interaction` and every messaging/review/comment/reaction/post/event/Crew gate resolve through the one function. Crew Bank's "organizations not allowed" rule stays (it is by `account_type`, intentional).
- **Client:** `canInteract(user)` in `/app/frontend/src/lib/plan.js` and `/app/mobile/src/lib/plan.ts`. Wired into web `Messages.jsx`, `MessageLink.jsx`, `Feed.jsx`, `UpgradeNudge.jsx`; mobile `messages.tsx`, `crew-bank.tsx`, `partner.tsx`.

### B-1 — Locked-DM notifications resolved at READ time
`notifications.resolve_locked_dm()` recomputes a locked DM/group notification from the recipient's CURRENT tier when served (`GET /api/notifications`). Paid/upgraded recipients never see "Upgrade to read and reply" or `/pricing`; they get "New message in <group or sender>" linking to `/messages`. Free recipients unchanged. `_notify_recipient` now stores `group_title`, `from_name`, `thread_id` in meta for faithful resolution.

### C-1 — Stale records backed up + rewritten
Backed up originals to the **`notifications_locked_dm_backup`** collection, then rewrote the **4** stale locked records belonging to now-paid accounts — **whitetrashyachtclub ×2, jeffmcdonald ×2** — to normal text + `/messages` link + `locked:false` (+ `relocked_at`), recovering real titles from the backups ("New message in Crew"). The other 5 locked records (still-free accounts) were left as upgrade prompts.

### Unlock banner (item 4)
- Web `Messages.jsx`: moved the "You have N messages waiting" banner OUT of the fixed-height `h-[70vh]` grid (it was a grid track → ~350px). Now a compact one-line block: measured **42px** at laptop/tablet/phone, close button kept, text wraps on narrow widths. Mobile banner was already compact.
- `GET /messages/unlock-banner` now shows only while there are UNREAD arrived-while-free notifications (`meta.locked` OR `meta.relocked_at`), via `can_interact`; `mark_thread_read` clears the matching notification when the thread is opened, so it goes away after opening or dismissing.

### Tests (live, web + mobile)
- Free (Captain Jane): upgrade wall, no reply box. Paid (White Trash): threads open, can reply (web + mobile parity). White Trash notifications: normal text + `/messages`, no "Upgrade to read and reply" (API-verified). Compact banner 42px at 3 widths (disposable paid account).
- Restored: disposable **testmoborg** returned to free + empty (deleted the 2 banner-demo notifications, tier back to `free`). No other account changed beyond the intended C-1 notification rewrites. No billing/badge/org-status changes.
---

- **Cause:** In the prior "one image for org accounts" change, the `logoCrop` state in `frontend/src/pages/Profile.jsx` was inserted after a `const [market...]` line that actually belonged to the `TestimonialForm` sub-component (line ~68), NOT the exported `Profile()` component (line ~153). So `Profile()` referenced `logoCrop`/`setLogoCrop` that lived in a different function scope → `ReferenceError` at runtime. It still compiled (the symbol existed in the file), so there was no build error.
- **Fix:** Declared `const [logoCrop, setLogoCrop] = useState(null)` inside `Profile()` (next to `iconOpen`) and removed the misplaced declaration. CropModal is wired exactly like the listing editor (round square crop → object-storage upload → `PATCH /org-listing`).
- **Mobile checked:** `app/(tabs)/profile.tsx` declares `logoCrop` inside `Profile()` (correctly scoped), all imports present — no equivalent bug; bundles clean.
- **Why not caught earlier:** The prior task verified the mobile Profile logged-in and the web public org page + Clubs card (no auth), but never opened the WEB Profile page in a logged-in session — the only page where the misplaced declaration throws. Public/Clubs pages don't use `logoCrop`, so they masked it.
- **Live logged-in web tests (console captured):** Profile opened for org (White Trash, read-only), Boat Pro (Salish Marina), Boat Owner (Captain Jane) — all load, no crash, no `logoCrop`/ReferenceError (only a benign pre-auth `401 /api/auth/me` and an unrelated `<span> in <option>` hydration warning). Disposable test org (testmoborg): profile camera pick→crop→save set BOTH `logo_url` and `picture` to the same `/api/files` URL (directory logo = profile image); testmoborg then restored to its original empty state (all null).
- **Other screens touched in that task but NOT loaded in a logged-in web session:** the web listing editor page (`/org-listing`, `ProfileCardEditor.jsx`), and the feed/comments/messages/followers screens whose backend `author_avatar`/`user_avatar` now use `display_image`. These compiled and the backend change is covered by API tests, but they were not visually opened logged-in this session.
---

### Why three images showed
White Trash had `picture=null`, `avatar="🪢"` (knot EMOJI glyph), `logo_url=`TEAMWORK badge. Web profile & mobile profile both read `picture || avatar`; web renders the glyph as text (knot emoji), mobile passes it to `<Avatar uri>` which can't load an emoji so the circle was blank; the directory reads `logo_url` (badge) — three different results from three fields.

### Single source of truth
- New `deps.display_image(user)`: org/club accounts show `logo_url` everywhere, falling back to `picture` when only one exists (no data change needed); other accounts use `picture` then emoji `avatar`. Wired into the author/user avatar serializers (`community_router.py` author_avatar/user_avatar ×4, `directory_router.py` ×2).
- Org save (`PATCH /org-listing`) now mirrors `picture = logo_url` and clears the emoji `avatar`, so the profile header, feed, messages and followers (all read `picture`) show the logo. This is the hook that makes the logo and the profile picture the SAME image.

### Two-way sync + profile camera (web + mobile)
- The Profile camera for org accounts now opens the same square logo crop (circle display) the listing editor uses and saves via `PATCH /org-listing {logo_url, logo_original_url}` through the object-storage upload endpoint (`/org-listing/upload`, URLs not base64). Editing the logo in the listing editor changes the profile image and vice versa (both hit the same endpoint → `picture` mirrors `logo_url`). Verified live on a disposable test org (testmoborg): logo set from the "listing" path and from the "profile" path both synced `picture===logo_url`; testmoborg then restored to its original empty state.
- Profile header renders the org image with the shared `LogoCircle` (circle, no ring, no background) on both surfaces.

### Mobile profile status chips (item 4)
- Replaced the raw uppercased tier chip ("COMMUNITY_PAR…") with the same label map as web (`{community_partner:"Community Partner", premium:"Premium", ...}`) and suppressed the duplicate account-type chip when it equals the tier label. Now one clean "Community Partner" chip.

### Data migration
- Only **whitetrashyachtclub** was migrated (authorized): `picture = logo_url` (TEAMWORK badge is the single image), emoji `avatar` preserved in `avatar_url_previous`, `avatar=None`. The other 19 orgs have no conflicting image — the bulk migration was a no-op and was skipped per user approval.
- Scope: Boat Pro / sailor / boat-owner avatars untouched; no billing changes.
- Verified live: mobile WT profile now shows the badge (blank circle fixed) with one "Community Partner" chip; web public org page and web Clubs card show the same badge.
---

### Root cause of "saving fails" (evidence-based)
- The mobile editor embedded every image as base64 inside the listing PATCH. A newly cropped gallery `url` is produced by `CropModal` at **1024px / quality 0.8** → for a detailed photo that is **~627,000 chars**, over the **400,000** (`MAX_PHOTO_URL_CHARS`) per-field cap. Backend returned **HTTP 400 "Each photo must be ≤400 KB"**. Reproduced headless: adding a realistic 3rd photo to White Trash → 400. NOT a proxy/ingress limit (a 2.9 MB body returned 200). Flat/simple images crop small enough to pass, which is why only occasional 400s showed in logs; detailed real photos fail every time.
- White Trash field sizes at diagnosis: logo_url 94,975 / logo_original_url 274,311; cover_url 48,391 / cover_original_url 295,427; photo1 url 181,591 + original 351,067; photo2 url 223,635 + original 358,019. Logo/cover differ from gallery: logo url=512px, cover url=1024px (short 3.5:1), gallery url=1024px (tallest at 3:2 → biggest); originals all 1000px/0.5.

### Fix (correct long-term fix = object storage, like the avatar flow)
- **New backend endpoint `POST /api/org-listing/upload`** (`routers/directory_router.py`): auth + partner-only, JPG/PNG/WebP, 15 MB cap, uploads to Emergent object storage via `put_object`, returns a short `/api/files/...` URL. Served by the existing `GET /api/files/{path}`.
- **Both editors now upload, then save only URLs.** Mobile `ProfileCardEditor.tsx` uploads via `expo-file-system` `File` + `FormData` (native) / Blob (web), exactly like the avatar upload; web `ProfileCardEditor.jsx` uploads the cropped data URL as a Blob through the axios `api` client. `CropModal` (mobile) now returns a file uri instead of base64. Each crop uploads the cropped image AND the resized original; the listing PATCH carries only `/api/files` URLs. Already-stored `/api/files`/http originals are reused (no re-upload). Kept the 400 KB server cap, the 5-photo limit, and "nothing is deleted". Verified live: a 459 KB detailed crop that used to 400 now uploads and the save returns 200 with 3 photos.
- Mobile render sites that show stored images (`u/[handle].tsx` cover + gallery thumbs, editor cover/logo/gallery) resolve `/api/` → `EXPO_PUBLIC_BACKEND_URL` (LogoCircle/Avatar/ImageLightbox already did). Web `<img>` uses the relative `/api/files` path (proxied).
- **Failed save is never silent (item 5):** optimistic local preview keeps the cropped image on screen during upload and on failure, with a clear "Could not save. Tap Try again." message + retry.
- **Re-crop behaviour (item 6):** the original is still stored (now as an `/api/files` URL of the 1000px/0.5 copy), so re-crop can zoom back out — but only to that resized copy, since the true full-res camera file was never stored (only a resized copy ever was). Not limited to the already-cropped copy.

### Full-size viewer sequence (item 7, web + mobile)
- Public org page now opens ONE sequence in order **cover → gallery 1..5 → logo**, empties skipped; tapping any item opens the viewer at that position; swipe (mobile) / arrows + Escape (web) move through the rest; a small "n of m" counter added to both `ImageLightbox` components. Close button, Escape, focus handling and alt text preserved. Boat Pro public listing untouched. Verified: web 1→2→(gallery)3 of 4; mobile counter + swipe.

### Test-data integrity note
- Diagnosis used headless reproductions against White Trash and PSSS with snapshot/restore. White Trash fully restored from a full-doc snapshot. PSSS cover/logo were left with noisy test images by one reproduction and restored to a navy 3.5:1 cover + no logo + Alpha/Beta gallery (original state).
- Not tested live: the on-device pick→crop→upload gesture (native image picker can't be automated in the preview); logic + backend contract verified. Billing untouched.
---

- **Web ImageLightbox root-cause fix (`frontend/src/pages/HandleProfile.jsx`).** The lightbox failed to open ONLY for org/club profiles. Root cause: `HandleProfile` has TWO return branches — an early `if (isOrg) return (...)` branch (renders the org page) and a separate member-profile return. The cover/logo/gallery buttons (which call `setLightbox`) live in the org branch, but `<ImageLightbox/>` was mounted only in the member branch, so for orgs the click fired `setLightbox` but the component was never rendered. Confirmed via a one-shot diagnostic: `[LB] cover click fired` logged, `[LB] render` never did. Fix: also mount `<ImageLightbox/>` inside the org branch (as a child of the outer `handle-profile-page` div, before its close). Diagnostics removed. Verified live: cover opens full-size; gallery opens with working prev/next.
- **Mobile ImageLightbox verified** (`mobile/app/u/[handle].tsx`) — opens for cover + gallery over a dark backdrop with a 44px close button. The earlier "weak backdrop" impression was only the navy cover blending into the dark overlay; a gallery image (orange) confirms the 0.92 black backdrop renders.
- **Mobile CP gallery upload fix verified** (`mobile/src/components/ProfileCardEditor.tsx`, prior agent's uncommitted work): per-slot error keys (`g${i}`) + empty-slot `pickFor(photos.length)` stop the traveling-error bug; gallery crop/tile/thumb share a 3:2 aspect; source resize dropped to 1000px width / 0.5 compression so both `url` and `original` stay under the 400 KB cap. Backend boundary confirmed via curl: a >400 KB photo → HTTP 400 "Each photo must be ≤400 KB"; a valid save stores `listing.photos` correctly. Editor renders the 5-tile grid (2 filled + Add-photo) for a CP org. NOTE: the native pick→crop→upload interaction itself can't be automated in the web preview; logic + backend contract verified, not an on-device upload.

## Changelog — Oct 2026 (Org/Club editor photo fixes + shared crop accuracy)
Scope: Org/Club listing editor (web `OrgListing.jsx`, mobile `org-listing.tsx`), shared `ProfileCardEditor` + `CropModal`, and server org-listing gating. Boat Pro editor intentionally untouched (verified still opens/saves; cover stays wide, logo crop keeps prior contain behavior).

- **Logo crop white-edge bug (root cause + fix).** Baked into the SAVED image, not display. `react-easy-crop@6.2.4` defaults `objectFit:"contain"` so a non-square source sits inside the square frame at min zoom and the crop square overhangs the image; `toCroppedDataURL`'s white `fillRect` then bakes into the JPEG. Fix: `CropModal` gained an opt-in **`objectFit` prop** (default `"contain"`); the Org/Club editor passes **`cropObjectFit="cover"`** so the image always covers the frame → saved 512×512 fills completely, min zoom can never show empty space. Verified: wide/tall/tiny crops all saved with 0/4 white corners. Mobile CropModal already covered (baseScale=max + clamp) — unchanged. Boat Pro keeps contain.
- **Cover 16:9.** `ProfileCardEditor` gained a **`coverAspect` prop** (default = old wide banner); Org/Club editor passes **16/9** for both the card preview and the crop frame. Verified live: org cover box ratio = 1.778. Boat Pro cover unchanged (ratio ~4.56). Public org page cover left as object-cover (no distortion) and flagged — not changed (separate screen).

Free orgs see a locked row: "More photos are part of Community Partner." + "Contact us" (mailto info@sailmorenow.com, subject "Community Partner inquiry"). Verified: CP max=5 (7→5 clamp), free max=0, free photos ignored-not-wiped. Report: 0 CP orgs >5 photos, 0 free orgs with gallery photos — nothing hidden/deleted.

 — BACKEND + DATA MODEL only, no UI)
One person signs up and pays once; the account holds a PERSONAL identity (users doc) and a BUSINESS identity (boat_pros listing). Code shipped and curl-verified; the data MIGRATION is built as a DRY RUN only and is PENDING operator approval of the counts before first write.
- **Single source of truth for tier**: `directory_router._derive_pro_tier` now ALWAYS reads the person's `users.subscription_tier` (free→basic caps). `boat_pros.tier` is now only a cached mirror, intentionally ignored for gating. `/boat-pros` list also derives tier from the person.
- **Tier matrix fixes (per Plans and Pricing)**: `_pro_tier_caps` Premium gallery 6→**5** (logo+cover+5). New `_apply_tier_visibility()` replaces `_strip_gated_fields`: credentials (certifications/licenses/insurance) are **Premium-only** (Featured no longer shows them); logo/cover Featured+; gallery Premium (cap 5); markets trimmed to tier cap publicly. All over-tier, non-photo data is KEPT in storage, hidden on PUBLIC reads, retained on OWNER reads, reported via `locked_fields` + `hidden_photo_count`, and reappears on upgrade. `PATCH /boat-pros/listing` no longer NULLs disallowed image fields on a low tier (skips the write) so downgrades never destroy data.
- **Business identity**: `boat_pros.business_handle` + `business_email` (optional; `business_email_effective` falls back to the account login email). Added to the schema, the listing editor (`PATCH /boat-pros/listing`), `/boat-pros/mine`, `/boat-pros/{id}`, and `/u/{handle}`.
- **New read/write endpoints**: `GET /api/account/identities` (personal + business + last_identity), `PUT|PATCH /api/account/personal` (first/last name, personal handle, photo, last_identity).
- **Shared handle namespace**: `deps.handle_taken()` + `deps.resolve_handle_user()` span `users.handle` AND `boat_pros.business_handle`. All handle setters (auth `/auth/handle`, onboarding, org-listing, personal, business) and `_generate_unique_handle` enforce it. @mentions (create_comment) and `/u/<handle>` resolve to either identity; `/u/<business_handle>` returns the business listing (`resolved_identity:"business"`), keeping old links working.
- **last_identity**: stored on the person ("personal"|"business", default personal); set on new register/session; backfilled by migration.
- **author_identity (schema only)**: new posts/comments/discussions/reviews/mutual_reviews/dm_messages/dm_threads now stamp `author_identity:"personal"` + `author_business_id:null`. No switching UI. Existing rows backfilled by the migration.
- **Review rule**: `reviews_router._roles_for` now has an explicit Boat Pro ↔ Boat Pro block (either identity) with a clear 400 message. No category logic added; all other pairings unchanged. NOTE/loophole: a Boat Pro's PERSONAL identity still shares `account_type=="boat_pro"`, so pro→pro is blocked in BOTH identities (no loophole). Single-side pro reviews (customer→pro, owner→crew) unchanged.
- **Migration** `backend/migrations/split_boat_pro_identity.py`: dry-run default (report only), `--commit` backs up users/boat_pros/author-identity collections to `migration_backups/<ts>/` then writes idempotently (skips rows already migrated / already having the field). Current handle→business_handle; email+photo stay personal; new personal handle generated + `personal_handle_pending:true`; business_email blank. Old user fields kept readable.
- **Dry-run counts (2026-10-10)**: 12 boat_pro users; 8 to split; 0 already migrated; 4 browse-only pros without a listing (skipped); 0 Featured accounts whose credentials become hidden. last_identity backfill 166 users. author_identity backfill posts 35 / comments 10 / discussions 9 / reviews 16 / mutual_reviews 2 / dm_messages 37 / dm_threads 26.
- **Subscription/payment fields audit (for the later billing project)**: `users.subscription_tier`, `users.subscription_expires`, `users.founding_partner`, `users.founding_cohort`, plus scheduler notify bookkeeping (`winback_sent_for`, `renewal_notified_for`, `subscription_expiring_notified_at`); `boat_pros.tier` (now a non-authoritative mirror); collection `payment_transactions` (session_id, user_id, email, package_id, amount, currency, metadata{tier,audience,months}, status, payment_status, applied_at, created_at); collection `identity_sessions` (Stripe Identity, separate product); config `backend/.env STRIPE_API_KEY`, `constants.SUBSCRIPTION_PACKAGES`/`package_total()`, webhook `/api/webhook/stripe`. Lazy expiry downgrade in `deps.current_user_from_token` + nightly `scheduler.py`. NO billing/Stripe code was touched in this task.
