# UI/UX STANDARDS (BINDING — read before any UI task)

This section is the single source of truth for all visual and interaction work on BOTH surfaces (web `/app/frontend`, mobile `/app/mobile`). It documents the design foundation that ALREADY exists and the rules every future task must follow. Do not redesign; reuse these tokens and components. If a value here conflicts with code, fix the code to match this section (flag it first).

## 1. Brand tokens (never hardcode in a screen)
- Navy `#1F3F5B` · Teal `#55B3AD` · Orange `#F2A03B`. These are the only brand colors. White/neutral only for backgrounds and secondary text.
- Web: use CSS variables in `src/index.css` (`--smn-navy`, `--smn-teal`, `--smn-orange`, and the semantic scales below). Mobile: use `useTheme().colors` / `makeStyles(colors => ...)` from `src/theme.ts`. Never write a hex literal or font family inside a screen/component; add or reuse a token.
- Font: base/body = **Montserrat** (web, set on `html,body,#root`; mobile = the platform system sans-serif). Existing display-heading face is **Poppins** via the `.font-display` utility — keep using it for display headings only. Do not introduce new fonts.

### Existing color tokens
Web (`src/index.css`, `:root` + `html.dark`):
- Surfaces: `--smn-bg` (#F7FAFC / dark #0B1220), `--smn-surface` (#FFFFFF / #1B2740), `--smn-band-alt`, `--smn-border` (#E2E8F0 / #2C3A52).
- Text scale (WCAG-AA tuned, prefer over ad-hoc `text-slate-*`): `--smn-text`, `--smn-text-secondary`, `--smn-text-tertiary`, `--smn-text-muted`, `--smn-text-disabled`, `--smn-text-placeholder`, `--smn-heading`, `--smn-link`, `--smn-on-accent` (#10233A — ink on orange/teal fills).
- Icon tones: `--icon-default/muted/accent/brand/hover/on-accent/disabled`.
- Shadcn base tokens (`@layer base`): `--primary` = navy, `--secondary` = teal, `--accent` = orange, `--destructive`, `--border`, `--input`, `--ring`, `--radius` = `0.75rem` (12px). Tailwind radii: `lg`=var(--radius), `md`=−2px, `sm`=−4px.
- Helpers: `.smn-heading`, `.smn-card`, `.smn-band-a/.smn-band-b`, `.no-scrollbar`.

Mobile (`src/theme.ts`, `light` + `dark`): `surface`, `onSurface`, `surfaceSecondary/Tertiary` + their `on*`, `muted`, `brand/brandPrimary/brandSecondary/brandTertiary` + `on*`, named `navy/teal/tealSoft/accent/onAccent/accentSoft/onAccentSoft`, status `success/warning/error/info` + `on*`, `border/borderStrong/divider`, `tabActive`. Every fill has a matching `on*` ink chosen for WCAG-AA — always pair them (e.g. `backgroundColor: colors.accent` with `color: colors.onAccent`).

## 2. Layout and spacing
- 16px side padding on every screen, sheet, chip row, and card. Mobile `Screen` (`padded` default) and `Card` already apply 16; web pages use `px-4` (16px) side gutters.
- Spacing scale in use (stick to these, no arbitrary values): 4, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 22, 24, 32. Treat 8 as the base grid.
- Corner radii by component type (as built): buttons / chips / badges = pill (`borderRadius: 999`); cards = 18 (mobile) / `--radius` 12px (web); inputs = 12; modal = rounded top (mobile sheet) / 12 (web dialog). Match the existing radius for the component type; do not invent new ones.

## 3. Dark mode (mandatory for every element)
- Web toggles via the `html.dark` class (`ThemeToggle`); mobile via `useTheme()` with pref light|dark|system persisted at `smn-theme-pref`. Both surfaces ship full light AND dark palettes.
- Every new or changed element must be legible in BOTH modes. Check text and icon contrast against the surface it sits on. On web, prefer the semantic `--smn-text-*` vars (they auto-flip) over raw `text-slate-*`. On mobile, use the `on*` token paired with its surface.
- Do not hardcode white text on orange/teal (fails AA ~2.1–2.5:1). Use `--smn-on-accent` / `colors.onAccent` (#10233A). Existing CSS contrast guards enforce this on web; respect the same on mobile.

## 4. Components (reuse — do not fork)
- Mobile shared (`src/components/ui.tsx`): `Button` (variants `primary`=orange, `secondary`=navy/teal-in-dark, `ghost`, `danger`; pill, h50), `Card`, `Badge` (read-only; tones teal/accent/navy/muted), `Chip` (tappable; active=navy fill), `Avatar`, `Loading`, `EmptyState`, `ErrorState`, `Eyebrow`. Chrome: `Screen` (`src/components/Screen.tsx` — safe-area top, navy header, back button, 16 padding). Overlays: `MotionModal`. Icons: `Icon` (`@react-native-vector-icons`-based).
- Web shared: Shadcn UI in `src/components/ui/` (`button`, `select`, `dropdown-menu`, `sheet`, `dialog`, `drawer`, `tabs`, `toggle-group`, `input`, `textarea`, `badge`, `card`, `popover`, `radio-group`, `switch`, `skeleton`, `sonner`/toast, etc.). App wrappers: `Modal.jsx` (portal, Esc/backdrop close, body-scroll lock, mobile bottom-sheet fill / sm+ centered dialog ≤85dvh, sizes sm/md/lg), `Icon.jsx`, `SectionEyebrow`, `Avatar`, `Rating`.
- Do NOT create a new variant of a button, card, sheet, chip, input, or badge if one exists. If something is genuinely missing, extend the shared component and state that you did so in your summary.

## 5. Touch and layout safety
- Tap targets ≥44px. No horizontal scroll at any width (web enforces `overflow-x: clip`; keep it). Sheets and menus must never cut off at screen edges. Keyboard must never cover the focused input (mobile: use the `expo-keyboard-experience` pattern / `KeyboardAvoidingView`; web: scroll into view). Respect safe areas on mobile (`SafeAreaView`/insets — `Screen` does the top edge).

## 6. Required states per screen
Every screen must define and handle: Loading (skeleton/`Loading`), Empty (`EmptyState` with a message + one primary action), Error (`ErrorState` with message + retry), Success (toast via `sonner`/`ToastContext`), and long-text / missing-image behavior (truncate or wrap gracefully; show avatar/initials or placeholder, never a broken image).

## 7. Platform parity
Any UI change applies to web AND mobile in the same pass, with the equivalent control and identical options/labels, unless the user explicitly says one platform only.

## 8. Naming and accessibility
Every interactive element has a stable test id and an accessibility label: `data-testid` + `aria-label` on web, `testID` + `accessibilityLabel` on mobile. Use kebab-case describing function, not style. Refer to screens and elements by these names in summaries.

## 9. Copy
No em dashes. Use exactly the copy the user provides; do not invent marketing text. Never use "Founding Member" wording in new copy.

## 10. Selection controls (reduce clutter)
- 2–4 mutually-exclusive, equally-important, always-visible options (e.g. Week | Month): use a **segmented control** — web `toggle-group` (or `tabs`), mobile a segmented row of `Chip`s acting as one control.
- 5+ options, long lists, or rarely-changed choices (market, category, sort): use a **dropdown** on web (`select` / `dropdown-menu`) or the existing **bottom-sheet picker** on mobile.
- Combine multiple filters into ONE "Filters" button showing a count of active filters; it opens a sheet/panel with Apply and Clear. Do not show several rows of chips.
- Active filters may render as one compact line of removable chips, and only while something is active.
- Maximum ONE horizontal chip row per screen; never stack two. Chip rows follow the mobile chip-row rule (single horizontal scroller, no wrap, `flexShrink:0`, spacing via `contentContainerStyle`).
- Remember the user's last selection where sensible (market, category) and show it as the control's current value.
- Read-only labels (status, tier, category tags on a card) must look different from tappable controls — use `Badge` (read-only) vs `Chip` (tappable); never style a static label like a button.
- Web and mobile use the equivalent control for each platform, with the same options and labels.

## 11. Verification (per UI change)
After any UI change, take one screenshot per changed screen at phone width (390px) in BOTH light and dark, plus one at laptop width for web. Do NOT run the full testing agent unless the user asks. Report anything you could not verify.

## Plans and Pricing (SOURCE OF TRUTH - Jenn, 2026-10-09)
This section is authoritative for plan names, prices, features, intervals, and gating. On any conflict, this wins; reconcile code/screens to it.

### Sailors and Owners
- Free: sign up free (the page does not list free-tier features; not verified).
- Boat Owner Premium: $6.99/month. Features: identity verification; short bio; experience and sailing preferences; unlimited posts and full post access; direct messaging; photo instead of avatar.
- Billing intervals: 1 month; 3 months (save 10%); 6 months (save 15%); 12 months (save 20%).
- Note (Jenn, 2026-10-09): certifications are NOT a Boat Owner Premium feature. The live Pricing page may still list "certifications" in the bio feature; flag for removal when reconciling.

### Boat Pros
Free Boat Pro accounts are browse-only: no public directory listing until a plan is chosen. One account, one login, one subscription; the tier belongs to the person who signed up and the business listing's features follow it.
- Basic: $12.99/month. Basic listing; 1 market; company name and contact info; 1+ category of work; website link; direct messaging; no reviews. No logo, no cover photo, no gallery.
- Featured: $19.99/month. Everything in Basic, plus 2 markets and reviews included. Logo and cover photo only, no gallery.
- Premium: $29.99/month. Everything in Featured, plus unlimited markets, logo + photos, credentials, newsletter feature. Photo limit: logo + cover + 5 gallery photos (see Photo limits below; the "logo + 6 photos" shorthand is superseded by the 5-gallery rule).
- Billing intervals: 1 month; 6 months (save 10%); 12 months (save 20%).
- Trust and credentials is Premium ONLY. Basic and Featured are gated with an upgrade nudge.
- Photo limits by tier: Basic = none; Featured = logo and cover; Premium = logo, cover, and 5 gallery photos.
- Data kept when a plan is lower (Jenn, 2026-10-09): any data EXCLUDING photos that an account entered before being downgraded (or while free) is kept but hidden publicly, and shown again on upgrade. Photos over a tier's limit: recommended kept-but-hidden and restored on upgrade (storage cost small) - Jenn to confirm.
- Boat Pro accounts cannot review other Boat Pros that share any category (see Rules).

### Clubs and Orgs
- Free to join: listed in the Clubs and Orgs directory; events auto-populated when possible; 1 post and 1 volunteer opportunity per month; logo and cover photo; reviews; RSVPs and attendee notifications; get discovered by local sailors.
- Community Partner: paid upgrade for direct messaging, unlimited event posts, more photos, more reach. Page shows "Contact us" rather than a price. Price fluctuates and is paid DIRECTLY to the business, NOT through Stripe (Jenn, 2026-10-09). Older note ($1000/yr, admin-activated only) superseded on price; activation method not verified. Community Partner is intentionally NOT a Stripe product: do not build checkout/billing for it.

### Rules (Jenn, 2026-10-09)
- Billing model (DECIDED; build PARKED - see the "Billing: Stripe subscriptions (PARKED)" section): plans paid at signup, auto-renew at end of committed term (1/3/6/12 months). Upgrades immediate with credit for unused time. Downgrades never charge and take effect at end of current term; user keeps current tier until then. Cancel stops renewal, keeps tier until term ends.
- Current behavior until the billing project is done: every plan change is a one-time Checkout payment charged at full price, no renewal or credit (agent diagnosis, 2026-10-09). Stripe in TEST mode. Do NOT change billing code in unrelated tasks.
- Boat Pro accounts, in either identity, cannot write reviews of other Boat Pros that share any category (updated 2026-10-09; replaces the earlier rule covering all Boat Pros; cautious reading chosen). Community guidelines should state a Boat Pro's personal account does not review competitors in the same category.
- The in-place photo editor currently shows 6 gallery slots for Boat Pros; under the Premium rule it should show 5 (cover plus 5 gallery). Fixed in Step 4.

## Billing: Stripe subscriptions (PARKED, pending approval)
Status: PLAN APPROVED, IMPLEMENTATION NOT APPROVED. Do NOT build until the user says "resume billing". UI fixes come first; the Oct 17 alpha runs on the CURRENT one-time test setup. Do not provision the sandbox, change any billing/Stripe code, or change the webhook path until resumed.

### Goal
Replace the current one-time Checkout payments (every plan change charges full price; downgrades wrongly charge) with real Stripe recurring subscriptions in TEST mode. Scope: self-serve paid plans only - Boat Pro tiers (basic/featured/premium) and Member Premium (Sailor + Boat Owner $6.99), through shared code. Community Partner is NOT a Stripe product - do not touch it. Change only: backend billing (new Stripe SDK module + webhooks), Pricing page buttons, and the Profile Subscription section, on web AND mobile. Do not change prices, tiers, or other screens.

### User's billing decisions (binding)
1. Plans paid at signup, auto-renew at end of committed term (1/3/6/12 months per plan).
2. Upgrades take effect immediately; unused time on the old plan is credited toward the new one.
3. Downgrades never charge; they take effect at the end of the current committed term; user keeps current tier until then.
4. Cancel stops renewal and keeps the tier until the term ends.

### Integration path (confirmed in plan)
- Move from `emergentintegrations` one-time `StripeCheckout` (Flow B, shared `sk_test_emergent`, cannot do subscriptions) to the **raw Stripe SDK on a claimable test sandbox (Flow A)**. Provisioning is one idempotent server call (`POST /stripe/sandboxes`) - NOT to be run until resumed. Gives its own `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`/`STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`/`STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`.
- Webhook path moves from `/api/webhook/stripe` (current) to `/api/stripe/webhook` (Flow A). Do NOT change this until resumed.

### Stripe objects / mapping
- Product A "Boat Pro Listing"; Product B "Member Premium" (Sailor + Boat Owner, both -> tier `premium`).
- One recurring Price per existing `package_id` (lookup_key = package_id so the frontend keeps sending the same identifier). `recurring = {interval: "month", interval_count: N}` where N = committed term (1/6/12 for Boat Pro; 1/3/6/12 for members); `unit_amount` = the already-discounted term total (discount baked into the amount, not a Stripe coupon). Tier/audience in price `metadata` for webhook price->tier mapping.
- Prices mirror `constants.py SUBSCRIPTION_PACKAGES` EXACTLY (live code values: Boat Pro 12.99/19.99/29.99 monthly; member 6.99; discounts pro 6/12 = 10/20%, member 3/6/12 = 10/15/20%). NOTE discrepancy to resolve at resume time: PRD "Original problem statement" line still reads Boat Pro 19.99/29.99/49.99 - the code values are the source of truth unless the user says otherwise (question was asked; default = code values).

### Lifecycle mechanics (verified against current Stripe docs)
- Signup: Checkout `mode=subscription` creates Customer + Subscription; store `stripe_customer_id` + `stripe_subscription_id` on the user.
- Upgrade (immediate + credit): `Subscription.modify(items=[{id, price: new}], proration_behavior="always_invoice")` - credits unused time, invoices only the prorated difference now; renewal date starts now. (Caveat: upgrading across a different term length resets the billing cycle and prorates by time remaining; clean within the same term length.)
- Downgrade (no charge, at term end): **Subscription Schedule** - phase 1 current price to `current_period_end`, phase 2 lower price from that date, `proration_behavior="none"`. No charge for the change; the lower price is first billed at the normal renewal boundary.
- Undo scheduled downgrade ("Keep [current plan]"): `release` the schedule / drop the future phase.
- Cancel: `cancel_at_period_end=true`; reactivate with `false`.
- Renewal: `invoice.paid` (billing_reason `subscription_cycle`) extends `subscription_expires`.

### Webhooks drive the DB (Stripe = single source of truth)
Signature-verified handler on `checkout.session.completed`, `customer.subscription.created/updated/deleted`, `invoice.paid`, `invoice.payment_failed`. Each sets `subscription_tier` (from active price->tier; Boat Pro also `boat_pros.tier`), `subscription_expires = current_period_end`, `subscription_cancel_at_period_end`, and `pending_tier`/`pending_change_at` (from any active schedule). Retire manual `_apply_paid_subscription` + lazy-expiry downgrade for subscription accounts so app and Stripe cannot diverge.
- Failed renewal: `invoice.payment_failed` -> keep tier, mark `past_due`, notify user to update card; Stripe Smart Retries (dunning) reattempt; if all fail -> `unpaid`/`canceled` -> `customer.subscription.deleted`/`updated` -> downgrade to `free`. Access kept through the retry/grace window, revoked only after Stripe exhausts retries.

### UI (at implementation time; follow UI/UX Standards)
- Profile Subscription section: current plan, renewal date, scheduled-change banner ("Your plan changes to Basic on [date]" + "Keep [current plan]" undo), cancel. Web + mobile.
- Pricing buttons: contextual Upgrade / Current plan / Downgrade. Word the scheduled-downgrade message so the lower renewal price reads clearly as a NORMAL RENEWAL, not a downgrade charge.

### PENDING conditions (pick up at resume)
- Keep the CURRENT checkout working until the new flow passes EVERY verification item on the list below, then cut over and tell the user.
- Alpha + beta deployments MUST stay on TEST keys until the user chooses to claim the sandbox for going live. ACTION/CONFIRM at resume: verify that deploying does NOT switch to live keys on its own (per the integration playbook the platform auto-swaps to live keys only AFTER the user claims the sandbox / completes KYC; confirm and state this explicitly before any deploy).
- Grandfather existing one-time-payment accounts as proposed (keep tier until it lapses; convert on next change; no migration, no charge). SKIP refunding the earlier test charge (jenn@sailmorenow.com pro_basic_1 $12.99, session cs_test_a1ZPFOldMCCZZx) - leave it.

### OPEN QUESTIONS to answer BEFORE building
- (a) How does Stripe hosted Checkout return to the app in **Expo Go** on mobile (deep link / return URL handling under Expo Go vs a native build)?
- (b) How does the **free trial convert to a paid subscription** (is there a trial today, and how should trial->paid be modeled - Stripe `trial_period_days` / trial on the subscription)?
- (c) Where does a user **update their card after a failed renewal** (Stripe Customer Portal vs an in-app update) - decide and wire it.

### Verification checklist (test mode, test clocks) - must all pass before cutover
New signup; auto-renewal via test clock; Premium->Featured, Premium->Basic, Featured->Basic (all scheduled, ASSERT zero charge); cancel; undo scheduled change; upgrade with proration credit; failed renewal (card `4000 0000 0000 0341`) through dunning to downgrade. Report anything not verifiable.

## Original problem statement
Build "Sail More Now" — an info hub for the Pacific NW sailing community: Boat Pro directory, community feed, events, DMs, mutual reviews, discussions. Subscription revenue (Boat Pros: Basic $19.99 / Featured $29.99 / Premium $49.99; Sailors Premium $6.99) + advertising. Platforms: React (Web) + Expo/React Native (Mobile) + FastAPI backend. Auth: Emergent Google login + email/password. Dark/Light theme, role-based UI, RSVP notifications, market filtering, reactions, photo attachments, referral attribution (?ref=), Pro/Org dashboards, insights trends, checklist completeness.

## User personas
Boat Owner · Sailor/Crew · Boat Pro · Community Partner.

## Architecture (Jun 2026)
- React (CRA+craco), Tailwind, Shadcn, React portals, service worker `/sw.js` for Web Push. ThemeContext (localStorage `smn-theme`).
- Expo SDK 57, expo-router file-based routing, mobile theme store in `src/theme.ts` (light/dark/system).
- FastAPI + Motor (MongoDB). Modular `/routers`, all `/api`-prefixed. APScheduler nightly sweeps (`scheduler.py`).
- Paywall gating in `deps.py` (`is_paid_subscriber` / `require_interaction()` → 402 upgrade_required).
- Integrations: Emergent Google Auth, Stripe (test mode), Resend, Zoho Campaigns, VAPID Web Push.

## Current subscription / gating model
- Tiers: `free`; `basic`/`featured`/`premium` (Boat Pro); `premium` (Sailor/Boat Owner $6.99/mo); `community_partner` (Org $1000/yr, admin-activated only).
- Gated (402): DMs, posts, comments, reactions, reviews, crew-bank listings. Free orgs post volunteer opportunities only.
- Founding Partner = first 5 community_partner accounts (gold badge). Clubs directory `/clubs`, `GET /api/partners`.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Import health, undo, dismiss/why-suggested, block + followers list)
- **Import Health Check**: web `/admin/events` `needsContact` = aggregator event missing organizer_name or organizer_email → `needs-contact-{id}` badge + `select-missing-contact` button (selects all such events straight into the bulk bar).
- **Undo (moderation)**: `POST /api/admin/moderation/remove` now returns `{snapshot,target_type}`; added `POST /api/admin/moderation/restore-doc` (re-insert) and `/hide` (re-hide). Web NotificationsBell uses a sonner toast Undo action; mobile notifications use an inline `notif-undo-{id}` pill.
- **Dismiss suggestion**: `POST /api/follows/suggestions/{id}/dismiss` (stored in `follow_dismissals`, excluded from future suggestions). X button on web + mobile suggestion rows.
- **Why suggested**: `/api/follows/suggestions` items carry a `reason` (In {market} / Popular · N followers / Community organization / New to the community); shown on both surfaces; ranking prioritizes same-market then orgs then follower count.
- **Block + followers list**: `follow_blocks` collection. `GET /api/follows/followers`, `GET /api/follows/blocked`, `POST/DELETE /api/follows/block/{id}`. Block removes the follow both ways and prevents re-follow (follow → 403); follow endpoint also guards self-follow (400) and following a user you've blocked (400). UI on the Following page/screen (web `FollowingList.jsx`, mobile `following-list.tsx`): Your followers list with Block, plus a Blocked list with Unblock.


- **Bulk event cleanup**: `PATCH /api/admin/events/bulk` {event_ids, organizer_name?, organizer_email?} (admin-only) sets organizer/RSVP-contact on many events at once, incl imported/aggregator. Web `/admin/events`: per-card `select-event-{id}` checkboxes + sticky bulk bar (`bulk-organizer-name`, `bulk-organizer-email`, `bulk-apply`).
- **Restore from alert**: `POST /api/admin/moderation/restore` (unhide + dismiss open reports) and `/remove` (delete target + resolve reports), admin-only, by {target_type,target_id}. Wired into the `content_hidden` admin notification with one-tap Restore/Remove — web `NotificationsBell.jsx` (rows now a div w/ inline actions) + mobile `notifications.tsx` pill buttons.
- **Follow suggestions**: `GET /api/follows/suggestions?limit=6` returns not-yet-followed accounts (orgs first, then by follower_count; excludes self + followed). Shown on the Following list when the user follows <5 — web `FollowingList.jsx` (`follow-suggestions` + `suggestion-follow-{id}`) and mobile `following-list.tsx` (footer + empty-state).


- **Flag threshold rules**: `app_settings` doc `moderation.inaccurate_auto_hide_threshold` (admin `GET`/`PUT /api/admin/moderation-settings`). When a target reaches N open `inaccurate` reports it auto-hides (`hidden_pending_review`); `inappropriate` still hides on the first flag; 0 = never. Web control on `/admin/reports` (`moderation-threshold-card`).
- **Report notifications**: on any auto-hide, every admin (platform_role admin or ADMIN_EMAILS) gets an in-app `content_hidden` notification linking to `/admin/reports` (deduped if already hidden).
- **Following search**: search-by-name/@handle box on the Following list (web `FollowingList.jsx`, mobile `following-list.tsx`).
- **Web engagement bar**: Landing CTA card now shows live counts (members, boat pros, upcoming events, PNW markets) from `/api/stats/public`; "Early access" wording removed, CTAs kept (`CommunityStats` in `Landing.jsx`).
- **Crew Bank**: removed **Helm** and **Navigator** from `CREW_POSITIONS` (backend, drives `/api/crew-positions`) and from web hardcoded lists (`CrewBank.jsx`, `CrewBankModal.jsx`); mobile crew-detail **Close** button is now a bordered, dark-mode-readable pill.
- **Admin event editing**: new admin-only `PATCH /api/admin/events/{event_id}` (schema `EventAdminUpdate`) edits ANY event — including imported/aggregator ones the owner-scoped PATCH blocks — covering title, description, organizer name, RSVP contact email, registration/link, time, location, market, category; sets `admin_edited`. Web UI: "Edit details" button + modal on each card in `/admin/events`.


- **Moderation/flagging** (extends existing reports system): `POST /api/reports` now accepts `target_type` in {post, discussion, comment, event} + `category` in {inappropriate, inaccurate, other}. A `category=inappropriate` flag sets `hidden_pending_review=true` on the target, which is then excluded from all public reads (`GET /api/posts`, `/api/events`, `/api/discussions` list + detail, `/api/posts/{id}/comments`). Admin `PATCH /api/admin/reports/{id}`: dismiss OR resolve-without-delete **restores/unhides**; resolve + `delete_target` permanently removes. Web `FlagButton.jsx` (3 structured reasons + hide note) added to posts/discussions/comments + Event modal; mobile new `ReportButton.tsx` on Hub feed cards + Event detail. Author never sees a flag button on their own content. Admin Reports (web + mobile) show category + Hidden badges. Photos are covered by flagging their containing post/event (no separate photo target).
- **Support inbox**: web `/admin/contact` gained source filter (From Profile / Public site / Partner) + per-message source badge; mobile admin dashboard gained a **Support tab** (list + reply + source filter) via existing `/admin/contact` + `/admin/contact/{id}/reply`.
- **Subject presets**: Billing/Bug/Feedback/Other quick-pick chips above the Subject box in the Profile Contact Us form (web + mobile) fill the subject.
- **Following list**: new manage-who-you-follow page (web `/following` `FollowingList.jsx`; mobile `/following-list`) reached from Profile; splits People vs Organizations (by `account_type` from `GET /api/follows`) with one-tap unfollow (`DELETE /api/follows/{id}`).
- **Verified filter/badge**: `GET /api/boat-pros` returns `identity_verified` per pro; `GET /api/crew-bank/standalone` + `/open` return `user_verified` and accept `verified_only=true`. Web + mobile: ID-verified badge on Crew Bank AND Boat Pro cards; a "Verified only" toggle on Crew Bank ONLY (per user request, no toggle on Boat Pros).


- **Mobile Hub filter reorder**: `mobile/app/(tabs)/feed.tsx` FILTERS now read All · Crew Bank · Feed · Forum · Blog · Sailing Opportunities · Volunteer (Crew Bank promoted to right after All). Mobile-only — web's Hub has no equivalent chip row.
- **Profile "Contact Us" form (web + mobile)**: new signed-in support form with a Subject field + open message box; the member's name/email are auto-filled from their account. New backend endpoint `POST /api/support/contact` (auth-required, rate-limited 5/10min, stored in `contact_messages` with `source:"profile_support"`) delivers via Resend to **customersupport@sailmorenow.com** (distinct from the public `POST /contact` → info@, left unchanged per user choice). On submit both surfaces show exactly: "Thank you for contacting us. We will respond to your inquiry within 48 hours." Web: `Profile.jsx` `support-contact-section`; mobile: `(tabs)/profile.tsx` CONTACT US section (between Appearance and Log out).


- **Free tier fully blocked from DMs (not just at send)**: added an upgrade wall on the Messages surface for free accounts — web `Messages.jsx` (`messages-upgrade-wall` + `messages-upgrade-btn` → /pricing) and mobile `(tabs)/messages.tsx` (same, and the "New group" button is hidden for free users). Backend already 402-gated create/send; this closes the UI entry point. (Paid = `subscription_tier !== "free"`.)
- **Group-compose avatar bug**: web was rendering the raw `avatar`/`picture` string as text (a long token spilled across the row). Added an `Avatar` helper in `Messages.jsx` that renders an `<img>` only for http(s) URLs, otherwise the name initial. Applied to the thread list + people-search results. (Mobile already used the shared `Avatar` component, so it was unaffected.)
- **Search box now clears after picking a person** in the group composer (web `pick()` + mobile `pick()` reset the query and results), so you can immediately search the next person.


- Mobile had NO central 402 handler (web's `frontend/src/lib/api.js` interceptor already toasts "See plans" → /pricing). Added `mobile/src/lib/upgrade.ts` `handleUpgrade(e, toast)` → on a 402 `upgrade_required` it shows a warning toast and routes to `/pricing`. Wired into mobile group create (`messages/new.tsx`), chat send (`messages/[id].tsx`, covers 1:1 + group), and Profile ID-verify (`profile.tsx`). Web `Messages.jsx` + `IdentityVerification.jsx` catches now skip their own toast on 402 to avoid double-toasting.
- `POST /api/identity/session` is now paywalled BEFORE the Stripe call: free tier (any account) → 402 generic upgrade; Basic-tier Boat Pros → 402 with pro-specific copy ("included with Featured and Premium Boat Pro plans"); Premium sailors/owners, Featured/Premium pros and Community Partners are eligible. (Group DMs were already 402-gated via `require_interaction`; the gap was purely client-side prompting.)


- **Group DMs (true group threads)**: `dm_threads` now supports `is_group:true` docs {thread_id:`group_<uuid>`, title, participants[], created_by, reads:{uid:iso}}; group messages have `from_id`/`from_name`, `to_id:null`. New endpoints `POST /api/messages/group` (create, ≥2 people, paywalled via require_interaction) and `POST /api/messages/group/{thread_id}` (send). `GET /messages/threads` returns groups with `members[]`+`title`+per-user `unread` (via reads map); `POST /messages/{tid}/read` branches to update reads for groups. 1:1 flow unchanged. Route order: group routes defined BEFORE `/messages/{recipient_id}`. Clients: web `/messages` new-group modal + group sidebar rows + sender-name bubbles; mobile `/messages/new` compose screen + group rows + `group=1` param on the chat screen. Verified iter80.
- **Follow people (not just orgs)**: follow backend already accepted any user_id; exposed counts on `GET /api/u/{handle}` (`follower_count`, `following_count`, `is_following`, now takes viewer token). Added Follow/Following button + counts to web `HandleProfile.jsx` and mobile `/u/[handle]`. Toggle uses existing `POST/DELETE /api/follows/{id}`.
- **Map clustering**: web `EventMap.jsx` now clusters venue pins with `leaflet.markercluster` (branded cluster bubbles, zoom-to-split); market-center aggregate pins unchanged. Mobile `EventMap.native.tsx` uses `react-native-map-clustering` (supercluster) — DEVICE-ONLY, not visible on the Expo web preview (web fallback list unchanged).
- **Mobile ID verification**: added IDENTITY section to the mobile Profile calling the existing Stripe Identity endpoints (`POST /api/identity/session` → open URL via `expo-web-browser` → poll `GET /api/identity/status`). Reuses existing STRIPE_API_KEY (no new keys). Under the current TEST key the session create returns a friendly 409 (expected until a live Identity-enabled key is set at launch).
- **users/search polish**: `GET /api/users/search` now matches inner name words (word-prefix) + handle prefix, so "marina"/"jane" resolve (also improves @mentions).

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Accessibility phase 2, iteration_72) — tested green, BOTH surfaces
Four user-requested a11y improvements across web + mobile. Frontend-only, no backend change.
1. **Visible keyboard focus outlines (web)** — replaced the blanket `button, a { outline:none }` in `index.css` with `:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid var(--smn-teal) !important }` (dark → #6FD7CF), plus `:focus:not(:focus-visible){outline:none}` so pointer clicks stay ring-free. `!important` beats the app's scattered Tailwind `outline-none` utilities.
2. **Respect reduce motion**
   - Web: expanded the `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block in `index.css` to globally neutralise ALL animations + transitions (`animation-duration/transition-duration: 0.001ms !important`, hero Ken-Burns/vignette off, scroll-behavior auto).
   - Mobile: new `src/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts` (subscribes to `AccessibilityInfo.isReduceMotionEnabled` + `reduceMotionChanged`) and `src/components/MotionModal.tsx` — a drop-in `<Modal>` wrapper that forces `animationType="none"` when reduced. Aliased as `Modal` in the 6 modal files (feed, directory, profile, my-events, crew-bank, index).
3. **DEV-only contrast checker overlay (web)** — new `components/A11yContrastOverlay.jsx`, mounted in `App.js` ONLY when `process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"`. Floating "A11y ⚠ Contrast" button (or Alt+Shift+C) scans visible text, composites effective background through translucent ancestors, computes WCAG AA ratios (4.5:1 normal / 3:1 large), outlines failures in dashed red + lists them in a summary panel with a Clear action. Never ships to production.
4. **Meaningful alt text**
   - Web: user/author/org avatars now carry descriptive `alt` (Header, Profile, CommunityHub, PartnerEvents). Purely decorative images (hero poster, background logo motif, role icons paired with visible labels) correctly remain `alt="" aria-hidden`.
   - Mobile: `accessibilityLabel` added to meaningful RN `Image`s (Avatar in ui.tsx, home logo, clubs/partner avatars, event flyer, post/hub photo, directory work samples, PhotoPicker preview); home hero `ImageBackground` marked non-accessible.

NOTE: the contrast overlay currently surfaces ~21 pre-existing low-contrast text pairs on Landing — these are the tool doing its job, NOT regressions; a dedicated pass could resolve them.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (following feed, follower counts, map filters, avatar fix; iter79)
- **Mobile avatar upload bug fixed:** expo-image-manipulator isn't reliable on the Expo web preview, so `changeAvatar` now uploads the picked image directly on web (`Platform.OS==='web'` → blob → `File`) and only downscales via the manipulator on native. Verified 200 + avatar updates on the web preview.
- **Following Feed:** `GET /api/follows/feed` merges upcoming events + recent posts across all followed orgs (org name/avatar per item). Web Clubs shows a "From orgs you follow" section; mobile Clubs shows it as a list header. Refreshes on follow/unfollow.
- **Follower counts + notify:** `/api/partners` returns `follower_count` (aggregated); shown on web + mobile club cards. Following an org creates a `new_follower` notification for that org on first follow (idempotent).
- **Map filters:** confirmed the market/category filters stay visible in Events map view on both surfaces and filter the pins (EventMap receives the filtered set) — no new controls needed.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (follow, event maps, avatars, revenue trend; iter78)
- **Org Following:** new `follows` collection + `routers/follows_router.py` (POST/DELETE `/api/follows/{org_id}`, GET `/api/follows`, GET `/api/follows/{org_id}/activity` = per-org merged upcoming events + recent posts). Web `/clubs` cards got Follow toggles + an Activity modal; mobile `/clubs` got follow buttons + a dedicated `/org/[id]` activity screen.
- **Event Map exact pins:** `backend/geocode.py` geocodes event locations via OpenStreetMap Nominatim (free, no key) — on event create (fire-and-forget) and a one-shot startup backfill of existing events. Web Leaflet map now shows individual venue pins. Mobile got a Map toggle on `/events` using `react-native-maps` (platform files: `EventMap.native.tsx` real map / `EventMap.web.tsx` pinned-venue list fallback — the native tile map only renders in a native build / Expo Go, not the web preview).
- **Avatar Everywhere:** flipped author-avatar resolution to `picture` first (was `avatar` first) across posts/comments/crew/reviews/hub so uploaded photos show consistently on feed, comments, crew cards, messages, and profiles on both surfaces.
- **Revenue Trend:** `/api/admin/revenue` now returns a 6-month `trend` (collected revenue from `payment_transactions`); web renders an SVG sparkline and mobile renders monthly bars in the Revenue snapshot card.
- Verified iter78 (testing agent): all 4 PASS on web + mobile, no regressions. Report: `/app/test_reports/iteration_78.json`.
- NOTE: mobile interactive map needs a native build/Expo Go (react-native-maps has no web renderer). Startup logs a pre-existing `users.email_1` duplicate-index warning (2 jenn accounts) — unrelated to these changes.

Startup best-effort `init_storage`.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (full icon migration + toast rest, iter75)
- FULL icon-system migration to the unified `<Icon>` wrapper. Web: ~64 files migrated off direct `lucide-react` imports; registry expanded to 96 names (semantic + decorative kebab). Mobile: 25 files migrated off `@expo/vector-icons`; `<Icon>` now falls back to a raw Ionicons glyph for dynamic names, and the codemod passes `footprint={size}` so icons stay inline.
- ESLint guards flipped to hard **error** blocking the whole icon module on both surfaces (web `.eslintrc.json` blocks `lucide-react`; mobile `eslint.config.js` blocks `@expo/vector-icons`). Exempt: the Icon wrappers, shadcn `ui/`, `lib/eventCategories.js` (web), and `app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx` (mobile). `docs/ICONS.md` regenerated (96 icons) as the single reference (also for the WordPress site).
- Per user: org/partner IDENTITY spots now use the burgee (web Clubs org-avatar, mobile Clubs/Directory/partner, both role selectors' Organization); megaphone kept only for post/announce-event ACTIONS. Boat-Pro role standardized to the canonical handshake; mobile role selector's wrench (`construct-outline`) retired → `boatPro`.
- Toast-the-rest: web already toasted reviews/event-blast/reaction-errors (now with semantic icons). Mobile: wired `useToast` into Reactions (error) and Boat-Pro review submit (warning/success/error), replacing `Alert`s.
- Codemods kept in `/app/scripts/codemod_web_icons.py` and `codemod_mobile_icons.py`.
- Build fix: `eslint-config-react-app@7.0.1` was missing from the frontend (required by react-scripts' webpack eslint base); installed it and removed the now-duplicate `extends: ["react-app"]` from `.eslintrc.json` (was causing a react-hooks plugin conflict under eslint 9). Web compiles clean; the icon-import guard stays active.
- Verified iter75 (testing agent): 13/13 web + 11/11 mobile routes render with no undefined-component crashes; mobile icons confirmed inline (footprint honored); mobile review toasts pass. Report: `/app/test_reports/iteration_75.json`.


- Landing role selector + Dashboard tiles migrated to name-based unified `<Icon>` (Sailor=crew, Boat Owner=boatOwner, Boat Pro=boatPro/handshake, Organization=org/burgee; dashboard tiles use edit/reviews/messages/events/myEvents/community/hub/crew/boatPro). Retired the last direct `Sailboat`/`Handshake`/`Wrench` lucide imports.
- ESLint guard (`frontend/.eslintrc.json`) now errors on direct `lucide-react` imports of `LifeBuoy`, `Sailboat`, `Handshake`, `Wrench` (Wrench fully retired — no service icon added, per user).
- Web sonner `<Toaster>` (App.js) now uses the four semantic `<Icon>` glyphs (success/error/info/warning) so web + mobile status feedback match exactly.
- Mobile toasts wired via `useToast` into RSVP (events/[id]), post-created (new-post), profile saves (handle/markets/avatar/account-switch), and DM send-error (messages/[id]); native `Alert` kept ONLY where action buttons are required (permission prompts, 402 upsell, account-type confirm).
- Docs-in-CI: `scripts/gen-icon-docs.js --check` fails when `docs/ICONS.md` drifts from the registries; installed as a git pre-commit hook (`scripts/hooks/pre-commit` + `scripts/install-hooks.sh`) and frontend package scripts `icons:docs` / `icons:check`.
- Verified iter74: 12/12 web+mobile+CI assertions pass, no bugs (`/app/test_reports/iteration_74.json`).

- **Review After Hire Nudge**: new daily scheduler job `review_nudges` (10am PT) — 3–5 days after a member marks a pro Hired, if they haven't reviewed, send an in-app nudge (deduped per hire via a `review_nudge` notification).
- **Saved Digest Links**: each saved item now carries a `url`; the weekly digest email wraps titles in links and the in-app Digest preview renders them as tappable links (post→/community, For Sale→/community?tab=marketplace, discussion→/community-hub/{id}).

UI now gates it up front — new `MessageLink` (web: CrewBank + CrewBankModal) and mobile `openDM` helper (crew-bank) show an "Upgrade to message" prompt instead of opening the thread for free/signed-out users.
- Verified: curl — free user crew-bank DM → 403 upgrade_required; sold toggle → {sold:true}; review with no comment → 200; ranking premium-first; digest preview includes saved. Web webpack compiled; mobile tsc clean on edited files. Backend + mobile restarted.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Bug fixes: render crash, For Sale placement, logged-in home, pro ranking, Hired? + free review, digest saved)
- **Render crash fix (critical)**: backend returns gated errors as `detail: {code, message}` (object); several screens passed it straight to `toast.error`/inline render → React "Objects are not valid as a React child {code, message}". Fixed centrally in `frontend/src/lib/api.js` interceptor — normalizes `detail` to a string (object→message, 422 array→joined). This also fixes the For Sale photo-upload crash and the "comments not working" symptom (free users hit the Premium paywall and crashed instead of seeing the upgrade prompt; backend comments verified working).
- **For Sale strictly on Marketplace**: removed For Sale from the Feed type filter + composer and excluded `for_sale` from the Feed list (web `Feed.jsx`) and from mobile non-marketplace views; mobile For Sale chip relabeled "Marketplace". Listings live only on the Marketplace tab/view.
- **Logged-in home**: Header brand link → `/community` when signed in; root `/` redirects signed-in users to `/community` (`HomeRoute` in `App.js`); signed-out landing/CTAs only for logged-out users.
- **Premium ranking**: `constants.TIER_RANK` corrected to `{premium:0, featured:1, basic:2}` so Premium pros always sort before Featured then Basic in directory + search (was featured-first).
- **Hired? + free review**: web button relabeled "Hired?"; `toggle_hire` no longer requires paid; `create_review` allows a free-tier member to review a pro they've marked Hired (otherwise still paid-gated). Verified: free user 403 before hire → hire ok → review 200 after.
- **Digest saved items**: new `compute_user_saved` (scheduler) feeds a "Your saved items" block into the weekly digest email (`mailer.community_digest_email_html` saved param) and the in-app Digest preview (`/api/digest/preview` + `DigestPreview.jsx`).
- Verified: backend curl (ranking order premium-first, digest preview saved, free hire+review), web webpack compiled + Community page smoke screenshot (no crash, correct tabs, For Sale absent from Feed), mobile tsc clean.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (newsletter copy + active-member welcome)
- **Newsletter opt-in copy** (web Onboarding + mobile Register signup): now "Sail More Now — the quarterly digest" / "PNW regatta calendar, community stories, and featured Boat Pros. One email a quarter, unsubscribe any time." (dropped "straight from Zoho", monthly→quarterly).
- **App signups are active members, not waitlist**: register + Google-onboarding set `member_status: "active"` and NO LONGER call the Zoho sync (the waitlist path); newsletter opt-in is kept in the DB for a future Zoho backfill. New signups get an active-member welcome email via Resend (`welcome_member_email_html`, from `notifications@notifications.sailmorenow.com`) — email-signup at register, Google-signup at first onboarding completion. `send_email` now supports an optional `from_email`/`EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS` (custom domain must be verified in Resend to actually override the managed proxy sender). The website waitlist form keeps its own (Zoho) path.


- **Event console bug (FIXED)**: root cause was `GET /admin/events` using a restricted field projection (dropped location/RSVP/flyer/links) while the feed returned full docs — admin edits to those fields never showed in the console. Now returns the full event doc (same source as the feed).
- **Relist in one tap**: `GET /api/posts/mine/for-sale` + a "My Listings" section on the profile (web + mobile) — one-tap Relist on sold items (clears Sold, renews 30 days).
- **Self profile edit**: `PATCH /api/auth/profile` — members edit their own name + email (email uniqueness checked). Account-details editor on web + mobile profile.
- **Admin moderation suite**:
  - Backend: `audit.py` (audit_logs with 30-day TTL index) + `routers/admin_content_router.py` — `PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/content/{entity}/{id}` for post/discussion(blog+forum)/comment/review (+event delete), admin event edit audited, `PATCH /api/admin/users/{id}` (safe fields), `POST /api/admin/users/{id}/ban|unban`, `GET /api/admin/users/search`, `GET /api/admin/audit-logs`. Ban = recoverable deactivate, gated in `current_user_from_token` (banned → logged out).
  - Web: reusable `AdminControls` (Edit + Delete, delete always confirms) on Feed posts, discussion detail, comments, reviews, and event detail. New admin console page `/admin/moderation` (Audit Log + Members: search/edit/ban) + Header nav link.
  - Mobile: `AdminControls` on post detail, discussion (hub) detail, comments (post + hub), reviews (directory), and event detail (delete; event edit is web-only).
  - Every admin edit/delete/ban is written to the audit log (who/what/when), auto-purged after 30 days.
- **Live wind embed**: SailFlow card is now config-driven — add an `embed_url` to any station in `backend/sailflow_stations.py` to render a live wind widget inline (web iframe / mobile WebView) with loading + error + fallback to the link card. Shipped with embeds OFF (all link cards) because a valid embed needs SailFlow's per-station widget generator URL; config comments explain how to enable.


- **Saved Searches (Marketplace)**: `saved_searches` collection + `POST/GET/DELETE /api/marketplace/searches`. New matching For-Sale listings notify savers (`saved_search` notification). Save-search button on web Feed + mobile Hub marketplace filters.
- **Make an Offer**: button on For-Sale listings (web Feed card + mobile post detail) → opens a DM to the seller pre-filled with the price (`?offer=`). Posting For-Sale already requires a paid tier (require_interaction), so sellers are always paid.
- **Unlocked Inbox Banner**: `GET /api/messages/unlock-banner` (+ `/ack`) — one-time "N messages waiting" banner for members who upgraded, precisely targeted by counting their locked-DM notifications. Shown on web Messages + mobile Messages list.
- **Price Drop Nudge**: `POST /api/posts/{id}/price` — a price drop sets `prev_price`/`price_dropped_at`/`bumped_at`, bumps the listing to the top of the Marketplace, shows a "Price drop" badge (7-day) + struck-through old price, and notifies members who bookmarked it. "Lower price" control for owners on both surfaces.
- **SailFlow weather card** (home, web + mobile): link card opening the nearest station per primary market (defaults to Greater Seattle when signed-out). Loading + error states. Editable mapping in `backend/sailflow_stations.py`, served via `GET /api/sailflow/station?market=`.
- **Web polish**: hero headline now clamps to 2 lines on desktop/tablet (`clamp()` + max-width, mobile unchanged); footer logo +40% (h-10→h-14); footer social icons (IG/FB/LinkedIn, new tab, aria-labels); signed-out Crew Bank video halved with text beside it.
- **Mobile**: social icons (IG/FB/LinkedIn, external) added to the Profile (Settings/About) screen.

## Changelog — Jun 2026 (Demo tour + seed variations + snapshot)
- **Seed variations**: `seed_demo(db, size)` supports `small` (4 users, 1 listing, 2 posts, 1 event, 1 RSVP, 2 reviews) vs `full` (everything). Admin `POST /api/admin/demo/seed` accepts `{size}`; `/admin/demo` page has a Small/Full toggle.

Back/Next/Exit + progress dots.
- Verified backend small/full/snapshot/reset via curl; web compiles clean. (Credit-saving mode: no testing agent / screenshots this round.)

- **Demo seed/reset** (`backend/demo_seed.py`, `routers/demo_router.py`): every demo record tagged `is_demo:true`. `seed_demo` creates 6 demo users (sailor, boat owner, org/partner, 3 boat pros), 3 vendor listings covering all 6 directory categories (Bottom Paint & Haul-out, Diesel Engine Specialist, Rigging, Sails & Canvas, Cleaning & Detailing, Sailing Instruction), 4 feed posts, 2 upcoming events, 3 RSVPs, 3 reviews. `reset_demo` deletes ONLY `is_demo:true` rows across users/boat_pros/posts/events/event_registrations/reviews — real data/settings/FAQs/testimonials/audit preserved. Admin endpoints: `GET/POST /api/admin/demo/{status,seed,reset}` (require_admin, audit-logged). Admin page `/admin/demo` (`DemoData.jsx`) with status counts + Load/Reset buttons, linked in Header admin menu (hidden from non-admins). Verified full seed→reset cycle via curl (real boat_pros 5→8→5) + UI.
- **Preview Markets** (homepage): `DirectoryPreview` now has a PNW market picker wired to the shared `MarketContext` (syncs with the header selector); the category CTA copy localizes ("Find the best Rigging pros in Bellingham") and `CommunitySample` filters the sample feed to the chosen market (falls back to all if none match).
- **Gate Redirect Return**: `AuthGate` stores the attempted path (sessionStorage `smn_post_auth` + `?next=`); `Login` (email) and `AuthCallback` (Google) send the visitor to that exact page after auth (paid-signup pricing redirects still take precedence).
- **Sample Refresh**: `CommunitySample` rotates its 5-post window per visit via a `localStorage` counter so repeat visitors see fresh posts.
- Demo users are password-less (view-only in directory/feed/events), per user choice. Verified: web compiles clean; admin demo page + homepage picker screenshots pass.

- **RequireAuth gate** (`frontend/src/components/AuthGate.jsx`): member-only web routes now render an in-place gate for logged-out visitors — a branded card with a feature-specific "You need a free account to …" message + **Create free account** / **Log in** CTAs (no real data leaks). Gated routes in `App.js`: /directory, /directory/:id, /clubs, /community, /events, /my-events, /partners/:handle, /crew-bank, /reviews, /following, /my-listing, /u/:handle, /:atHandle, /community-hub/:id, /dashboard, /messages, /profile. Public: /, /login, /register, /pricing, /contact, /faq, /community-guidelines, /onboarding, /digest, payment/verify callbacks (admin routes self-guard).
- **Directory preview** (`DirectoryPreview.jsx`, on Landing): exactly 6 STATIC category cards (Boat Haul Out, Detailing, Diesel Engine Service, Rigging, Sails/Canvas, Sailing Instruction) — stock photo + category name only, no vendor/contact/rating/badge, NOT real listings. Clicking any card opens a CTA overlay ("Find the best [category] pros in your area — create a free account to browse the full directory") with sign-up/log-in.
- **Community sample** (`CommunitySample.jsx`, on Landing): 3–5 recent REAL posts shown read-only (types general/update/note/discussion/volunteer_opportunity; excludes for_sale & sailing opportunities). Like/Comment/Reply are visibly disabled; CTA to create an account. Renders nothing if no posts.
- Added `lock` icon to the web Icon registry. Verified via web compile + guest screenshots (gate on /directory, 6-card preview + CTA overlay, 5 read-only sample posts). Web-only per user; mobile unchanged.

- **SailFlow default**: signed-out home (no port chosen) now shows a fixed default station `https://sailflow.com/spot/480` (`station_for(None)` in `sailflow_stations.py`); market-specific stations still resolve once a market is set.
- **Free Club/Org (account_type "partner", free tier) allotment** — resets on the 1st of each calendar month (`community_router`):
  - Feed posts: 1/month (others get unlimited when paid; non-partner free accounts still fully gated).
  - Events: volunteer-only, 1/month; non-volunteer events still require Community Partner tier.
  - Blog/forum: still fully blocked for free orgs (unchanged).
  - All limits return `402 upgrade_required` with an upgrade message.
- **Crew Bank**: org/partner accounts are blocked (403) from both standalone crew listings (`/crew-bank/standalone`) and joining event crew (`/crew-bank/{event}/crew`).
- **Following**: following remains a paid gate for ALL free accounts incl. orgs. Hid/disabled the Follow button on your OWN org card in the Clubs directory (web `Clubs.jsx` + mobile `clubs.tsx`) → shows "Your organization" instead, fixing the self-follow 400 error. Backend self-follow guard retained.
- **Org signup identity**: for `account_type=="partner"` with an `org_name`, the public display `name`, `handle`, and `/partners/<org-slug>` URL now use the ORGANIZATION name (not the registrant's personal name); person kept as `first_name`/`last_name`. Account `name` stays editable later via `/auth/profile` (`auth_router.register`).
- Verified via curl (sailflow default + market; partner register→org name/handle; feed 1-then-402; volunteer 1-then-402; non-volunteer 402; crew-bank 403) + iter87 regression 15/15 pass + web compile + mobile tsc clean. Not run through testing_agent (user save-credits rule).

- **Testimonial prompt after 5★ review**: posting a NEW 5-star boat-pro review opens an inline testimonial composer modal (prefilled role/market) that posts to `/testimonials` (pending). Web `components/TestimonialModal.jsx` wired in `DirectoryDetail.jsx`; mobile `src/components/TestimonialModal.tsx` wired in `app/directory/[id].tsx`. Only fires on new reviews (not edits) and only at exactly 5 stars.
- **FAQ drag-reorder (web admin only)**: `POST /api/admin/faqs/reorder {ordered_ids}` persists per-category order (audit-logged). Web `/admin/moderation` FAQ tab now groups by category with HTML5 drag-and-drop (drag handle) to reorder within a category. Mobile admin FAQ order left as-is (per user choice).

- **Searchable FAQ** (`backend/routers/content_router.py`): `faqs` collection seeded idempotently (by `seed_key`, preserves admin edits) with 15 Q&As across 5 ordered categories. Public `GET /api/faqs` (active only); admin `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/faqs` (audit-logged). `[Subscription path]`→"Profile → Subscription", `[My Markets path]`→"Profile → Markets you follow".
  - Web: `/faq` page (search + category accordion, highlights matches), footer "Help & FAQ" link; admin "FAQ" tab in `/admin/moderation`.
  - Mobile: `app/faq.tsx` screen (search + accordion), Profile "Help & FAQ" row; admin "FAQ" tab in `app/admin.tsx` (inline edit/hide/delete).
- **Testimonials** (`content_router.py`): `testimonials` collection {quote ≤280, role, market, show_name, display_name="First L.", status pending/approved/hidden}. `GET /api/testimonials` (approved only, public), `POST /api/testimonials` (signed-in → pending), admin `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/testimonials` (approve/hide/delete, audit-logged).
  - Web: `TestimonialsStrip` on signed-out Landing + Pricing (everyone); renders nothing until ≥1 approved; submission form in Profile; admin "Testimonials" tab.
  - Mobile: strip on Pricing screen; submission form in Profile; admin "Testimonials" tab.
- Verified via curl (full submit→approve→public→delete lifecycle; FAQ CRUD + hide + 401 on unauth), web compile clean, mobile tsc clean (no new errors). NOT run through testing_agent (user save-credits rule).
- Zoho re-enable explanation delivered to user (needs ZOHO_ACCOUNTS_URL/CLIENT_ID/CLIENT_SECRET/REFRESH_TOKEN + CAMPAIGNS_LIST_KEY in backend/.env, then re-wire signup to call zoho.sync).

- 2026-10-09: Boat Pro listings brought to full Org/Partner parity + in-place image editor (web + mobile, shared backend). Backend verified via curl (all tiers + idempotency); both clients compile/bundle. Authenticated UI not visually smoke-tested (Expo preview auth-injection quirk) — testing_agent recommended next.
  - Backend (`directory_router.py`, `schemas.py`): new `BoatProListingUpdate` + partial **`PATCH /api/boat-pros/listing`** (mirrors `PATCH /org-listing`). `GET /boat-pros/mine` now enriched with tier caps (`can_logo`, `can_cover`, `max_photos`), normalized `cover_url`/`logo_url`/`cover_original_url`/`logo_original_url` + gallery objects `{url, original}`, `categories_all`, `markets_all`, plus a one-time legacy `photo_url`→`cover_url` migration guarded by `cover_migrated` (idempotent — a cleared cover stays cleared).
  - Tier matrix (image entitlements): **Basic** = name + tier badge only (no logo/cover/gallery) · **Featured** = logo + cover, no gallery · **Premium** = logo + cover + 6-photo gallery. Enforced server-side in PATCH (strips logo/cover when not entitled, clamps gallery to `max_photos`; fixed off-by-one so Featured keeps 0 gallery photos). No captions on Boat Pro photos (per user).
  - `POST /boat-pros` upsert hardened: image fields (`logo_url`/`photo_url`/`photos`) are now only touched when explicitly sent, so a lightweight text save from Profile never wipes a logo/cover/gallery. `BoatProUpsert.photos` default `[]`→`None`.

Links added from `Profile.jsx` (replaced the old `ProPhotosUploader` + Photo-URL field) and `MyListing.jsx` ("Edit card & photos"). `DirectoryDetail.jsx` now renders cover banner + logo + gallery; `Directory.jsx` cards prefer `logo_url`.

Boat Pro business fields) + `compute_summary` summary; (4) review-back on `POST /accept` now requires PAID (403 upgrade_required; plain accept stays open to free); (5) notification copy rewritten (no em dashes) and session links now `/reviews?session={id}`; em dashes removed from review digest + reminder email text. ## R3d-4 — Button label truncation fix (MOBILE ONLY) — 2026-06-14
PROBLEM: On real iPhone (Expo Go, dark), side-by-side button labels showed an ellipsis ("See my re...", "Accept and s...", "Review...", "Just ac..."). ROOT CAUSE in shared Button (src/components/ui.tsx): Text was numberOfLines={1} + fixed height:50 + paddingHorizontal:22, with no stacking fallback; rows using flex:1/1.4 squeezed longer labels.
FIX (shared, not per-screen): new `ButtonRow` component in src/components/ui.tsx. It measures each label's intrinsic width (hidden layer, onLayout — chosen over onTextLayout because onTextLayout does NOT fire on react-native-web and rendered blank) vs the real container width (so it adapts to iOS Dynamic Type) and decides BEFORE showing: fits -> side by side honoring `weight`; does not fit -> stack full width, primary on top. Nothing renders side-by-side-then-reflows (reserved minHeight:50 avoids collapse). `allowStack={false}` (keyboard-pinned form footers) never stacks (height must stay stable) and instead wraps the label to 2 lines. Button now: minHeight:50 (was height), paddingVertical:6, added `numberOfLines` prop, label textAlign center.
STACK ORDER (primary/main action on top, safe/cancel below so the safe one is easiest to reach): reject-review, delete msg/conv, cancel-event, blast, markets/orgcat save, register Next/Create account, testimonial, multiselect Apply, success "See my reviews", review footer submit -> default (last=primary top). reviews Accept and Review-back rows pass stackPrimaryIndex={0} (primary is on the left).
APPLIED TO (all two-button rows): review-compose (success + footer), report/admin-edit/admin-reply/admin-faq-edit footers (allowStack=false), profile/reviews (accept-choice, accept/reject, reject modal), messages/[id] (2 delete modals), (tabs)/messages (delete), (tabs)/profile/index (markets, orgcat), (tabs)/my-events (cancel, blast), register (step2, step3), EventDetailScreen (volunteer Email/Call), MultiSelectPicker (Clear/Apply), TestimonialModal (Not now/Submit).
VERIFIED (Expo web preview): review-compose "back" footer "Accept and send review" wraps to 2 lines, no ellipsis, footer stays one row — 390 light+dark and 360 light. My Events cancel modal renders "Keep it"/"Cancel event" side by side at 390 (fits -> unchanged, confirming "no change where it fits"). ButtonRow measurement confirmed working on web via onLayout. Keyboard scroll-to-focus unchanged (footer height stable in write mode; +~4px only when the back-mode label wraps, within FOOTER_RESERVE=80).
COULD NOT VERIFY without a real phone / live data: success screen "See my reviews" and the Reviews "Needs your response" card (Review back/Just accept, Accept/Reject) — captain_jenn has no incoming pending review; register Back/Next, sign-in (single button, no row — no truncation risk), Messages Cancel/Delete, Profile Cancel/Save stacking under iOS Dynamic Type extremes. On native iOS the measurement uses the real scaled font so stacking adapts automatically.

## R3d-4b — ButtonRow regression fix (register blank circles) — 2026-06-14
REGRESSION: On register step 2, ButtonRow rendered Back/Next as two empty circles and the orange Next was a tiny dead hit-area, blocking sign-up on mobile.
ROOT CAUSE (src/components/ui.tsx): register passed `style={styles.navRow}` and navRow includes `flexDirection:"row"`; that merged into the ButtonRow wrapper, making the wrapper a row whose single visible child (the nested decided `<View flexDirection:row>`) shrank to content width, so the `flex:1` buttons collapsed to ~0 width (circles). Same class of bug for any caller whose style injected flexDirection (my-events confirmRow, MultiSelectPicker footer).
FIX (ui.tsx only): (1) render the visible buttons as DIRECT children of ONE wrapper (removed the nested decided View) so there is no row-in-row collapse; (2) the wrapper now OWNS flexDirection/gap/minHeight via a trailing style object that overrides any caller style; (3) ALWAYS render a safe side-by-side row with full labels (numberOfLines=2) until a VALID measurement (containerW>0 AND every label width>0) proves a button will not fit, then stack full width primary-on-top; never render null/collapsed; (4) decision is now per-button flex-share (stack if any one-line label exceeds its flex share) so it STACKS instead of wrapping when a label does not fit, and leaves one-line-fitting rows unchanged. allowStack=false footer branch unchanged.
VERIFIED (Expo web preview, captain_jenn): register steps 1/2/3 at 360 AND 390, light AND dark, all buttons labeled (no circles); Next (orange) advances step2->step3; Back returns step3->step2. Step2 Back/Next stay side by side at both widths. Step3 Back/Create account: side by side at 390, STACKED (Create account on top, Back below) at 360. My Events cancel modal: "Cancel event"/"Keep it" stack in the narrow modal (destructive on top, safe Keep it below) with no circles. Review "back" footer: Cancel + "Accept and send review" stays one row, wraps to 2 lines, no ellipsis, at 390 and 360.
COULD NOT VERIFY without live data / real phone: review success "See my reviews", Reviews "Needs your response" cards (jenn has no incoming pending review), TestimonialModal, EventDetail volunteer Email/Call, Messages Cancel/Delete (short labels, low risk) - all use the same now-safe ButtonRow (MultiSelectPicker footer injects flexDirection like confirmRow which was verified, so it is covered by the same override). Native iOS Dynamic Type extremes.

## R3d-3 — Long full-screen forms converted to KeyboardFormScreen (MOBILE ONLY) — 2026-06-14
PROBLEM (real iPhone, Expo Go): keyboard covered fields on long forms (sign-up below Email; new post/blog Tags; create/edit event volunteer contact email+phone).
APPROACH: reused the existing KeyboardFormScreen shell + useKbField(id) (onLayout records y in the scroll content, onFocus scrolls that y above the keyboard; footer lifts with the keyboard; header Done button). No new mechanism invented. Each form: <Screen padded=false> + raw <ScrollView> replaced with <KeyboardFormScreen>, the primary submit moved into the `footer` prop, and every TextInput wrapped in <View onLayout={kb.onLayout}> with onFocus={kb.onFocus}.
NESTED-INPUT RULE: useKbField onLayout gives y relative to its PARENT, so each field wrapper must be a DIRECT child of the scroll root. For new-event the date row got onLayout on the row (direct child) and the volunteer email/phone were LIFTED out of their nested volunteer sub-block to be direct children of the scroll root (field order preserved: race, volunteers, email, phone, lead-days, paid). Verified on web preview that focus registers a per-field target (register Email target=240, hub Tags target=472, confirming the registry fires).
CONVERTED (8 files): app/register.tsx (dynamic footer: none on step 1, navRow ButtonRow on steps 2/3; wired reg-name-row, reg-email, reg-pw, reg-extra, reg-boat), app/signin.tsx (no footer, kept Sign in/Google/link inline; wired signin-email, signin-pass), app/onboarding.tsx (onb-handle, onb-bio), app/new-post.tsx (post-title, post-body, post-price), app/hub/new.tsx (hub-title, hub-body, hub-tags), app/new-event.tsx (event-title, event-desc, event-date, event-location, event-vol-email, event-vol-phone), app/(tabs)/profile/boat-pro-listing.tsx (pro-name, pro-handle, pro-contact-email, pro-biz-email, pro-website, pro-phone, pro-other, pro-bio, pro-creds section), app/(tabs)/profile/org-listing.tsx (org-name, org-handle, org-website, org-email, org-phone, org-bio).
NOT DONE: CrewBankScreen ListingForm (availability + about-you inputs) is still an inline <Modal> bottom-sheet. Converting it to KeyboardFormScreen requires extracting it to a standalone full-screen route with event-based reload wiring; deferred to avoid risking the working Crew Bank tab, and it was not in the real-phone failure list. The two CrewBank inputs remain the banned Modal pattern and are the top R3d-3 follow-up.
UNVERIFIED: Expo web preview has NO native keyboard, so only a real iPhone can confirm fields sit above the keyboard. Verified on web: all 8 screens render as KeyboardFormScreen (KB-DEBUG banner shows), footers pinned, and onFocus registers the correct per-field target. KB_DEBUG left ON per instruction.

PROBLEM (real iPhone): the My Events "Cancel this event?" confirmation was an inline MotionModal (fade) with a reason TextInput, preset chips, and a ButtonRow (Keep it / Cancel event) at the bottom of a centered card. The keyboard opened and covered BOTH buttons; the backdrop had no tap-to-dismiss and a Modal has no iOS back gesture (onRequestClose is Android-only), so the only escape was cancelling the event. Trapped.

Reason box uses useKbField, NO autoFocus (verified focused=none on load, keyboard stays closed until tapped). Footer ButtonRow (allowStack=false) keeps Keep it + Cancel event reachable with keyboard open/closed; Keep it is variant "teal" (safe, primary-looking), Cancel event keeps its current wording and default color. Preset reasons (Weather, Low turnout, Rescheduling, Venue issue) and cancel behavior/endpoint unchanged.
CODE PATHS: Back arrow = KeyboardFormScreen showBack -> router.back() (no API call). Keep it = onPress router.back() (no API call). Cancel event = api.del(`/events/{id}?reason=...`) then router.back(); My Events reloads via its useFocusEffect. Verified on web: tapping Keep it returned to My Events with event count unchanged (nothing cancelled).
my-events.tsx: Cancel card button now router.push("/cancel-event", {id, title}); removed the confirm Modal, confirmId/cancelReason state and the old cancelEvent fn (the blast/remind Modal is untouched).

## R4a — Sign-up duplicate-email handling (MOBILE + WEB) — 2026-06-14
REPORT (real responses, temp accounts, deleted after): POST /auth/register already normalized (strip+lower) and returned 409 for a duplicate, case-insensitively and trimmed (verified "X" / "X.UPPER" / "  X  " all -> 409). DB users has a UNIQUE index on email (email_1 unique=True) and ZERO duplicate emails (20 users). WEB register (Login.jsx) and LOGIN already normalize; GOOGLE /auth/session did NOT (email = data["email"], no trim/lower). So the backend rejected duplicates; the real gap was CLIENT UX: mobile only rejected at the final "Create account" step (whole form filled first) and showed it as a generic error, not inline under the email.
BUILD:
- backend/routers/auth_router.py: (1) Google /auth/session now `email = (data.get("email") or "").strip().lower()`; (2) register 409 message -> "An account with this email already exists. Sign in or reset your password."; (3) NEW GET /auth/check-email?email= -> {available, email} (normalizes trim+lower, used by sign-up before advancing).
- mobile app/register.tsx: step 2 "Next" now calls /auth/check-email before advancing; if taken, shows the message INLINE under the email field (testID reg-email-error) with a red border and stays on step 2 (new user is not forced to fill the whole form). Next shows "Checking" while it runs. submit() 409 also routes back to step 2 + inline. emailError clears on edit.
- frontend/src/pages/Login.jsx: on register 409 (e.response.status===409) shows the same message INLINE under the email input (testID reg-email-error, red border) instead of the generic banner; clears on edit / tab switch.
INDEX: unique index on email already exists and there are 0 duplicates, so no index change was needed (and no need to pause for Jenn).
VERIFIED: check-email normalizes (false for existing in any case/spacing, true for fresh); register dup -> 409 new message; mobile step-2 inline error screenshot; web inline error screenshot. All temp accounts deleted (users back to 20, 0 duplicate groups). No protected/real account touched.
UNVERIFIED: nothing material (native keyboard irrelevant here; verified via web preview + API).

## R5b — Mobile photo upload "too large" (Feed/Marketplace/Blog; Forum reported) — 2026-06
REPORT: All 4 composers send a base64 data URL in JSON; backend measures the data-URL LENGTH, limit MAX_PHOTO_URL_CHARS=400_000 (backend/constants.py:79), enforced in community_router.py (/posts, "Photo too large") and hub_router.py:335 (/discussions, "Photo too large"). Limit lives on the SERVER (not phone/proxy). Feed=new-post.tsx(general)->PhotoPicker; Marketplace=new-post.tsx(for_sale)->MultiPhotoPicker; Blog+Forum=hub/new.tsx->PhotoPicker (identical). TODAY the pickers did NO on-device resize (only quality 0.6/0.5) so a 12MP iPhone photo = ~3.2M/2.8M chars -> rejected. Forum "working" was not structural (same code as Blog) — just a smaller/tighter test crop slipping under 400KB. Web already lives under the same 400,000 limit via frontend/src/lib/imageCompress.js (compressImageToDataURL ladder 1600/1200/900 + quality walk), so the server limit is NOT the only control -> did NOT raise it, did NOT need Jenn.
BUILD (mobile only): NEW mobile/src/lib/photo.ts = compressPhotoToDataUrl(uri,w,h) mirroring the web ladder (long-edge caps 1600->1200->900, quality 0.8->0.4, never upscale) returning a JPEG data URL under 400,000 chars (ok:false if none fit). Wired into PhotoPicker.tsx (keeps allowsEditing crop on Feed; quality:1 now, compress on-device; busy spinner; plain "Photo too large" message) and MultiPhotoPicker.tsx (per-photo compress; counts + plain message for any too-large). Backend + web untouched.
EVIDENCE (PIL simulation of the same ladder; exact on-device bytes need a real phone): 12MP src -> TODAY 3,190,551 chars (REJECTED) / NEW picked 1200px q0.45 = 390,127 chars (FITS). Real (smoother) photos usually keep 1600px/higher quality.
UNVERIFIED (device-only): cannot drive the native iOS photo library in the web preview, so before/after on a real iPhone library photo and the exact resulting byte sizes were not captured on-device; Metro bundled clean (1702 modules). No protected accounts or existing posts touched.

KeyboardFormScreen lifts the footer by the FULL keyboard height (marginBottom: lift = kb = 335), and on iOS the keyboard height is measured to the PHYSICAL screen bottom and ALSO spans the tab-bar/home-indicator region. So the footer is pushed up by kb on top of the tab-bar offset -> ~tab-bar-height (~83-100pt) gap. Root-level routes (cancel-event, review-compose, report, and now remind-volunteers) are outside (tabs) so their content bottom IS the physical bottom and show no gap. Location of the duplication: tab-bar reserved height (in app/(tabs)/_layout) + KeyboardFormScreen footer marginBottom:lift. NOT FIXED (report only, per instructions).

## R5i — Event volunteer reminder becomes a group message thread (backend + mobile + web) — 2026-06
REPORT: /events/{id}/blast (community_router) sent each recipient an in-app notify(kind="event_blast", body=message[:240], link="/events?event={id}") plus optional email (event_blast_email_html, replies go to host) and logged an event_blasts row. Group messaging (messaging_router): POST /messages/group creates dm_threads {thread_id "group_"+hex, is_group, title, participants[], created_by, reads{}} + first dm_messages; POST /messages/group/{tid} appends (require_interaction gate -> free users 403 upgrade_required). Reading (GET /messages/{tid}) only checks participant membership (NO paid gate) so free members can read; /messages/{tid}/info returns members via _member_summary = user_id/name/handle/avatar ONLY (no email/phone). Adding members later: no API, but $addToSet participants works; no removal API (only per-user soft delete_for). Free host: create_group calls require_interaction, but the blast endpoint allows any event organizer. Web host sends via components/EventBlast.jsx (audience volunteer = Remind volunteers; also used for all/attendee). Volunteer data: event_registrations kind=volunteer with user_id (accounts only; non-account invitees are not reachable as thread members). Old event_blast notifications remain and still link to the event listing (historical, not migrated).
BUILD (scoped to audience=="volunteer" only; all/attendee blasts unchanged):
- BACKEND community_router.event_message_blast: for volunteer audience, find-or-create ONE group thread per event (dm_threads.event_reminder_event_id). First reminder creates it (host + current volunteers); later reminders $addToSet current volunteers and post into the SAME thread. Message authored by host, body = subject + blank line + message. Notify every participant except host with notify(kind="dm", title=thread title "<event> Volunteers", body=word-safe first-line preview via new _word_safe_preview(), link="/messages?thread={tid}", meta={thread_id,is_group,event_id}). Email kept (unchanged, only when send_email). event_blasts row now stores thread_id; returns {recipients, emailed, thread_id}. CANCELS: members are never removed, so a volunteer who cancels keeps read access + history and still gets later reminders in the thread (we do not eject people from a conversation); only currently-signed-up volunteers are newly added.
- MOBILE: notificationHref dm now returns /messages/{thread_id}?group=1 when meta.thread_id && is_group (opens the thread, not the event). remind-volunteers.tsx success copy updated to "...in their Messages inbox and by email." (keeps success screen + recipient count).
- WEB: Messages.jsx now honors ?thread={id} (auto-opens that thread, incl. groups); NotificationsBell already navigates to n.link (= /messages?thread=). EventBlast.jsx unchanged (same endpoint -> same new behavior).
Free reply rule UNCHANGED (send_group_message require_interaction); RSVP reminders / event rules / who-can-message untouched. KB-DEBUG untouched.
VERIFIED (manual, NO testing agent; TEMP paid host + paid vol + free vol + late vol, deleted after): 26/26 API checks - one thread, title "<event> Volunteers", members host+2 vols; message from host body=subject+blank+message; dm notif with meta.thread_id/is_group and short word-safe preview (63 chars, not mid-word); 2nd reminder same thread (2 msgs); paid vol reply visible to host; free vol can READ (200) but reply blocked 403 upgrade_required; /info has names and NO email; late volunteer added on next reminder; temp cleaned.
