## R3d-3h — About you (multiline, last field) not scrolling above keyboard on crew-listing (mobile) — 2026-06
REPORT: In app/crew-listing.tsx, About you (crew-notes) and Availability (crew-availability) are wired IDENTICALLY via useKbField (own id, onLayout on wrapper View, onFocus on the TextInput); both are direct children of the ScrollView content (not in a nested card), so measured y is correct. About you is a multiline TextInput (minHeight 90, textAlignVertical top). Root cause is in the shared KeyboardFormScreen: scrollToId scrolls to y-16, but max scroll = contentHeight - viewportHeight; with keyboard open the content bottom padding was only 20 + kb + FOOTER_RESERVE(80), which is NOT enough for the LAST field (About you) - its y-16 exceeded max scroll, so the scroll clamped short and the field stayed behind the keyboard. Availability works because About you + padding sit below it, giving room to scroll Availability up.
FIX (KeyboardFormScreen.tsx only): record the ScrollView height (onLayout -> viewH) and, when the keyboard is open, set contentContainerStyle.paddingBottom = 20 + FOOTER_RESERVE + viewH (was 20 + lift + FOOTER_RESERVE). A full viewport of bottom padding guarantees any field - including the last multiline one - can scroll until its top is at the top of the visible area, well above the keyboard; the keyboard scrollTo (y-16) then no longer clamps. Keyboard-closed layout is UNCHANGED (still 20 + FOOTER_RESERVE = 100). No change to useKbField, fields, validation, saving, or the KB-DEBUG banner.
AFFECTED SCREENS (shared component): cancel-event, review-compose, report, remind-volunteers, crew-listing, admin-edit, register (delete-post has no inputs). Behavior preserved: the only difference is MORE scrollable empty space below content while the keyboard is open; fields that already scrolled above the keyboard still do, and the resting layout is identical.
VERIFIED (web preview, TEMP paid account, deleted after): on /crew-listing, focusing Availability -> KB-DEBUG focused=crew-availability target=1030; focusing About you -> focused=crew-notes target=1113 (its own recorded y-16, the largest/last field that previously clamped). This confirms About you is registered with its own id, onLayout recorded its y, onFocus fires, and a distinct scroll target is computed. Expo bundle clean. No em dashes.
UNVERIFIED (stated): the web preview has NO native keyboard, so keyboardWillShow never fires (kbHeight=0) and the keyboard-open path - the new viewport-sized bottom padding + the actual scroll that lifts About you and its cursor above the keyboard - CANNOT be exercised here. User will verify on iPhone. testing_agent was NOT run (user explicitly forbade it; it also cannot drive a native iOS keyboard in the web preview).

## R3d-3h (follow-up) — About you STILL covered on iPhone: layout-settle timing fix (mobile) — 2026-06
REPORT: Prior R3d-3h added viewport-sized bottom padding (20 + FOOTER_RESERVE + viewH when kb>0), which is sufficient to reach the last field. On-device the About you (crew-notes) field was still covered: root cause was TIMING, not padding. The scroll fired from onFocus (kb=0, small padding) and once from a keyboardWillShow setTimeout(60ms); that timer can run BEFORE the two-stage re-layout settles (setKb grows padding -> ScrollView shrinks -> setViewH grows padding again), so the last field's y-16 target was clamped against stale padding. Availability sits higher so a clamped scroll still showed it.
FIX (KeyboardFormScreen.tsx only): added a useEffect keyed on [kb, viewH] that re-scrolls to lastFocused.current whenever kb>0, i.e. AFTER the keyboard-open padding/viewport layout commits. Deterministic; replaces reliance on the fragile 60ms timer (timer kept as harmless same-target reinforcement). No change to useKbField, fields, validation, saving, or the KB-DEBUG banner.

## CL-5c (2026-06): Edited crew-listing markets not saving (WEB) — DIAGNOSED + FIXED
CAUSE (web state race, cause i/v): pages/CrewBank.jsx held ONE shared `form` state for the listing editor, and `loadMine()` re-seeded `form` from the DB entry. `loadMine` is a useCallback([user]) run in useEffect([loadMine]) and also after every save. AuthContext.refresh() does setUser(data) with a NEW object each call (mount, identity switch, etc.), so `user` identity can change at any time -> loadMine re-runs -> it overwrote the open edit buffer with the ORIGINAL DB markets, discarding the user's unsaved dropdown change, so Save posted the old markets. Backend (POST /crew-bank/standalone $sets body.markets correctly, owner-scoped by user_id) and the MultiSelectPicker (openPanel setDraft(value); Apply onChange(draft); CrewBank onChange sets form.markets) are both correct in isolation (proved: a clean edit captured POST body markets:["Tacoma","Olympia"], GET mine returned the same). The bug is intermittent, which is why CL-5b's create-only test missed it and a clean single-session edit also works.
DID CL-5b CAUSE IT? No. CL-5b only swapped chips for the dropdown; the old chip path used the same shared `form` seeded by the same loadMine, so the same race could discard chip edits. CL-5b exposed/retested it, it did not introduce it.
FIX (smallest, web only): removed the form-seeding block from loadMine (it now only setMine) and moved form seeding into openForm (seed from `mine` on edit, or EMPTY+primary on create). Now the edit buffer is populated only when the user opens the form and is never clobbered by an async refetch. Payload/field names unchanged; MultiSelectPicker and backend untouched. Changed file: frontend/src/pages/CrewBank.jsx.
MOBILE (read-only check): app/crew-listing.tsx seeds its form (mkts/positions/etc.) ONCE in useEffect([]) on mount and never reseeds on user/focus change, so its edit buffer cannot be clobbered. Mobile EDIT saves changed markets correctly. Mobile is NOT broken; not changed.
VERIFIED (web preview, TEMP premium account, deleted after): create markets [Greater Seattle, Tacoma]; edit -> [Tacoma, Olympia] -> card shows "Tacoma · Olympia" and GET mine returns ['Tacoma','Olympia']; reload page -> still B,C; reopen edit -> dropdown shows "Tacoma, Olympia" with Tacoma/Olympia ticked and Greater Seattle unticked; edit availability only to "Weekends only" -> markets stayed ['Tacoma','Olympia']. Webpack compiled successfully. No em dashes; no Stripe; seed data untouched.
UNVERIFIED: the intermittent clobber race was not forced deterministically in the browser (it needs an auth refresh mid-edit); the fix removes the only code path that writes form from an async refetch, so the race is structurally eliminated.


## MODAL-FIX (2026-06) — shared Modal focus-steal root fix

## ALLPNW-VIS (2026-06) — null-market items in every market feed + wording
- Backend: `GET /posts` (community_router.py ~62-66) and `GET /discussions` (hub_router.py ~267-271) now match a specific market OR null/missing: single `{"$in":[market,None]}`, multi `{"$in": _mk+[None]}`. This mirrors POST /posts (also no admin special-case). A hypothetical free-tier admin would be blocked, same as today on /posts.

## HUB-KB-SYMBOL (2026-06) — fix iOS keyboard stuck on symbols after "?" on New Forum/Blog (mobile only)
- FINDINGS: hub/new.tsx Title & Content TextInputs set only testID/value/onFocus/onChangeText/placeholder/placeholderTextColor/style (Content adds multiline); neither set keyboardType or autoCapitalize (RN defaults). No remount risk: both are static JSX, no key prop, no inline component in render, no state rebuilding the field; useKbField handlers are stable; inp()/area() only return new style arrays (prop change, not remount).
- GUESS (labeled): with autoCapitalize/keyboardType left implicit, iOS does not reliably re-evaluate the keyboard layer after a "?" and stays on the symbols layer; setting them explicitly nudges iOS to restore the letters layer.

## AUTHOR-EDIT-DELETE (2026-06) — authors can edit/delete their own posts & discussions (backend only)
- FINDINGS (pre-change): (1) PATCH /posts and DELETE /posts were for_sale-ONLY (owner|admin, else 400/403); no edit path for feed/general/sailing. DELETE /discussions ALREADY allowed author|admin but cleaned up comments only; there was NO PATCH /discussions. (2) Admin delete via reports (reports_router moderation_remove / admin_resolve_report delete_target) deletes the doc, cascades child comments for a comment, and closes open reports with target_deleted; the for_sale DELETE additionally cleared reactions + saved_items. Neither cleared notifications. Images are URL-only (no blob store) so deleting the doc removes them. (3) author_id is ALWAYS the posting human's user_id regardless of identity (business/org only changes author_identity/author_business_id/author_name), so authorship = author_id == user_id.
- NEW shared helper deps.purge_content(report_target_type, target_id, actor_id): deletes comments (post_id), reactions (target_id), saved_items (target_id), notifications (meta.post_id), and closes open reports (status resolved + target_deleted + note). Reused by both author-delete paths; admin routes left unchanged.
- POSTS (community_router): edit_post now branches - for_sale keeps owner|admin title/price/condition/photos; feed/general/sailing are AUTHOR-ONLY, edit body + image_url/image_urls only (no title/type/market/author/identity/created_at), stamp edited_at on body change, validate non-empty body <=20000 + photo size. delete_post now branches - for_sale owner|admin; others AUTHOR-ONLY; both call purge_content("post", ...). Added image_url to PostEdit. list_posts returns can_edit/can_delete (author_id==viewer).
- DISCUSSIONS (hub_router): NEW PATCH /discussions AUTHOR-ONLY editing title(<=200)/body(<=20000)/tags/image_url, edited_at on text change; PUBLISHED blog stays published (status untouched, no re-review, no admin notify); pending blog just updates draft. delete_discussion keeps author|admin and now calls purge_content("discussion", ...). Added image_url to DiscussionUpdate. list_discussions + get_discussion return can_edit/can_delete.
- PLAN RULE (item 4): the free-individual create gate (require_interaction) is NOT applied to edit/delete of already-owned content - neither new path calls it; only ownership (author_id) is checked. A free author can edit/delete their own content.
- VERIFIED by curl (4 temp accounts: author, non-author, admin, logged-out; business-identity post; all deleted, 0 remaining):
  EDIT (PATCH) status by caller - feed/general/sailing/forum/blog each: logged_out=401, non_author=403, admin=403, author=200. edited_at set on all. Published blog status stayed "published". Business-identity post edited by its human author -> 200, author_identity still "business", edited_at set.
  DELETE status - feed/general/sailing/forum/blog each: logged_out=401, non_author=403, author=200. Admin delete: posts (feed/general/sailing)=403 (author-only; admin deletes via reports = unchanged), discussions (forum/blog)=200 (pre-existing admin power, unchanged).
  can_edit/can_delete in GET: author=True/True, non_author=False/False, logged_out=False/False (posts list + discussion GET). edited_at returned in responses.
- No frontend/Stripe changes. No existing records modified. No em dashes. No real emails/phones/names printed.

Event crew sign-ups live in db.crew_bank keyed by (event_id,user_id): positions, markets, status, event_title, event_starts_at, event_market. Both tie to owner via user_id; event sign-ups also carry event_id.
- Q2 existing reuse: standalone owner actions via PATCH /crew-bank/standalone/status (seeking/found), POST /crew-bank/standalone/renew, DELETE /crew-bank/standalone, edit via POST /crew-bank/standalone (upsert) through the CrewBank.jsx "Edit my listing" modal. Event sign-up: PATCH /events/{id}/crew/status, DELETE /events/{id}/crew (leave); no separate edit (re-join upserts). The new page reuses these exact endpoints and the browser confirm; standalone Edit links to /crew-bank where the full edit modal already lives (not rebuilt).
- Q3 locations: Profile "My listings" section (data-testid my-listings-section); Events page "My events" link (my-events-link, gated to event posters).
BUILD:

## BP-4 (2026-06) — review delete for author + admin (backend + web + mobile)
STEP 1 findings:
- Reviewer delete TODAY: yes, author-only, via DELETE /reviews/{id} (directory_router). Admin delete TODAY: NO (endpoint was author-only; admins had no review delete). Web/mobile had NO delete UI on review items (web ReviewsSection.jsx, mobile src/components/reviews/ReviewsSection.tsx) - only create/edit via ReviewButton.
- Cleanup TODAY: delete_review removed the doc only - did NOT recompute rating, close reports, remove notifications, or remove a business reply.
- Rating avg/count: computed LIVE on every read via reviews_router.compute_summary() from db.reviews (no cached field), so it auto-updates on delete. Reports CAN target reviews (target_type "review"). Business reply: ABSENT (reviews have no reply field; the only "reply" refs are email reply_to, unrelated).
- web vs mobile: identical - both only showed reviews, neither had delete. Only one-way org reviews (type one_way) are real /reviews/{id} docs; two-way (tw_*) use the mutual-review flow (out of scope).
STEP 2 build (gaps only):
- Backend DELETE /reviews/{id} (directory_router): now author OR admin (is_admin); others 403, anon 401. Reuses purge_content("review", id, actor) to close open reports as target_deleted + drop related notifications. Rating recompute is automatic (live compute_summary).
- Backend GET /reviews/list/{target_id} (reviews_router): added optional auth; each item now carries can_delete = one_way AND (viewer is author OR admin). Two-way items can_delete false. Creation/edit/moderation unchanged.
- Web ReviewsSection.jsx: Delete button (window.confirm "Delete this review? This cannot be undone.") shown when r.can_delete; calls DELETE /reviews/{id} then reloads.
- Mobile src/components/reviews/ReviewsSection.tsx: Delete button (native Alert confirm "Delete this review?"/"This cannot be undone.") shown when r.can_delete; ui Button danger, height 44, full label; calls api.del then reloads.
VERIFIED on backend via curl + compute_summary (temp org + 2 authors + third user + admin; all deleted, 0 remaining): initial avg 3.0/count 2; third-user delete=403, logged-out=401, author delete own=200 (avg->2.0 count->1), report on it closed status=resolved target_deleted=True; admin delete other review=200 (avg->None count->0). Backend loads; mobile ReviewsSection tsc clean; mobile restarted.
UNVERIFIED (needs real iPhone): native Alert confirm, 44pt tap target and button label on the mobile review Delete cannot be verified in the Expo web preview (behind login). Web behavior mirrors the deterministic can_delete flag.

## EMAIL-3 (2026-06) — consistent phone spacing in the 4 welcome templates (layout only)
CAUSE of uneven spacing: the EMAIL-2 step blocks separated heading / paragraph / "Why:" with <br> tags inside one 15px cell whose line-height was 24px, so each <br> produced a ~24px line-height gap. That inside-block gap (24px) was LARGER than the between-block wrapper padding (14px), and Gmail iOS rendered the <br> height inconsistently across blocks (the strong heading was 16px, the cell 15px), so some heading-to-paragraph gaps looked huge and others tight. Also found + fixed a stray EMAIL-2 regex artifact: literal backslashes in the shared-section font-family ("font-family:\'Montserrat\'") which broke the Montserrat font there (fell back to Arial) in all 4 files.
FIX (all 4 templates, backend/email_templates, layout only - no wording/link/color/logo changes):
- Each step block rebuilt from <br>-in-one-cell to a nested table: heading row (padding-bottom 4px), optional paragraph row (padding-bottom 6px), "Why:" row (no bottom padding). Teal left line + tint kept. Content cell padding 0 0 0 16px, vertical-align top. No <br> remain in step blocks.
- Spacing values: heading->paragraph 4px; paragraph->Why 6px; between blocks 20px (wrapper padding-bottom 14px -> 20px). 20 > 6 > 4, so between-block is always clearly larger than inside-block.
- Blocks with no paragraph (e.g. "Complete your profile", "Follow clubs and organizations", "Post volunteer opportunities", "Share updates in the feed"): heading row (4px) then Why row - same structure/look.
- Intro headings (shared section): between-item gap 18px -> 20px; heading->paragraph stays 4px.
- "A quick note on plans": converted from padding divs to a nested table (heading 4px, para 14px, final para) - table-based, no margin-collapse.
- Montserrat restored everywhere (backslash artifact removed).
TEST: rendered all 4; no leftover {{ }}, no em-dash char (remaining <br> is only the sign-off address block, not the steps). Screenshotted boat-pro steps + a 360/390/600 side-by-side iframe comparison - spacing uniform at all three widths, between-block gap clearly larger than inside. Sent ONLY to jenn@sailmorenow.com (status ok): [TEST Boat Owner] 01a1489f-3f7f-7381, [TEST Sailor] 4053-71e8, [TEST Boat Pro] 410c-7826, [TEST Club/Org] 41be-7685.
UNVERIFIED: Gmail, Apple Mail and Outlook client rendering cannot be verified here.
NOT touched: other emails, wording, links, colors, logo, real seed data, DB, testing_agent (forbidden). No real names/emails/phones printed. Temp scripts/preview files deleted.

## WEB-EV-1 (2026-06, web only, layout/spacing only) — MyEvents organizer card fix
Cause: the event <article> was `flex flex-wrap lg:flex-nowrap items-center` with 4 flex children (details flex-1 min-w-0, counts shrink-0, actions shrink-0, and the open roster panel w-full). At >=1024px (lg:flex-nowrap) all 4 were forced onto ONE non-wrapping row; the roster's w-full crushed the details column toward 0 (date wrapped one word per line) while shrink-0 counts/actions overlapped it. Title was CLIPPED (truncate in a ~0-width column), not missing. Pre-existed R5f (both lg:flex-nowrap and the w-full roster child date to the post-fork init commit; R5f only enriched roster row contents).
Fix (frontend/src/pages/MyEvents.jsx only, no wording/data/backend/mobile changes):
- Article is now a block; a wrapper is `lg:flex lg:items-start lg:gap-6` ONLY when the roster is open. Left column holds header + a counts+actions row; roster is the right column.
- Header: badge row, title (truncate, visible), then date + location on one line (date span `whitespace-nowrap` so it never breaks mid-value; location truncates). 
- Counts + action buttons moved into their OWN row below the details: `flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-2`; both groups `flex-wrap` so they wrap instead of overlapping. Every button/testid/behavior kept (Roster, CSV, edit, duplicate, cancel).
- Roster: stacks BELOW details under 1024px (mt-4 pt-4 border-t); at >=1024px sits beside with left `lg:flex-1 lg:min-w-[320px]` (details keeps >=320px) and roster `lg:flex-1 lg:max-w-[460px] lg:min-w-0`.
- Roster rows: `max-w-md` on each <li> so the Message button stays near the name/email; the two groups now stack vertically (`flex flex-col gap-5`) within the narrower roster column.
- Dark mode: all existing dark: classes preserved (borders slate-700, text dark:text-white/slate-100/300).
VERIFIED in preview as a TEMP paid org host (temp event w/ long title+location, 3 volunteers + 1 attendee, session cookie injected): screenshots at 1440px (roster beside, no squeeze/overlap, title visible, date one line, Message near names) and via constrained iframes at 820px and 390px (stacked, title visible/ellipsis, date one line, counts+buttons own row, roster below). Temp data + wrapper deleted (0 residue).
UNVERIFIED: dark mode not screenshotted (classes preserved); the screenshot tool ignores sub-1024 viewport resize, so 820/390 were verified via same-origin iframes rather than true device viewports; native iPad/phone rendering not verifiable here.

## EV-FIX-2 (2026-10-17, web + mobile + backend) — explicit "Free" label; removed "Paid"/"Paid · $price"
NEW FIELD: added `is_free` boolean to the event (EventCreate default false; EventUpdate Optional=None). Default absent/false, so existing events keep working with NO migration. is_free=true means the organizer explicitly chose Free; is_paid=false alone does NOT mean Free (older events predate the flag).
FORM LOGIC (web frontend/src/pages/Events.jsx, mobile app/new-event.tsx): the "Free event" checkbox/toggle still displays from !is_paid (continuity for older events). A transient freeTouched flag tracks real interaction. Checking Free => is_paid=false, is_free=true, touched; unchecking => is_paid=true, is_free=false, touched. is_free is sent ONLY when touched (web: `if (freeTouched) payload.is_free`; mobile: spread `...(freeTouched ? { is_free } : {})`), so editing an older event and saving WITHOUT touching the toggle never sets is_free true and changes nothing else. EMPTY_DRAFT / draftFrom seed is_free from the event (default false) + freeTouched=false.
SERIES: create_event_series uses body.model_dump(exclude repeat/occurrences); EventSeriesCreate extends EventCreate so is_free carries to every occurrence.
BACKEND: create_event stores is_free via model_dump; update_event applies only non-None fields (so omitted is_free = unchanged); reads return the full doc including is_free (absent on older events).
DISPLAY: "Free" badge shows ONLY when is_free === true. Web EventDetailModal now renders a teal "Free" row (data-testid event-free-badge) instead of the old "Paid"/"Paid · $price" row (removed). Mobile events list (app/(tabs)/events/index.tsx) and EventDetailScreen now render `<Badge label="Free" tone="teal" />` gated on is_free instead of the "Paid" badge. No "Paid" label anywhere. RSVP still decoupled from is_paid (unchanged). Filters unchanged.
CHANGED FILES: backend/schemas.py, frontend/src/pages/Events.jsx, frontend/src/components/EventDetailModal.jsx, mobile/app/new-event.tsx, mobile/app/(tabs)/events/index.tsx, mobile/src/screens/EventDetailScreen.tsx. No new/moved files. (create_event_series needed no edit.)
VERIFIED (manual; testing agent NOT used; 3 temp events + temp partner created and DELETED, 0 residue): API returns is_free=true for an explicitly-free event, None for an older is_paid=false event and for a paid event. WEB detail modal shows the "Free" badge on the explicit-free event, NO badge on the older no-flag event, and no "Paid" text anywhere; RSVP button present. Frontend compiles; mobile Metro bundles with no errors (only pre-existing tsc warnings remain).
UNVERIFIED: mobile Free badge not visually captured logged-in (RN-Web auth automation unreliable); verified via code + clean bundle + shared backend contract. Native iPhone layout cannot be verified in the web preview.

## SPACING-0 (2026-10-17) — REPORT ONLY (no code/data changed)
Audited web form spacing per Jenn. NO code or data touched (only this PRD note). Full findings delivered in chat. Key result: the app has ~4 competing input definitions; EditPost.jsx (FIELD=px-3 py-2.5 text-sm + dark-mode border + focus:border, LABEL const, space-y-5, gap-4) is the cleanest and is the recommended shared standard. Worst offender = Create/Edit Event form (Events.jsx): base inputs px-3 py-2 with no text-sm/dark-border/focus, and the Repeats + Occurrences row uses rounded-full py-1 PILLS next to rounded-xl py-2 fields (this is what Jenn saw). Also: Feed marketplace CREATE composer (py-2, no dark mode) does not match EditPost EDIT form (py-2.5); Onboarding inputs carry a unique focus-ring + py-2; Profile FIELD = py-2 with no dark-mode border; Contact/CrewBank = py-2. Recommendation: one shared field/label/select/check-row class (index.css @apply or a constants module) fixes Events/Feed/Onboarding/Profile/Contact/CrewBank at once; the Repeats-row pills need a 2-line dedicated fix. COULD NOT visually verify: the Event form and Profile at laptop/tablet/phone widths (both require auth; event form needs partner/boat_pro/admin) and native datetime-local control height/width (browser-dependent). Awaiting Jenn's approval on what to fix.

## PROFILE-STRING-1 (2026-10-17, web + mobile) — raw base64 avatar printed as text on Profile header
ROOT CAUSE: Jeff McDonald (handle jeffmcdonald) has users.picture=null and users.avatar = a VALID ~248,935-char `data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/4AAQ...` JPEG data URI (the `avatar` field is meant to hold a short EMOJI like the sailboat glyph). Web Profile header did `user.picture ? <Avatar/> : <div>{user.avatar || sailboat}</div>` — so the giant data URI was rendered as raw TEXT in the fallback div, overflowing/overlapping the name+email.
FIX (web): (1) frontend/src/components/Avatar.jsx — the text-fallback branch now only renders `src` as a glyph when it is a short (<=8 char) non-URL string; any longer value shows the default fallback, so raw data can NEVER be printed as text again for any usage of Avatar (feed, comments, messages, directory cards, crew listings, public profile all share this component). (2) frontend/src/pages/Profile.jsx — personal header now always routes through `<Avatar src={user.picture || user.avatar} alt={user.name} fallback={emoji-if-short-else-sailboat} onError->fallback(built in)/>`, so a data-URI avatar renders as an IMAGE (Jeff's photo now shows) and a missing/invalid/dead one falls back to the emoji/default. Header name h1 got `break-words`, email got `break-all`, wrapper got `min-w-0` so no long string can push/overlap layout. (3) frontend/src/components/MentionTextarea.jsx — replaced raw `<img src={u.avatar} alt="">` with the shared `<Avatar>` (alt=name, initials fallback, onError built in).
FIX (mobile): the reported overflow bug does NOT exist on mobile — mobile Profile header (mobile/app/(tabs)/profile/index.tsx:431) and the shared Avatar (mobile/src/components/ui.tsx) render via RN `<Image>`, which cannot print a URI as text and is size-constrained. Still hardened ui.tsx Avatar: it now only hands `resolved` to <Image> when it looks like a URL/path/data URI (regex), else shows initials — so an emoji/invalid avatar falls back to initials instead of a blank image circle.
DATA REPORT: Jeff's stored avatar is a VALID JPEG image (correct `data:image/jpeg;base64,` prefix + JFIF `/9j/4AAQ` magic), just oversized; it only "failed" because it was placed in the emoji `avatar` slot and printed as text, not loaded as an image. After the fix it displays correctly as his photo. Count of users whose `avatar` holds a data:/long (>100 char) value instead of an emoji: 3 (count only, no names/emails). No user has a data: URI in `picture`. I did NOT modify or delete Jeff's record (confirmed still picture=null, avatar len 248935). RECOMMENDATION (needs your approval): migrate those 3 oversized `avatar` data-URIs into real object-storage `/api/files/...` uploads and set `picture`, then clear the base64 from `avatar` (keeps payloads small); until then they now render fine via the new image path.
SENSITIVITY: the string is image data only (standard JPEG/JFIF). It is NOT a token, password hash, session id, or private key.
VERIFIED (manual; testing agent NOT used): minted a temporary READ-ONLY session for Jeff, screenshotted /profile at 1440px and 390px — avatar now shows his photo, header name/email clean, automated check `raw_base64_text_present=False`. Temp session deleted; Jeff's record untouched. Web JSX compiles; mobile ui.tsx tsc clean; mobile service restarted.
UNVERIFIED: mobile profile header not visually captured logged-in as Jeff (verified via code + clean bundle + the fact RN Image can't leak text); native iPhone rendering cannot be verified in the web preview.

## AVATAR-WRITE-0 (2026-10-17) — REPORT ONLY (no code/data changed)
Follow-up audit to PROFILE-STRING-1. Findings (full report in chat): Write paths today: all UI photo UPLOADS go to `picture` (web ProfilePhotoEditor.confirmCrop + Profile.onPhotoFile + mobile -> POST /auth/avatar/storage_router -> stores /api/files path in picture); emoji picks -> PATCH /profile/photo (identity_router, avatar capped [:40], picture=null); legacy /profile/photo picture arg stores a data: URI into picture (<=400k); registration writes picture=None/avatar=None; Google sign-in writes picture=Google URL only, never avatar. TWO current BACKEND paths can still put a base64 data URI into `avatar` with NO data:/emoji guard: directory_router.py:510-514 (PATCH/PUT /account/personal) and auth_router.py:621 (PATCH /auth/onboarding) - both allow up to MAX_PHOTO_URL_CHARS=400000. BUT no current UI photo-upload flow sends avatar as a data URI, so a tester uploading a photo tomorrow would NOT hit them through the app (only a crafted API call / old onboarding client could). Password reset + login do NOT read or write avatar/picture (reset only sets password_hash, marks token used, clears login_attempts + user_sessions) - the reset Jenn did only invalidated sessions, forcing Jeff to re-login, which is when the pre-existing broken avatar became visible (timing coincidental). DATA (read only): 3 users with data-URI avatar: raejen (Google-only, no password_hash, picture=valid Google URL, created 2026-07-08), captain_jenn (has password_hash, picture=valid Google URL, created 2026-07-22 - PROTECTED SEED), jeffmcdonald (has password_hash, picture=null, created 2026-09-11). No updated_at/last_login fields stored so the avatar-write date is unknowable; google_id/auth_provider fields don't exist so Google-only detection relies on password_hash absence. RECOMMENDATION (not applied): (1) guard the two unguarded avatar writes so `avatar` can only be a short preset key (reject values starting with data:/http, cap ~40 chars) - 2 small edits; uploads already go to picture as a stored file. (2) One-off migration (run on temp verify, exclude PROTECTED captain_jenn unless Jenn approves): decode avatar data URI -> put_object -> set picture if empty (jeffmcdonald) -> set avatar=None to drop the ~250KB blob; raejen already has a valid Google picture so just clear avatar. Nothing modified. Awaiting Jenn's approval for AVATAR-WRITE-1 (guard) + AVATAR-MIGRATE-1.

## ANDROID-NAV-0/1 (2026-10-21, MOBILE ONLY) — Android bottom tab labels clipped/missing
ROOT CAUSE: app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx used a hard-coded `tabBarStyle: { height: 84, paddingTop: 6 }` with NO paddingBottom and NO useSafeAreaInsets. On iOS 84px happens to clear the home indicator; on Android (Expo SDK 57 edge-to-edge, 3-button or gesture nav) the system nav-bar inset is not reserved, so the label row is pushed under/behind the nav bar and clipped. Labels also had no lineHeight / single-line / font-scaling cap, so large system fonts or the long "Pros/Orgs" label could grow out of the slot.
FIX (app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx): added useSafeAreaInsets; tab bar height = TAB_BASE(52) + insets.bottom with paddingBottom = insets.bottom (total never hard-coded) so the bar background extends under the system nav and labels always sit above it on both nav modes; replaced tabBarLabelStyle with a custom tabBarLabel <Text numberOfLines={1} maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.2} fontSize 11 lineHeight 14 width:100% textAlign center> so labels stay single line, cap font scaling at 1.2, and each tab's equal flex share prevents "Pros/Orgs"/"Messages" clipping. iPhone keeps the icon-over-label look (52+~34 ≈ prior 84).
BONUS (same pass): (1) app/(tabs)/directory/index.tsx Boat Pro card specialty/category meta Text -> added flexShrink:1 (+ existing numberOfLines=1) so long specialties like "Bottom Paint & Haul-out" truncate inside the card instead of running past the right edge. (2) src/screens/CrewBankScreen.tsx standalone crew row name Text -> added flexShrink:1 so a long name ("Andrew Haliburton...") ellipsizes and no longer overlaps the verified badge or the Message button (badge/icons keep their space; Message button is a separate flex sibling).
FILES CHANGED: app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx, app/(tabs)/directory/index.tsx, src/screens/CrewBankScreen.tsx. Web app NOT touched.
VERIFIED: Metro bundled cleanly (1509 modules, no errors); mobile service restarted. COULD NOT VERIFY on a real Android device/emulator (none available here); the Expo WEB preview reports insets.bottom=0 so it cannot reproduce Android 3-button/gesture nav insets at all. Pre-existing TS "ColorValue not assignable to string" warnings on the unchanged tabBarIcon lines remain (Icon color prop typing; Metro transpiles without typechecking, app runs). Native iPhone cannot be verified in the web preview either.

## PARTNER-ACCESS-0 (2026-10-21, backend) — "Community Partner blocked like free" investigation + fix
FINDING: NOT an entitlement bug. Backend + both clients ALREADY share one entitlement definition and treat community_partner and founding_partner IDENTICALLY. All access gates (POST /events line 712, POST /posts line 162, volunteer/event limits, messaging) key on is_paid_subscriber(u) -> deps.can_interact() -> subscription_tier != "free", which includes BOTH partner tiers. Web + mobile use the shared frontend/src/lib/plan.js canInteract() (same != "free"). The ONLY code keyed specifically on "founding_partner" is deps.partner_badge() (cosmetic badge choice) + the /founding-partners honor-roll list + directory sort; none gate event/post access. Admin sets status via ONE endpoint POST /admin/org-status -> _set_org_status -> writes subscription_tier=status, subscription_expires=None (correct for both). PROVEN empirically: created 3 temp partner orgs (free / community_partner / founding_partner), POST /api/events one-time non-volunteer event -> free=402 plan message, community_partner=200, founding_partner=200 (identical). 
ROOT OF THE REPORTED SYMPTOM: "Too many events posted. Try again in an hour" is the GLOBAL per-IP RATE LIMIT (deps.rate_limit, bucket "event_post", 10/hour, HTTP 429) at community_router.py:698 (/events) and :906 (/events/series). It applies to EVERY account regardless of tier. It is NOT a plan/tier limit. During QA, posting >10 events/hour from one IP triggered the 429; switching to Founding Partner "fixing it" was coincidental (the hourly window reset). The wording read like a quota/plan cap, which is why it looked like Community Partners were capped like free orgs.
FIX (backend only, no entitlement behavior change because none was wrong): (1) deps.py - added explicit ENTITLED_ORG_STATUSES = frozenset({"community_partner","founding_partner"}) as a documented single definition so a future tier change can't split the two partner tiers. (2) Reworded the two rate-limit messages (community_router.py:698 + :906) to clearly state it is a temporary hourly limit that applies to every account, NOT a plan or partner limit (no em dashes). Plan-limit (402) messages already say why and were left as-is (they do not reuse the rate-limit wording).
FILES CHANGED: backend/deps.py, backend/routers/community_router.py. Web/mobile NOT changed (their canInteract already correct).
VERIFIED (manual; testing agent NOT used): empirical 3-tier POST /events test (above); after the edit, backend healthy (root 200), community_partner still 200, free still 402 plan message, no backend log errors. All temp orgs/events/sessions DELETED (0 residue). No seed data touched.
COULD NOT VERIFY: I could not reproduce the exact in-app sequence the reporter used (admin UI status flip then post as that org) end-to-end through the browser; the DB+API-level proof shows the entitlement is tier-agnostic for both partner tiers, so a reproducible tier block does not exist in current code. If the reporter still sees a block as a confirmed Community Partner (subscription_tier == "community_partner"), capture the exact HTTP status + message so we can tell a 429 rate limit apart from a 402 plan message.

## PARTNER-ACCESS-1 (2026-10-21) — deeper cap audit: community vs founding parity (REPORT, no entitlement change needed)
EXPANDED on PARTNER-ACCESS-0 per Jenn (said it was a plan/monthly cap, not the hourly rate limit, and community was blocked like free). COMPLETE CAP INVENTORY (backend, community_router.py): (a) POST /posts: free partner -> 1 feed post/calendar month (402 "Free organizations get 1 feed post a month. Become a Community Partner for unlimited posts."), keyed on is_paid_subscriber->can_interact->subscription_tier!="free"; paid orgs unlimited. (b) POST /events: free partner non-volunteer -> 402 "Free organizations can post volunteer opportunities only. Become a Community Partner to post events."; free partner volunteer capped 1/calendar month -> 402 "Free organizations get 1 volunteer event a month. Become a Community Partner to post more." (counts db.events by organizer_id+is_volunteer+created_at>=month_start); paid orgs skip the whole block. (c) POST /events/series: only the hourly rate limit. No monthly/total cap exists for PAID orgs anywhere. No separate org-tier/plan field: admin /admin/org-status + /admin/community-partner/activate both write subscription_tier (+subscription_expires=None) on the USER doc via _set_org_status, which is the SAME field every cap reads; current_user_from_token re-reads the user fresh each request (no cached snapshot); lazy-downgrade only triggers when subscription_expires is in the past (partners have None).
EMPIRICAL MATRIX (temp orgs, deleted after): FREE (preseeded 1 volunteer this month): non-volunteer->402 volunteer-only; 2nd volunteer->402 1/month. COMMUNITY_PARTNER (preseeded 2 events): 3rd non-volunteer->200, volunteer->200. FOUNDING_PARTNER (preseeded 2 events): 3rd non-volunteer->200. ADMIN-FLIP/order test (Q5): org free with 1 volunteer event already posted -> non-volunteer blocked 402; admin POST /admin/org-status status=community_partner -> DB subscription_tier=community_partner -> same org immediately posts non-volunteer -> 200 (prior events do NOT count; upgrade lifts the cap instantly, order does not matter).
CONCLUSION: community_partner and founding_partner ALREADY have IDENTICAL limits everywhere (both unlimited); the only caps hit FREE orgs. I could NOT reproduce any backend cap that blocks a community_partner. The alpha symptom was almost certainly pre-unification logic (deps now routes ALL entitlement through the single can_interact()/PAID_TIERS, with community_partner+founding_partner both included) that has since been consolidated and already fixed. Nothing to change for parity; kept ENTITLED_ORG_STATUSES from PARTNER-ACCESS-0. Plan messages already say why + offer the next step (become a Community Partner) and do not reuse the hourly wording, so left as-is.
Q4 (client vs server): web gates WHO sees the post form by account_type (partner/boat_pro/admin), not tier; the cap is server-side only; web api.js interceptor handles 402 AND 403 upgrade_required. NOTICED (adjacent, NOT a parity bug, NOT fixed per scope): mobile app/new-event.tsx:128 only calls handleUpgrade when e.status===403, but the event plan caps return 402 -> a FREE org on mobile sees a generic "Couldn't create event" alert instead of the upgrade prompt (handleUpgrade itself already accepts 402||403). Recommend a 1-line follow-up: drop the ===403 restriction and branch on e.code==="upgrade_required". Does not affect community/founding (they never hit 402).
FILES CHANGED: none (report only; PARTNER-ACCESS-0's deps.py ENTITLED_ORG_STATUSES retained). COULD NOT VERIFY: Jenn's exact alpha-era org/state (pre-fork, git squashed) so I cannot confirm the original trigger; if a confirmed current community_partner still sees a block, capture the exact HTTP status+message (402 plan vs 429 hourly) and the org's stored subscription_tier.

## EVENTS-SRC-0 (2026-10-21) — REPORT ONLY (no code/data changed; no events stored)
Auto event aggregation: backend/aggregator.py run_aggregator() fetches 3 sources and upserts into events (source="aggregator"), deterministic id agg_{source_key}_{sha1(source_key,UID|title,starts_at)} (idempotent upsert = built-in dedupe); past events skipped (end < now-1d). Triggered by scheduler.py _weekly_job (DAILY 03:00 PT via APScheduler CronTrigger, name is misleading) which also purges past aggregator events + runs category-review alert; started at server.py:202 start_scheduler() on startup. Also a MANUAL admin button POST /api/admin/events/aggregate (+ GET refresh, DELETE purge). SOURCES: (1) sarc-48north 48N Seattle Area Racing iCal (curl, Cloudflare); (2) ocsa-sailpdx = OCSA/SailPDX, Google public iCal https://calendar.google.com/calendar/ical/admin%40ocsaracebook.org/public/basic.ics, default market Portland/Vancouver; (3) owsa HTML scraper. So SailPDX/OCSA IS already a configured source (and was at last run).
STATUS: last recorded run app_meta.aggregator_last_run = 2026-10-18T05:38:53Z, all 3 ok (48north fetched 10, ocsa-sailpdx fetched 2 ins1/upd1, owsa fetched 10, 0 errors). DB now has 14 aggregator events (ocsa-sailpdx 3, owsa 10, sarc-48north 1). Scheduler currently alive (backend restarted 18:24Z today; next _weekly_job = 2026-10-22 03:00 PT).
WHY IT LOOKS STOPPED (evidence): (A) SOURCE DATA IS DRY: read-only fetch of the OCSA basic.ics = HTTP 200, valid VCALENDAR "OCSA Events/Race Calendar", 102 VEVENTs but 0 FUTURE (latest 2026-10-03; today 10-21); 48north feed = 1 event, 1 future (10-23). The 2026 racing season has wound down and the source calendars have not posted the next season, so the aggregator imports ~nothing even when it runs. This is a source-data condition, not an app bug. (B) SCHEDULER RELIABILITY (partly a GUESS for production): APScheduler uses an in-memory jobstore + misfire_grace_time=1h, daily 03:00 PT, runs only while the backend process is alive; a restart reschedules only the NEXT occurrence and skips a missed one (no catch-up). app_meta shows a gap Oct 19-21 (no scheduled run recorded); the Oct 18 05:38Z run time does not match the 03:00 PT cron, so it was likely a MANUAL admin press. In preview/prod, if the process sleeps/restarts across 03:00 PT, that day's auto-run is silently skipped.
READ RELIABILITY (STEP4): sailpdx.org/events-calendar/racing-schedule/ = HTTP 200 but a JS/iframe Google Calendar embed (src=admin@ocsaracebook.org); no inline events, no ICS link in page text, so fetching the PAGE cannot see events. The Google public iCal export CAN be fetched reliably (HTTP 200, no login) and is exactly what the aggregator uses; it just has no upcoming events right now.
DEDUPE/SOURCE LABEL: already handled (upsert by deterministic id; events carry source_key/source_label/organizer_name=label). No new source needed.
RECOMMEND (NOT built): (1) Nothing to add for SailPDX; it is already wired and readable. (2) Reliability hardening so scheduled runs are not skipped: run the aggregator on startup when last_run > 24h old, and/or widen misfire grace / use a persistent jobstore. (3) Optionally surface a "Source: ..." line in the event UI from source_label if not already shown. (4) Confirm with Jenn whether OCSA/48north will repost the next season; until the feeds carry future events there is nothing to import.
ROBOTS/TOS (STEP6): sailpdx.org/robots.txt allows the page (only /wp-admin/ disallowed). calendar.google.com/robots.txt is "Allow: /$, Disallow: /" (disallows path crawling) but a PUBLIC iCal subscription is the calendar's intended public-sharing mechanism, not web crawling. COULD NOT VERIFY: OCSA/Google ToS for automated polling frequency (keep it to once daily, identify politely), and I could NOT verify the PRODUCTION deployment's scheduler uptime or its own app_meta/last-run (separate environment+DB from this preview pod) - that is the key unknown for whether prod auto-updates actually stopped. No code/data changed; test reads went to /tmp only; no events stored.

## PARTNER-ACCESS-2 (2026-10-21, MOBILE ONLY) — event create upgrade prompt not shown on 402
FIX: mobile/app/new-event.tsx catch block was gated `e.status === 403 && e.code === "upgrade_required"` before calling handleUpgrade, but the event plan caps return 402 -> a free org saw the generic "Couldn't create event" alert instead of the upgrade prompt. Changed to `if (handleUpgrade(e, toast)) {...}` (handleUpgrade already accepts 402 OR 403 with code upgrade_required and shows the prompt + routes to /pricing). The event_posting_closed branch and the generic Alert fallback are kept; the 429 hourly rate limit (no upgrade_required code) still falls through to the Alert showing its own "temporary hourly limit" message.
AUDIT of all mobile handleUpgrade callers / create-post flows (examples checked: new feed post, comments, reactions, RSVP, messaging, marketplace listing, crew bank listing, review, hub, verification): ONLY new-event.tsx had the status-gated bug. Already-correct (left alone): app/(tabs)/feed/index.tsx:188 and src/components/Reactions.tsx:34 (both explicitly handle 402||403 + upgrade_required); app/new-post.tsx:98 (shows its own correct "Post limit reached / Go Premium" alert on 402, lets 429 fall through); profile/index.tsx, hub/new.tsx, messages/new.tsx, messages/[id].tsx, PublicProfileScreen, PostDetailScreen, HubDetailScreen, InlineComments (all pass e straight to handleUpgrade, so handle both statuses). ReviewButton uses an API eligibility reason_code (not an error catch). crew-signup.tsx + EventDetailScreen RSVP catches are plain toast.error(message) with no status gating and EventDetail pre-gates crew join with a proper "Premium feature / See plans" Alert, so they show a prompt/message correctly.
FILES CHANGED: mobile/app/new-event.tsx (only). COULD NOT VERIFY on a phone: verified another way via tsc (clean for new-event.tsx), a clean Metro web bundle, and by reading handleUpgrade (returns true only for ApiError 402||403 + code upgrade_required, false otherwise so 429 keeps its own message). Native iPhone/Android behavior not testable in the web preview. SEPARATE OBSERVATION (not this bug, not fixed): the Crew Bank join/list-yourself 402s in backend (community_router.py ~1611/1995) send a plain string detail with NO code "upgrade_required", so handleUpgrade can never catch them on any client - mobile relies on the EventDetail pre-gate + the server message string instead; worth a later backend tidy to attach the upgrade_required code.

## EVENTS-SRC-1 (2026-10-21) — recurrence expansion + startup catch-up + admin Event Sources panel
PART A (root cause): the OCSA public ICS DOES have future events; my EVENTS-SRC-0 "0 future" was a parse error (I skipped all-day date-only VEVENTs). Real feed: 102 VEVENTs, 24 with RRULE incl. "Winter Sailing on Sundays (WSoS)/PYC" (weekly SU, 2026-09-27 UNTIL 2027-03-14) and single future "OCSA - Tropical Party" 2026-11-06; entries through 2027. aggregator.py ICalSource only read each VEVENT's own DTSTART and skipped end<now-1d, so recurring series with a past first DTSTART were dropped entirely. FIX: expand RRULE/EXDATE/RECURRENCE-ID for the next 12 months using the maintained `recurring-ical-events` lib (3.8.2, built on icalendar 7.2.2; added to requirements with x-wr-timezone). Dedupe id per occurrence stays agg_{source_key}_{sha1(key,UID,occurrence_start)}. Verified: expansion yields 117 future occurrences (21 winter-Sunday + Nov 6 party). The single calendar is sufficient; no 2nd ICS needed.
PART B (catch-up + logging): server.py startup now seeds the source registry then, if app_meta.aggregator_last_run is missing/>24h, runs the aggregator ONCE in a background task (asyncio.create_task, non-blocking) guarded by an app_meta aggregator_lock (15-min stale window) so it never overlaps the 03:00 PT job. run_aggregator now takes trigger (scheduled|startup_catchup|manual|test) + only_key, writes per-source stats back to event_sources, and appends a full run log to aggregator_runs (trigger, started_at, ended_at, per-source results). Verified: startup catch-up ran and imported OCSA 115 fetched / 113 inserted.
PART C (admin panel): new web page /admin/event-sources (frontend/src/pages/AdminEventSources.jsx) + route in App.js + "Event sources" link in Header.jsx admin menu. Table: name/url, type (ICS feed | custom scraper), market, enabled, last run, result (ok/error text), found/added/updated/skipped, added-by + when. 3 existing sources seeded from code via backend/event_sources.py DEFAULT_SOURCES (OWSA read_only=custom scraper). Add form (ICS https only) with default market (10 PNW markets), optional organizer + source label; Google embed/share detection -> shows converted public ICS address and only uses it on confirm (never silent). "Test this source" fetches + expands and shows future count, date range, next 5 (stores nothing). Enable/disable, edit name/market/label, Run now per source, delete only when never imported (read_only + imported sources are disable-only; disabling keeps already-imported events). Nightly job + startup catch-up now read sources from event_sources (aggregator._load_sources), not hard-coded lists. SAFETY (backend/event_sources.py): admin-only; https only; blocks localhost/.local/.internal + private/loopback/link-local/reserved IPs (resolves every A/AAAA, re-checks each of <=3 redirect hops); only ports 80/443; 10s timeout; 5 MB cap; polite User-Agent. Verified SSRF blocks localhost + http.
SOURCE LABEL (Part C.8): web EventRow.jsx ALREADY shows a "via <label>" chip for aggregator events (left as-is). Mobile EventDetailScreen.tsx did NOT show it; added an InfoRow "Source: <source_label>" for source==aggregator in the same secondary style.
FILES CHANGED: backend/aggregator.py, backend/event_sources.py (new), backend/routers/event_sources_router.py (new), backend/routers/community_router.py (SOURCES import + /admin/events/sources now DB-driven), backend/scheduler.py (trigger="scheduled"), backend/server.py (router include + seed + catch-up), backend/requirements.txt (recurring-ical-events, x-wr-timezone), frontend/src/pages/AdminEventSources.jsx (new), frontend/src/App.js, frontend/src/components/Header.jsx, mobile/src/screens/EventDetailScreen.tsx.
TESTED (temp data only, deleted): temp admin session for admin endpoints (list/convert/test/SSRF), screenshots of the panel + add-modal conversion; temp admin + session DELETED. The 113 OCSA events imported by the startup catch-up are REAL aggregated events (the feature working) and were kept. No seed data touched, no em dashes.
COULD NOT VERIFY: mobile "Source:" line not visually captured logged-in (verified via tsc clean + code); native iPhone cannot be verified in web preview; production scheduler/catch-up behavior is a separate environment; OCSA/Google ToS for automated polling (kept to daily + polite UA). The run-now per-source bypasses the daily guard by design; the nightly 03:00 PT job dedupe is idempotent so overlap with catch-up only updates.

## MKT-MOBILE-0 (2026-10-21, MOBILE ONLY) — easier marketplace posting + photo cards + instant refresh
PART A findings: (1) Mobile marketplace listings are created on app/new-post.tsx by choosing the "For sale" type pill (the only entry was the Feed compose FAB -> "New post" -> pick For sale, which testers missed). Web exposes an explicit "List an item"/marketplace action. (2) The mobile Marketplace IS the `for_sale` filter tab inside app/(tabs)/feed/index.tsx, rendered by the in-file HubCard. The first photo (item.image_url, plus image_urls[]) is ALREADY returned by GET /posts?type=for_sale - NO backend change needed. But for_sale cards never showed it: the card image only rendered when `expanded`, and for_sale cards are not expandable, so testers had to tap in. (3) Feed top actions = a compose FAB (bottom-right) opening a sheet (New post/forum/blog/event) + the filter segmented control at the header.
PART B built (all mobile): (1) Added "Add marketplace listing" in TWO places: a new option in the Feed compose sheet (go /new-post?type=for_sale) and a dedicated accent button at the top of the Marketplace (for_sale) view. Both reuse new-post (no 2nd screen); new-post.tsx now reads a `type` param to preselect For sale. Shared Icon + Pressable, 44px min touch target, light/dark via theme colors. (2) Old Post -> For sale pill path untouched and still works. (3) HubCard now renders a square (aspectRatio 1) photo for for_sale cards with an image-outline placeholder when none; fixed ratio = no layout shift, expo-image with cachePolicy memory-disk + transition for lazy/smooth load; title keeps numberOfLines=2 + flexShrink, price numberOfLines=1 so long titles/prices do not clip on narrow Android. (4) Free accounts: both new actions pre-gate with isFreeIndividual(user) -> promptCommentUpgrade(toast, ...) (the same standard upgrade prompt the sibling compose options use), so no dead button and no generic error. [Interpretation note: handleUpgrade needs an API 402/403; for a proactive button the established mobile pattern is isFreeIndividual + promptCommentUpgrade, which shows the same upgrade prompt - used here.] (5) INSTANT REFRESH root cause: the Feed only refetched via useEffect([load]) (deps: filter/market/filters) and pull-to-refresh; it had NO focus refetch and NO listener for the MARKETPLACE_CHANGED event that new-post already emits on for_sale create, so a new listing never showed until a manual refresh. ## MOBILE-KB-1 (2026-10-27, MOBILE ONLY) — fix large empty gap between a bottom input bar and the keyboard (post-SDK58)
SYMPTOM: iPhone/Expo Go SDK 58, after the 57->58 / RN 0.88 upgrade, the comment bar on Post detail (and other bottom-bar screens) floated ~150-200px above the keyboard when it opened.
ROOT CAUSE (not a magic number): every bottom-bar screen sits inside our custom `<Screen>` chrome, which renders its header (and, inside a tab navigator, the tab bar) INSIDE the React tree. Since RN 0.88, KeyboardAvoidingView measures its frame in WINDOW coordinates, so the header and tab bar are ALREADY reflected in that frame. The old `keyboardVerticalOffset = insets.top + 52 + (inTab ? tabBarH : 0)` (added during R3D-3H for RN 0.86, which used relative frames) is now entirely DOUBLE-COUNTED, so KAV over-pads by ~(header + tabBar) = the observed gap. With this chrome the correct offset is 0. (Confirmed by walking the KAV padding formula: final = frameBottom - keyboardTop + offset; with window-coord frames the flush result needs offset 0.) react-native-keyboard-controller was NOT used because it is not in Expo Go and would break Jenn's testing.
FIX (one shared place): new `src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx` (KeyboardAvoidingView, behavior padding on iOS / height on Android, keyboardVerticalOffset=0) replaces the hand-rolled KAV on every bottom-bar screen; new `src/lib/useBottomBarPadding.ts` hook returns the input bar's paddingBottom (inside a tab: 10; outside a tab: insets.bottom+10 when keyboard closed, 10 when open via Keyboard show/hide listeners, so a non-tab bar sits flush on the keyboard instead of leaving a safe-area gap). Removed the now-dead useTabBarHeight/inTab/tabBarH/insets locals from the two screens (useTabBarHeight.ts left in place, now unused/harmless).
SCREENS COVERED (all 4 that used KeyboardAvoidingView + a pinned bottom bar): src/screens/PostDetailScreen.tsx (Hub post/marketplace comments), src/screens/HubDetailScreen.tsx (forum thread replies), app/messages/[id].tsx (direct/Crew Bank/Marketplace message thread), app/messages/new.tsx (compose). Closed-keyboard behaviour unchanged (bar still above tab bar / safe area). NOT in scope / left as-is: src/components/InlineComments.tsx (feed inline comments - inline, not a pinned bar, no KAV) and the review screens (REVIEWS-3 rebuilds them).
VERIFIED: expo bundle builds (iOS 200) on runtime exposdk:58.0.0; app boots with no crash on the web preview; `tsc` shows ZERO new errors from this change (only the pre-existing EXPO-SDK-1 strict-TS backlog remains). Native keyboard motion CANNOT be reproduced on the web preview.
JENN SHOULD CHECK ON-DEVICE (Expo Go SDK 58, link exp://marina-connect-11.expo.preview.emergentagent.com): open the keyboard on each and confirm the input bar sits flush on top of the keyboard (incl. with the iOS predictive/suggestions row), and that with the keyboard closed the bar still sits above the tab bar / home indicator: (1) a Hub post / Marketplace item -> "Add a comment...", (2) a forum thread reply, (3) a direct message thread (incl. a group thread), (4) New message compose, (5) a Crew Bank / Marketplace message thread. Also sanity-check Android if a tester is available (behavior=height + edge-to-edge adjustResize).
FILES CHANGED: NEW src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx, NEW src/lib/useBottomBarPadding.ts; edited src/screens/PostDetailScreen.tsx, src/screens/HubDetailScreen.tsx, app/messages/[id].tsx, app/messages/new.tsx. No seed data touched; no temp accounts needed; no em dashes.


## MOBILE-KB-2 (2026-10-27, MOBILE ONLY) — measurement-based keyboard lift (MOBILE-KB-1 failed on device)
ON-DEVICE RESULT of KB-1: keyboardVerticalOffset=0 left the comment bar HIDDEN behind the keyboard on the FlatList screens (Post/Marketplace comments, forum replies, DM thread); it only worked on New message and Crew Bank edit. The offset=0 theory was never device-verified.
WORKING vs FAILING (evidence from code): New message (works) = KeyboardAvoidingView whose SINGLE direct child is a bounded `<View flex:1 paddingBottom:insets.bottom+16>` with the search FlatList in the MIDDLE and the message input + Send as flex:0 children at the column end. The failing screens = KAV with a `<FlatList flex:1>` as a DIRECT child SIBLING of the pinned input bar (PostDetailScreen ~L213/L376, HubDetailScreen, messages/[id] L165/L253). Under RN 0.88 KAV's behavior="padding" self-padding resolves to ~0 in the sibling-FlatList layout inside our custom <Screen> chrome, so the bar never lifts. Crew Bank edit / other forms work because they use KeyboardFormScreen, which ALREADY measures window coords (focused input vs scroll wrapper) - confirming measurement is the right model.
FIX (one shared place, measurement, no offset guess): rewrote src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx to DROP KeyboardAvoidingView entirely. It now listens to keyboard events (iOS keyboardWillChangeFrame/WillHide, Android keyboardDidShow/DidHide), reads the keyboard top Y in window coords from the event, measures the container's bottom edge with measureInWindow (the container bottom == the input bar bottom; resting bottom recovered by adding back any lift already applied), and lifts the whole bar via an Animated `marginBottom` by exactly max(0, barBottom - kbTop), animated to the keyboard's own duration. Closed keyboard -> lift 0 -> bar sits where it did before. Deterministic and identical on every screen regardless of nesting. react-native-keyboard-controller NOT used (absent from Expo Go). useBottomBarPadding simplified to a CONSTANT resting inset (inTab?0:insets.bottom)+10 so the bar height is stable for measurement (when lifted this leaves at most a small safe-area gap above the keyboard on non-tab screens - acceptable; bar always fully visible).
DEBUG STRIP (item 3): BottomBarKeyboardView renders a green on-screen strip (testID kb-debug-strip) at the top of every bottom-bar screen showing kb height, kb top Y, window height, bar window Y + bottom, LIFT applied, bottom inset, and tab Y/N. Controlled by ONE constant `KB_DEBUG` in src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx -> set it to `false` to hide (MUST be false before shipping to users). Verified on the Expo web preview: New message compose renders the strip with no crash (values read 0 at rest because they populate on a keyboard event - which is what Jenn will capture on device).
SCREEN AUDIT (searched the whole app for TextInput near the bottom / sheets / modals):
  - PINNED bottom bar, now fixed by the shared measurement lift (all use BottomBarKeyboardView): PostDetailScreen (Hub post + Marketplace item comments), HubDetailScreen (forum thread replies), app/messages/[id] (direct + GROUP + Crew Bank + Marketplace message thread), app/messages/new (compose).
  - FORMS, untouched (already correct via KeyboardFormScreen's own window-coord measurement; Crew Bank edit = crew-listing): crew-listing, new-post, edit-post, new-event, cancel-event, register, onboarding, report, testimonial, remind-volunteers, crew-signup, hub/new, become-partner, admin-* , profile/org-listing, profile/boat-pro-listing, forgot-password, review-compose (review screen - left for REVIEWS-3).
  - INLINE/not pinned (scroll brings into view), untouched: src/components/InlineComments.tsx (used only in feed, and there it defers to PostDetail via onCompose, so no inline keyboard case ships).
  - TOP-of-screen search/filter inputs (not bottom), untouched: directory/clubs, marketplace-filters, MultiSelectPicker, compose search.
SAFETY NET: the 4 pinned screens were converted uniformly; if the measurement is ever 0 (e.g., Android adjustResize already lifted the window) the lift is simply 0 and the bar stays visible via the OS - never hidden. No screen was left in the KB-1 hidden state.
JENN MUST TEST ON DEVICE (Expo Go SDK 58, exp://marina-connect-11.expo.preview.emergentagent.com) and SEND SCREENSHOTS of the green strip with the keyboard OPEN on each: (1) a Hub post / Marketplace item -> Add a comment, (2) a forum thread reply, (3) a direct message thread AND a group thread, (4) New message compose, (5) a Crew Bank / Marketplace message thread. For each confirm the bar sits on the keyboard (incl. with the predictive/suggestions row) and the strip's LIFT is > 0 with barBtm ~= kb top; and with the keyboard closed the bar sits above the tab bar / home indicator. Android screenshot too if a tester exists. After sign-off, set KB_DEBUG=false.
FILES CHANGED: src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx (rewritten, measurement + debug strip + KB_DEBUG), src/lib/useBottomBarPadding.ts (constant resting inset). The 4 screens were already wired to these in KB-1 (no further screen edits needed). No seed data touched; temp accounts deleted; no em dashes.


## MOBILE-KB-3 (2026-10-27, MOBILE ONLY) — tighten the keyboard gap on non-tab bottom bars
DEVICE RESULT after KB-2: all bars respond to the keyboard. In-tab screens correct (tab=Y, LIFT=249, barBtm=788, kbtop=539). Non-tab screens (DM/group thread, New message; tab=N, LIFT=335, barBtm=874, kbtop=539, inset=34) left a ~44pt gap because the bar kept its resting bottom padding (inset+10) while open: the bar's bottom EDGE was flush with the keyboard but the INPUT sat inset+10 above it.
FIX (shared files only, no per-screen keyboard logic): the bar's bottom padding is now OWNED and animated by BottomBarKeyboardView and handed to each bar through a React context (BottomBarPaddingContext); useBottomBarPadding() just reads that context Animated.Value. On keyboard OPEN the component animates, IN PARALLEL with the lift and over the keyboard's own duration: padding -> 10 (every screen, same as in-tab) and marginBottom(lift) -> max(0, closedBottom - kbTop). On CLOSE both animate back (padding -> resting inset: inTab?10:insets.bottom+10; lift -> 0) so the bar sits above the home indicator again. The lift is derived from the CACHED closed-keyboard bottom edge (measureInWindow on layout while the keyboard is down, re-cached on first open and on rotation/winH change), NOT from a value that already includes the lift or the shrunken padding - so the bar's bottom edge stays flush while the padding animates (no jump/flicker). Handles the suggestions row (keyboardWillChangeFrame re-runs the lift with the new kbTop while padding stays 10) and a WillChangeFrame that is really a hide (kbHeight<=1 -> treated as close).
SCREENS CHANGED (bar View -> Animated.View reading the shared animated padding; all already wrapped in BottomBarKeyboardView): PostDetailScreen (Hub post + Marketplace comments), HubDetailScreen (forum replies), app/messages/[id] (direct + group + Crew Bank + Marketplace threads), app/messages/new (compose; its inline paddingBottom insets.bottom+16 replaced by the shared animated padding, insets import removed). Review screens untouched (REVIEWS-3).
EXPECTED NUMBERS (iPhone, keyboard OPEN): in-tab (Post/Marketplace comments, forum replies) unchanged -> tab=Y, PAD=10, LIFT~249, barBtm~788, kbtop~539. Non-tab (DM, group, New message) -> tab=N, PAD=10 (was ~44), LIFT~335, barBtm~874, kbtop~539; bar bottom edge flush at ~539, input ~10 above keyboard (same tightness as in-tab). Keyboard CLOSED non-tab -> PAD~44, LIFT=0, bar above the home indicator.
DEBUG STRIP: kept ON (KB_DEBUG in src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx) and now ALSO shows `PAD=` (the applied bottom padding) alongside kb height/top, win, barBtm, LIFT, inset, tab. Set KB_DEBUG=false to hide before shipping. Verified on web preview: New message renders the strip with PAD field, no crash; tsc adds no new errors.
JENN SHOULD SCREENSHOT (keyboard OPEN, strip visible) on each: (1) Hub post / Marketplace item comment, (2) forum thread reply, (3) direct message thread, (4) GROUP message thread, (5) New message compose, (6) Crew Bank / Marketplace message thread. Confirm PAD=10 and the input sits ~10 above the keyboard (as tight as the in-tab comment bars). Also: toggle the predictive/suggestions row (bar should stay glued as kb height changes), switch between two inputs, and rotate; and one CLOSED-keyboard shot of a non-tab thread (PAD~44, bar above the home indicator). Android shot too if a tester exists.
FILES CHANGED: src/components/BottomBarKeyboardView.tsx (context + parallel padding/lift animation + cached closed geometry + PAD in debug strip), src/lib/useBottomBarPadding.ts (reads context Animated.Value), src/screens/PostDetailScreen.tsx, src/screens/HubDetailScreen.tsx, app/messages/[id].tsx, app/messages/new.tsx (bars -> Animated.View). No seed data touched; temp accounts deleted; no em dashes.



## MOBILE-KB-4 (2026-10-27, MOBILE ONLY) — Profile-section form keyboard handling + build stamp
DEVICE RESULT after KB-3: pinned bottom bars are correct. On the Profile screen and its inline forms, tapping a field opened the keyboard but the focused field stayed HIDDEN behind it and the screen did not scroll/lift. Root cause: the Profile tab and a few form screens used a plain ScrollView (no keyboard-safe shell), unlike Crew Bank edit (crew-listing) which works via KeyboardFormScreen.
APPROACH (user-approved 1a + 2a/2b): keep the Profile forms INLINE (no layout change); reuse the DEVICE-PROVEN scroll-to-focused-field logic. Refactored src/components/KeyboardFormScreen.tsx to EXTRACT a reusable `KeyboardFormBody` (the exact measure/scroll engine: ScrollView + DeviceEventEmitter field registry + keyboard-show re-scroll + content-size retry + measureInWindow fallback + footer lift). `KeyboardFormScreen` now = Screen chrome (title/back/"Done") wrapping `KeyboardFormBody`. No new measuring code. Added props to the body: padded, contentBottom, contentContainerStyle, onKeyboardChange.
KEY DETAIL: Profile fields sit DEEP in the tree, so their onLayout y is parent-relative (not content-space). They pass ONLY onFocus; the body's measureInWindow fallback (focused input vs scroll-wrapper window coords) computes a content-space y at any depth. scrollToId now always uses the fallback for fallback-sourced ids.

## SEARCH-0 (2026-10-27) - REPORT ONLY, no code/data changes. Why can't a paid user find a free personal account in the web top-nav search, and how is search wired across web + mobile.
FINDING (root cause): the web top-nav bar (frontend/src/components/TopSearch.jsx) calls GET /search (backend routers/admin_router.py content_search). That endpoint searches ONLY posts, discussions, events, and partner/ORG accounts. It NEVER queries sailors/owners/Boat-Pro personal profiles. So Jenn's free personal account cannot appear there by name or @username - by design, people are not searched. This is NOT a free-tier exclusion; NO personal account of any tier is searchable in the top nav.
1) Top-nav /search: no auth; q>=2 chars; case-insensitive substring regex (re.escape, no accent folding). posts(title/body), discussions(title/body), events(title/description/location), orgs = users where account_type=="partner" matched on name/org_name/bio/org_categories/org_category. 5 per bucket. Placeholder literally "Search posts, discussions, events...". UI renders Community feed / Community Hub / Clubs & Orgs / Events. No people bucket at all.
2) People search DOES exist but only at GET /users/search (members-only; used by the web @mention textarea and mobile New Message). Prefix on handle (^q) and word-boundary on name ((^|\s)q), case-insensitive, @ stripped, limit<=20, min 1 char, NO email match, NO tier/account_type/banned filter. Reproduced from a paid account: found free sailor, free owner, paid sailor, and Boat Pro personal by full name, partial name, @handle and handle-without-@; email returned nothing. So every account type + free & paid ARE findable via /users/search; they are just not surfaced in any general search UI. Boat Pro BUSINESS listings are found via GET /boat-pros (directory, company_name/short_bio, market+category filters; hides lapsed subscriptions). Orgs via /search orgs bucket and /boat-pros is pros only.
3) What hides a profile: there is NO privacy / findable / searchable / visibility / private-profile toggle anywhere, and NO soft-delete user flag. Only flags are `banned` (admin) and `onboarding_complete`. /users/search and /u/{handle} do NOT filter out banned users or follow_blocks (minor gap, report only). Lapsed subscription hides only Boat Pro BUSINESS directory cards, not the person. A personal profile is reachable at GET /u/{handle} by EXACT handle regardless of tier/onboarding. Free accounts are excluded from the top-nav results only because people are never queried there (accidental-by-omission), not by a tier rule.
4) Reproduction (temp accounts, deleted; 0 remaining): /search?q= returned 0 people for "Marina Freesailor", "Marina", "@handle", "handle", "Bianca Propersonal", "Harbor", "Pablo" (only an unrelated event matched "Marina"). /users/search found all four by name/partial/@handle/handle; email query empty (no email matching).
5) MOBILE lookup surfaces (NO app-wide search confirmed): messages/new.tsx "Search people by name or @username" -> /users/search (the ONLY people search; opens a MESSAGE thread, not a profile). directory/index.tsx -> /boat-pros (Boat Pro businesses, category/market). directory/clubs.tsx "Search organizations by name" -> org list. marketplace-filters.tsx "Search gear" -> listings. MultiSelectPicker -> in-sheet option filter. FollowingListScreen "Search by name or @username" -> client-side filter of already-loaded follows. FaqScreen -> help articles. Crew Bank -> /crew-bank/standalone. There is no global search entry point in the mobile nav.
6) How to reach a person to review TODAY. Web: top-nav search CANNOT (people absent); Boat Pro directory -> business listing -> review (business identity); /u/{handle} by exact handle -> PublicProfileScreen -> Write a review (any tier, incl. free personal) but nothing leads you there except an @mention click or a known URL; @mention autocomplete in feed/comments; event attendee/crew lists; message thread; Reviews inbox requests. Mobile: Boat Pro directory -> ProDetail (business); PublicProfileScreen via mention/handle/following -> Write a review; New Message people search exists but lands in messaging, not on the profile/Review button; Crew Bank; event crew; following list. GAP: neither web nor mobile has a name/@username search that lands on a person's profile with the Review button; mobile has no people-finding path to a profile at all.
7) Privacy/abuse of making people searchable by @username: member enumeration/scraping (/users/search has NO rate limit, unlike handle-availability which is 30/60s/IP), unwanted contact/harassment, and reviews are a strong motive to find strangers. Existing controls: follow_blocks (block), members-only gate on /users/search (no anon), NO per-user findable opt-out, NO rate limit on search.
8) RECOMMENDATIONS (S/M/L): (a) S - point the web top-nav search at /users/search and add a "People" results bucket; add a mobile global people-search entry (reuse /users/search) that opens the PROFILE (not a message). (b) S - add a rate limit to /users/search. (c) M - a "Review someone" flow that starts from an existing relationship (message thread / shared event attendee / crew list) so you review people you actually interacted with, reducing stranger-search. (d) M - opt-in `findable` setting (default on) + exclude banned/blocked from search. (e) L - full people directory with filters. RECOMMENDED: (a)+(b) now (directly fixes Jenn's report and gives mobile a path), then (c) as the primary review-discovery flow, with (d) before any broad rollout. Reasoning: the capability already exists (/users/search); the real fix is wiring it to the profile and adding guardrails, not building new search infrastructure.


## REVIEWS-4 (2026-10-28, BACKEND + WEB) - review disputes, 30-day self-removal, private attachments, admin queue. No publish. Campaigns OFF.
APPROVED COPY: project doc 92 sections B & C pasted verbatim by Jenn into ONE module backend/review_copy.py (constants + REASON_LABELS + form notices + full SECTION_B/SECTION_C arrays), served to the web via GET /review-disputes/meta. Section B "Who can review" uses Jenn's corrected last sentence (Boat Pro personal-only, no shared category, once per pair). Mobile (REVIEWS-4m) and Help Center (REVIEWS-5) will reuse this module.
CONSTANTS: SELF_REMOVAL_DAYS=30, MAX_IMAGE_MB=10, MAX_PDF_MB=25, ATTACHMENT_RETENTION_DAYS=365 (placeholder, counsel), MAX_ATTACHMENTS=5, EXPLANATION_MAX=1000, MAX_DISPUTES_PER_DAY=5 (per filer), MAX_REQUESTS_PER_REVIEW=2 (original + 1 contest).
BACKEND new routers/review_disputes_router.py (registered in server.py), collection review_disputes {id, review_kind two_way|one_way, session_id, review_author_id, review_id, target_user_id, filed_by, filer_role reviewee|org_admin|reviewer_self_removal, reason(9 enum), explanation<=1000, attachments[], status open|decided, decision stays|removed|kept_with_details_removed, decision_reason(required internal), decided_by, contest_count, is_contest, flagged_filer, history[], timestamps}. Endpoints: GET /review-disputes/meta; POST /review-disputes (file; resolves filer role; self-removal requires reviewer within 30d of revealed_at/created_at and a live review; 5/day per filer; 1 open per review per filer; <=2 requests per review); POST /review-disputes/{id}/attachments (multipart, content-sniffed magic bytes jpg/png/webp/pdf, size caps, PRIVATE path sailmorenow/private/disputes/...); GET /review-disputes/{id}/attachments/{i} (AUTH-GATED: filer or admin only, streams bytes, no public URL, Cache-Control private no-store); GET /review-disputes/mine (+can_contest); POST /review-disputes/{id}/contest (reviewer once, new info, blocked at 2 requests); admin GET /admin/review-disputes?status=&reason= (review snapshot + attachments meta + filer/target names + mutual_pair + held + session_status + history); POST /admin/review-disputes/{id}/decide (stays/removed/kept_with_details_removed + required reason; removed deletes the disputed side and sets session removed if none remain, OTHER side stays live; kept redacts the comment to REDACTED_COMMENT; both-party neutral in-app notify + email; audit_logged in admin_actions); POST /admin/review-disputes/{id}/flag-filer. A disputed review STAYS live and counted until decided (compute_summary keys off session status, unchanged by a dispute). Pre-reveal hold/release/remove reuse the existing reviews_router admin endpoints. Email: new mailer.dispute_decision_email_html (neutral, no review text). notify kinds reused (review_completed/comment). Rate limits via deps.rate_limit (IP) + per-user DB counts.
WEB: new components/ReviewDisputeForm.jsx (modal: reason select, explanation+1000 counter, up to 5 attachments w/ the exact "Please do not upload..." notice, collapsible section-C policy from meta, secondary disputes@ note; also exports MyDisputesPanel with one-time Contest). ReviewReply.jsx ReportReviewLink now opens the in-app form via onReport (mailto only as fallback). pages/Reviews.jsx: Live tab "their review of you" -> Report or dispute; "your review" -> Remove my review (self_removal); Review requests -> Report or dispute (reviewee can dispute a PENDING review, which queues without hiding/delaying); new "Disputes" tab = MyDisputesPanel. pages/AdminModeration.jsx: new "Disputes" tab (DisputesTab + DisputeCard + DisputeAttachment) with status/reason filters, filer/target, review snapshot, mutual_pair + "filed while pending" + held + flagged badges, authenticated blob attachment viewing (api responseType blob -> objectURL), decide (3 outcomes + required reason), hold/release/remove-this-side, flag filer, and a history timeline. Icons swapped to existing names (add/delete/upload).
COPY GUARD: scripts/check_review_copy.py (passes) - fails if 48h/reject/not-accepted/delete-review/accept-or-reject wording leaks into the review+dispute copy.
TESTED (backend E2E with temp accounts, deleted; 0 residue): 21/22 PASS - file on pending (no hide/delay), duplicate-open blocked 409, attachment valid-pdf ok + bad-type 400, third-user attachment 403 + filer 200 + admin 200, review-back reveal, averages unchanged during dispute then B's average drops after removal while the other side stays live, self-remove inside 30d ok + outside 30d 409, admin decide removed, my-disputes decided, admin hold+release, admin queue content. The 1 non-pass was a FLAWED TEST SCENARIO not a bug (the test consumed both request slots before contesting, so contest was correctly the 3rd request -> blocked; the "blocked after 2" assertion passed for the same reason; counting logic is correct). Contest-in-isolation is code-verified. WEB verified by screenshot: Live tab Report/Remove links + Disputes tab render; dispute modal renders with reason select, 0/1000 counter, exact attachment notice, Add a file, policy link, Submit.
NOT FULLY VERIFIED: the web Admin "Disputes" TAB render was not screenshotted - a script-promoted temp admin's platform_role was not surfaced in /auth/me for that browser session, so the admin guard redirected. The tab is wired into the same TABS/switch as the 4 working admin tabs and ALL its endpoints passed the backend E2E (decide/hold/release/flag 200, queue returns items). Needs one check with a real admin account. Email delivery not exercised (temp @example.com; send_email构造 is wired and called in the decided path).
ORPHANED BLOBS: removed disputes leave their private attachment objects in object storage (no delete API); covered by the ATTACHMENT_RETENTION_DAYS cleanup policy.
OUT OF SCOPE (unchanged): REVIEWS-4m mobile dispute UI, REVIEWS-5 Help Center.
