## MSG-FIX-1 - 2026-10-29
- Fixed business/personal DM thread splitting (MSG-0 root cause). A conversation's channel (personal vs the Boat Pro's business "~b" thread) is now decided ONCE when the thread starts and stored in `identities`; replies target their thread via a new `MessageSend.thread_id` and identity is read from the thread, never re-derived from the recipient. New conversations go business if the recipient is addressed as business OR the sender acts as business.
- Backend: `schemas.py` (+thread_id), `routers/messaging_router.py` (send_message channel logic; 1:1 DM notification now carries thread_id and links to `/messages?thread=`).
- Web: `Messages.jsx` send includes `active.thread_id`.
- Mobile: list passes real `thread`+`label`+`peer` and shows an identity-label pill; `[id].tsx` uses the passed thread id to load+send (keeps "~b"), shows the header label; Boat Pro profile "Message" opens the business thread (`as:"business"`); dm notifications open the exact thread. BUILD_ID rv3a-2 -> rv3a-3.
- Verified in a real browser (temp accounts, deleted): personal reply stays personal; business reply sent via the UI stays in the business thread; both threads show side by side with correct labels (MSGUI RIGGING CO vs PERSONAL) and do not merge; deep-link `?thread=...~b` opens the correct thread. Mobile changes are code-verified only (Expo not runnable in the scripted browser).


## MSG-FIX-2 - 2026-10-29
- Root cause of web label mismatch: list_threads built identity_label/peer_identity from the OTHER participant's identity (_thread_peer_label). From a Boat Pro's own inbox the peer in both threads is the same customer (personal), so web showed "PERSONAL" on both. Fixed by a viewer+channel label model.
- Backend: list_threads now returns per 1:1 thread `channel` (personal|business), `business_name` (pro company on business threads), and `viewer_is_boat_pro`. New `GET /messages/thread-meta/{thread_id}` gives a single thread its label/header (used by mobile when opened from a notification). send_message rejects an identity that conflicts with an existing thread (identity locked to thread; resolve_author_identity moved into the new-conversation branch). DM notification titles: business->pro "{Name} messaged {Business}"; business->member "{Business} messaged you"; personal w/ pro "{Name} messaged you"; sailor-to-sailor unchanged.
- Web (Messages.jsx): pill shows business_name ONLY when viewer_is_boat_pro && channel==business (list) or Personal/business_name in header for pro viewers; removed in-thread "Posting as" switch (kept only in New message, gated to boat_pro); replies send only thread_id (no identity).
- Mobile: list pill same rule; [id].tsx fetches thread-meta for its header (no param reliance); new.tsx business option gated to boat_pro. BUILD_ID rv3a-3 -> rv3a-4.
- Browser-verified (temp accounts, deleted): Boat Pro inbox shows names only with HARBOR RIGGING pill on the business thread only and PERSONAL header on the personal thread; no in-thread switch; reply stays in thread; New message offers the choice; sailor sees 0 pills (row name conveys business). Owner/org = same non-pro path (code-verified). Mobile changes code-verified only.

## MSG-FIX-3 + ADMIN-EVENTS-1 (2026-06-24) — BUILD_ID rv3a-4 -> rv3a-5
Testing agent NOT used (forbidden). Manual: curl/python + real-browser screenshots with temp data, cleaned up. Campaigns OFF. No publish.

### PART 1 — MSG-FIX-3 (mobile fix; web defensive)
- Root cause: mobile `app/messages/new.tsx` single-recipient send ignored the server's returned thread_id and navigated to a client-recomputed personal id (no "~b"), so a Boat Pro business new message opened the PERSONAL thread.
- Fix (mobile): capture the send response and pass `thread: res.thread_id` to `/messages/[id]` (the thread view already prefers the explicit `thread` param and drives its header pill from `GET /messages/thread-meta/{id}`). Individual flow unchanged. Other thread-open entry points (Boat Pro profile, Crew Bank, directory, event, public profile) open the thread view BEFORE sending and route via `as`/`thread`, then reply with `thread_id` - already correct, no change.
- Fix (web, defensive): `Messages.jsx` send() now syncs `active.thread_id` to `data.thread_id` from the server response for 1:1 so follow-ups never target a recomputed id.
- Verified: mobile = code-verified only (bundles clean); backend returns authoritative thread_id with "~b"; web change compiles.

### PART 2 — ADMIN-EVENTS-1 (web + backend)
- (a) Edit: admin event edit now exposes category, title, date/time, location, description, photo on BOTH surfaces - calendar/detail (`AdminControls.jsx`, new select/datetime/flyer field types) and portal (`AdminEvents.jsx` EventEditModal + inline category). Uses existing `/admin/events/{id}` validation. Photo edits event `image_url` via existing FlyerUploader.
- (b) Delete + tombstone: deleting an aggregated event (`admin_content_router.admin_delete_content`) writes a tombstone to `event_tombstones` keyed by the stable id `agg_{source_key}_{external_id}`. `aggregator.run_aggregator` skips any tombstoned id, so deleted feed events NEVER re-import (proven by stubbed-source importer test: run1 import -> delete -> tombstone -> run3 not re-imported, all PASS). Portal gained a Delete button + confirm modal; event detail already had delete via AdminControls.
- Dedup: aggregator dedupes by stable id `agg_{source_key}_{external_id}` (external_id = hash of source+uid/title+start). Admin edits are protected per-field: `/admin/events/{id}` records changed keys into `admin_locked_fields`; the importer drops locked fields from its `$set` so an edited aggregated event is NOT overwritten on the next run (untouched fields still refresh; category stays protected by category_manual). Proven: locked title kept + unlocked description refreshed on re-import.
- (c) Admin nav: removed the hover dropdown. New `/admin` landing page (`AdminHome.jsx`) with a button grid to every portal (Moderation & disputes, Reports, Events, Event sources, Analytics, Contact, Sponsors, Partners, Referrals, Zoho, Demo). Top nav = single "Admin" link; mobile drawer = single "Admin" entry. Admin-only guard on page + links.
- Browser-verified (temp admin jenn@, temp aggregated event + temp non-admin, all deleted): /admin grid; portal edit modal (category+date+photo); calendar-detail edit modal (category+date+photo); delete confirm + event gone; non-admin sees no Admin link and "Admin access required" at /admin. Phone/tablet width = code-verified (reuses existing `2xl:hidden` responsive drawer; screenshot tool forces 1920px).

### Note
- Object Storage (`storage.py`) still lacks a delete fn - blocker remains for ACCOUNT-DELETE-1 STAGE B (photo purge).

(1) Duplicate contact email fixed: `/contact` and `/support/contact` now send ONE branded navy email to CONTACT_INBOX_EMAIL (sender + subject + full message + Reply-To sender + Open-in-admin deep link); the separate admin_notify alert for contact was removed (admin@ is an alias of customersupport@). (2) Missing dispute email fixed: admin alert now fires on EVERY dispute filing path with distinct wording + deep link - regular filing, reviewer self-removal (root cause: self-removal skipped `_notify_filed`), and contest (previously had no alert). (3) 4 welcome + 2 password-reset emails + the plain contact/support email converted to the shared navy header with right-aligned logo (SMN_HEADER_DONE marker prevents double logo). (4) Dark mode hardened in `_harden_dark_mode`: color-scheme/supported-color-schemes meta (light dark), dark-mode <style>, explicit bgcolor on navy cells, brighter orange #FF9E2C + navy #10233A button text. (5) Send failures now logged as `EMAIL SEND FAILED` + recorded to `email_failures` Mongo collection. (6) Missed-alert audit: added admin alert to `POST /api/markets/request` (previously silent); reports/content-hidden alert fine, messages not reportable, no claim/verification feature exists. (7) Real test emails sent to jenn@sailmorenow.com ONLY (4, all ok). Sender report: all mail via Emergent managed Resend proxy, From name "Sail More Now", From address = proxy default hey@emergentmails.com (EMAIL_FROM_ADDRESS unset) - provider unchanged per instruction. No em-dashes. Testing agent NOT used (forbidden); verified via browser screenshots + offline capture + real sends.


## EMAIL-SWITCH-1 (2026-06, BACKEND) -- build stamp EF3-2026.06 -> ES1-2026.06
Full detail in /app/memory/EMAIL-SWITCH-1_REPORT.md. Switched the email transport in `mailer.send_email` to Jenn's OWN Resend account (secret `RESEND_API_KEY`, added via secure card). PRIMARY path: `POST https://api.resend.com/emails`, From `"Sail More Now" <notifications@notifications.sailmorenow.com>` (env `RESEND_FROM_EMAIL`/`EMAIL_FROM_NAME`), Reply-To customersupport@ (sender's address for contact forms), List-Unsubscribe headers preserved. All 21 email types flow through this one function so all now send via Resend. Retry/backoff on 429 + 5xx (up to 4 attempts, honours retry-after, sleep capped 10s); non-retryable 4xx recorded as EMAIL SEND FAILED. FALLBACK: missing/invalid key or exhausted retries logs `EMAIL FALLBACK` (recorded to `email_failures` with kind) and re-sends via the Emergent managed proxy so mail is never dropped (verified live for invalid-key and missing-key). Templates/layout/logo/footer/burst-guard UNCHANGED. Free plan: 3000/mo, 100/day; if daily cap hit, short retries then proxy fallback (digests not dropped). SPF/DKIM/DMARC: could not read via API (restricted send-only key -> /domains 401); expected to align since From domain = verified notifications.sailmorenow.com; Jenn to confirm via Gmail "Show original" or Resend dashboard. Real tests to jenn@sailmorenow.com ONLY (4 via Resend + raw + 2 fallback), all ok. Testing agent NOT used (forbidden).

## ADMIN-MAIL-1 (2026-06, WEB ADMIN + BACKEND) -- build stamp ES1-2026.06 -> AM1-2026.06
Full detail in /app/memory/ADMIN-MAIL-1_REPORT.md. A) New admin page /admin/email "Email delivery" + Admin-home tile: lists email_failures (time, subject-as-type, recipient, reason, route = Failed/Sent-via-backup) with plain-language hints, filter All/Failed/Fallback + date range, Mark resolved/Reopen + Clear resolved, NO message bodies shown/stored, 90-day TTL auto-delete; unresolved count badge on the Admin nav button (desktop+mobile), polled 60s from GET /api/admin/email-failures/summary. New endpoints: GET/POST /admin/email-failures(+/summary,/{id}/resolve,/clear-resolved), POST /admin/contact/{id}/status, POST /admin/contact/bulk, DELETE /admin/contact/{id}; all admin-only via Depends(require_admin). C) contact_messages stores user_id ONLY for in-app support submissions (source profile_support); public /contact has no user_id (only email) - relevant for ACCOUNT-DELETE Stage B, changed nothing now. Verified in browser at laptop/tablet/phone + 17/17 API checks (incl. anon blocked, redaction, audit log). Temp data + scripts cleaned; no seed data touched. Testing agent NOT used (forbidden).
## BILLING-1 (2026-10-30, BACKEND + WEB + MOBILE) -- build stamp AM1-2026.06 -> BILL1-2026.10
Full detail in /app/memory/BILLING-1_REPORT.md. Replaced the legacy one-time stacking/queued-plan model with Stripe auto-renewing subscriptions; webhooks (customer.subscription.*, checkout.session.completed, invoice.paid, invoice.payment_failed) are the single source of truth for tier + expiry via billing.apply_subscription_entitlement (the ONLY place a Stripe tier is granted). Entitled = active/trialing/past_due; canceled/unpaid/incomplete_expired resets to free. Endpoints: /payments/checkout (new sub, 30-day trial via trial_period_days when eligible, one trial per account), /payments/subscription/upgrade (immediate, proration_behavior=always_invoice, Boat Pro, re-anchors term), /payments/subscription/change (scheduled term/downgrade at period end via Subscription Schedule, proration_behavior=none, no charge now, undoable), /cancel + /resume (cancel_at_period_end toggle), /undo-change, /portal, status poll, admin trial toggle, webhook (idempotent via stripe_events). Failed payment -> past_due keeps access during dunning + "payment didn't go through" email w/ hosted_invoice_url. Trial reminder: scheduler _trial_ending_job cron 09:30 PT, 2-4 day window, idempotent via trial_reminder_sent, cannot send twice. THIS SESSION: mobile my-plan.tsx rewritten read-only (removed all legacy stacking/queue/upgrade UI so stale data cannot crash; status line mirrors web; "Manage on the website" note; no purchase/cancel/portal buttons) - CODE-VERIFIED ONLY (tsc clean except pre-existing /contact typed-route warnings; Metro bundles; headless gated to login). KNOWN GAP (pre go-live): /subscription/upgrade + /change are NOT wired to any UI - both web Pricing.jsx and mobile pricing.tsx route all purchases through /checkout, so an existing subscriber switching plans would get a new Checkout Session, not a modify/proration. Legacy will_queue/queue_message remnants still in mobile pricing.tsx. Still TEST mode; live-mode + Stripe test-clock scenarios (trial->charge, renewal, dunning, cancel-at-period-end, scheduled change) PENDING on Jenn claiming the sandbox. See report section 7 for full go-live checklist. testing_agent NOT used (forbidden); no seed/temp data touched; no em-dashes; Zoho Campaigns OFF.

## BILLING-2 (2026-10-30, BACKEND + WEB + MOBILE) -- build stamp BILL1-2026.10 -> BILL2-2026.10
Full detail in /app/memory/BILLING-2_REPORT.md. WIRED PLAN SWITCHING for existing subscribers: backend `/payments/checkout` now hard-guards with HTTP 409 when a live subscription exists (active/trialing/past_due) so a second Stripe sub can never be created from the API; new `/payments/subscription/preview` returns the amount charged today (upgrade, create_preview always_invoice) or the effective date (downgrade, no charge); `/subscription/upgrade` (higher tier -> immediate proration) and `/subscription/change` (lower tier or same-tier term change -> scheduled at period end via Subscription Schedule) now validate audience + require a live sub + reject same-package; `/payments/subscription` also returns package_id. Web Pricing.jsx fetches the subscription, shows "Change my plan" (vs "Current plan - manage") for subscribers, and doSwitch() runs preview -> confirm dialog (amount today for upgrade / effective date + "No charge today" for downgrade) -> upgrade|change -> /my-plan; new Checkout only for users with no live sub. Mobile pricing.tsx is now READ-ONLY: removed Stripe checkout button, WebBrowser+polling, payment/success nav, will_queue/queue_message, and the "Plans stack" note; shows plan info + "See full plan details" link + "subscribe on the website" note; "Continue with free" kept. (Deeper legacy queued_plans reads remain in deps.py/scheduler.py/directory_router.py, harmless, flagged for later.) TEST CLOCKS (sandbox now claimed): S1 trial->first charge $6.99 PASS; S2 3-day reminder once-only PASS; S3 renewal $6.99 PASS; S4 upgrade+term change preview==charge $266.47 PASS; S5 downgrade flips at period end, no charge now, $12.99 at flip PASS; S6 cancel->free PASS; S7 resume PASS; S8 failed payment kept past_due access then canceled->free at ~16d PASS. DUNNING (Part 4): no API; Dashboard-only (Billing > Revenue recovery > Retries) - enable Smart Retries ~2wk + "Cancel the subscription" on exhaustion, per-mode (repeat for live) - exact clicks in report. SECURITY (Part 5, report only): only 1 admin (jenn@sailmorenow.com, platform_role=admin) and it HAS password login (password_hash) and NO google_id -> password-based, not Google-only; to go Google-only: link Google first, then unset password_hash on admins + harden auth (via integration_expert) + keep break-glass. Real-password files: /app/memory/test_credentials.md (plaintext admin pw), migration_backups/*.json (bcrypt hashes); test_iter*.py hold throwaway test-account pws. Verified: web "Change my plan" render [BROWSER], mobile read-only [BROWSER], switch endpoints [STRIPE test clocks], confirm dialog [CODE]. All temp Stripe customers/clocks + temp DB users deleted; sandbox + DB clean. testing_agent NOT used; no em-dashes; no real emails delivered; Zoho Campaigns OFF.

## BILLING-3a (2026-10-30, BACKEND + WEB) -- build stamp BILL2-2026.10 -> BILL3A-2026.10
Full detail in /app/memory/BILLING-3a_REPORT.md. INFRA FIX: identity_router.py was clobbering global stripe.api_key with the placeholder STRIPE_API_KEY (broke all authed Stripe endpoint calls) - now uses STRIPE_SECRET_KEY; pinned stripe.api_version=2024-06-20 app-wide (account default 2026 "clover" changed coupon/promo/discount param shapes). (A) SIGNUP CODE: native Stripe promotion code (100% off, repeating 3mo), Jenn-managed in dashboard, no code DB. /payments/promo/validate (monthly-only, not if trial_used/had_paid_plan, free tier, active Stripe code; rate-limited 8/user+IP/10min) returns valid+first_charge_date+price or plain reason. Checkout accepts promo_code -> REPLACES trial, payment_method_collection=always, discounts=[{promotion_code}]. apply_subscription_entitlement records is_promo + subscription_free_until (coupon discount.end). Web promo box on Pricing (approved wording). Plan status "Free until {date}, first payment ${amt} on {date}". New _promo_ending_job (09:40 PT) 3-day reminder, idempotent via promo_reminder_sent; trial reminder unchanged; both emails now include plan lengths + Your plan link. (B) COMP GRANTS (net-new, none existed): comp_grants collection + provider="comp"; admin /admin/comps grant(reason req, optional end_at, granted_by)/list/revoke, require_admin + audit; guard blocks grant on live Stripe sub (409); apply_subscription_entitlement SKIPS comp accounts (never billed/overwritten); _comp_expiry_job (02:05 PT) drops expired to free; revoke->free; web /admin/comps page (grant form + list + revoke confirm), member plan says "Complimentary plan" (never "lifetime"). Found only org-partner status as a prior tier-grant path (4 orgs, kept separate); MIGRATED 25 legacy non-Stripe member paid accounts into comp (tier+expiry preserved, reversible) so one mechanism. (C) CHOOSE TERM before first charge: /payments/subscription/choose-term + undo-term; members 1/3/6/12, pro 1/6/12; default monthly. TRIAL = modify price in place (keeps trial, term charged ONCE at trial end, no extra monthly) - existing /change did NOT fit. PROMO = Subscription Schedule phase1 monthly+coupon hard-bounded to discount.end, phase2 term no-discount at coupon end. Web free-period chooser on Your plan w/ confirm+undo. (D) TEST CLOCKS all PASS w/ real amounts: promo 3-free->$6.99; pro featured->$19.99; reject 6mo/unknown/used-up/prior-trial; cancel-during-free->free; trial->monthly $6.99; CRITICAL promo+6mo-> $0,$0,$0 then ONE $35.65 (coupon never touches prepaid term); trial+12mo->$67.10 once no double-charge; undo->monthly; comp grant/expiry/revoke/blocked-on-active-sub. (E) REVIEW PASS: no double-charge, no missed charge, no comp billing, no access left on after cancel/non-payment (reasoning in report). (F) go-live: recreate coupon+promo code in LIVE (per-mode), keep STRIPE_API_VERSION pin. [BROWSER] verified: promo box, free-period chooser (5 term buttons), admin comps page w/ 25 migrated. All temp accounts/sessions/Stripe test objects deleted; no real emails sent; Zoho Campaigns OFF; did not touch email setup/scrapers/account-deletion; BILLING-3b (reward codes, "Give 1 free month") NOT built.
